379: 
380: @dataclass(frozen=True)
381: class RuntimeCapture:
382:     """Closed-handle evidence and construction recipe for exact-H planning.
383: 
384:     Mutable runtime objects stay private and are copied before every planning
385:     operation. Public declaration, document, fact, tick, and source accessors
386:     likewise return copies, so caller mutation cannot alter a later plan.
387:     """
388: 
389:     basis: ReadBasis
390:     custodian: str
391:     max_atomic_records: int | None
392:     evaluated_at: float
393:     _effective_declaration: VertexFile = field(repr=False, compare=False)
394:     _locator: VertexFile = field(repr=False, compare=False)
395:     _declaration_anchor: Any = field(repr=False, compare=False)
396:     _declaration_documents: tuple[dict[str, Any], ...] = field(repr=False, compare=False)
397:     _facts: tuple[SnapshotFact, ...] = field(repr=False, compare=False)
398:     _ticks: tuple[SnapshotTick, ...] = field(repr=False, compare=False)
399:     _candidate_template: Vertex = field(repr=False, compare=False)
400:     _post_boundary_candidate_template: Vertex | None = field(
401:         default=None, repr=False, compare=False
402:     )
403:     _sources: tuple[Any, ...] = field(default=(), repr=False, compare=False)
404:     _pending_boundaries: tuple[Any, ...] = field(default=(), repr=False, compare=False)
405:     _signing_context: CapturedSigningContext | None = field(
406:         default=None, repr=False, compare=False
407:     )
408: 
409:     @property
410:     def effective_declaration(self) -> VertexFile:
411:         from .declaration import effective_declaration_from_documents
412: 
413:         return effective_declaration_from_documents(
414:             self._declaration_documents,
415:             self._locator,
416:             verify_pins=False,
417:         )
418: 
419:     @property
420:     def declaration_documents(self) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], ...]:
421:         return copy.deepcopy(self._declaration_documents)
422: 
423:     @property
424:     def facts(self) -> tuple[SnapshotFact, ...]:
425:         return copy.deepcopy(self._facts)
426: 
427:     @property
428:     def ticks(self) -> tuple[SnapshotTick, ...]:
429:         return copy.deepcopy(self._ticks)
430: 
431:     @property
432:     def sources(self) -> tuple[Any, ...]:
433:         return copy.deepcopy(self._sources)
434: 
435:     @property
436:     def pending_boundaries(self) -> tuple[Any, ...]:
437:         return copy.deepcopy(self._pending_boundaries)
438: 
439:     def _locator_copy(self) -> VertexFile:
440:         return _capture_locator_ingress(self._locator)
441: 
442:     def _candidate_copy(self, *, after_pending_boundaries: bool = False) -> Vertex:
443:         template = (
444:             self._post_boundary_candidate_template
445:             if after_pending_boundaries and self._post_boundary_candidate_template is not None
446:             else self._candidate_template
447:         )
448:         return template.detached_copy()
449: 
450: 
451: def _capture_locator_ingress(locator: VertexFile) -> VertexFile:
452:     """Detach the residence and ingress values capture may use after return."""
453:     from lang.ast import InlineSource, SourcesBlock, VertexFile
454: 
455:     def frozen_ast(cls: type, **fields: object) -> Any:
456:         # lang.ast deliberately implements its own frozen slots rather than
457:         # dataclasses. Constructing through object.__new__ is the narrow copy
458:         # primitive that neither invokes its custom setter nor shares a caller
459:         # object; all supplied values below are immutable scalars/tuples.
460:         instance = object.__new__(cls)
461:         for name, value in fields.items():
462:             object.__setattr__(instance, name, value)
463:         return instance
464: 
465:     blocks = tuple(
466:         frozen_ast(
467:             SourcesBlock,
468:             mode=block.mode,
469:             sources=tuple(
470:                 frozen_ast(
471:                     InlineSource,
472:                     command=source.command,
473:                     kind=source.kind,
474:                     observer=source.observer,
475:                     every=source.every,
476:                     on=source.on,
477:                     format=source.format,
478:                     timeout=source.timeout,
479:                     origin=source.origin,
480:                     env=tuple(source.env),
481:                     parse=tuple(source.parse),
482:                     path=source.path,
483:                 )
484:                 for source in block.sources
485:             ),
486:         )
487:         for block in (locator.sources_blocks or ())
488:     )
489:     return frozen_ast(
490:         VertexFile,
491:         name=locator.name,
492:         loops={},
493:         store=locator.store,
494:         store_backend=locator.store_backend,
495:         store_location=locator.store_location,
496:         sources_blocks=blocks or None,
497:         path=locator.path,
498:     )
499: 
500: 
501: def _json_default(value: object) -> object:
502:     from types import MappingProxyType
503: 
504:     if isinstance(value, MappingProxyType):
505:         return dict(value)
506:     raise TypeError(f"{type(value).__name__} is not JSON serializable")
507: 
508: 
509: def _payload_text(payload: object) -> str:
510:     """Match the live SQLite writer's fresh-row payload encoding."""
511:     return json.dumps(payload, default=_json_default)
512: 
513: 
514: class _BatchSnapshot:
515:     """One in-memory extension of a captured snapshot during planning only."""
516: 
517:     def __init__(
518:         self,
519:         base: QuerySnapshot | RuntimeCapture,
520:         facts: tuple[SnapshotFact, ...],
521:         ticks: tuple[SnapshotTick, ...],
522:     ) -> None:
523:         if isinstance(base, RuntimeCapture):
524:             projected = base.basis.projected_through
525:             self.represented = (
526:                 None
527:                 if projected is None
528:                 else Watermark(lineage=projected.lineage, ordinal=projected.ordinal)
529:             )
530:             self.view_generation = base.basis.view_generation
531:             self.declaration_anchor = base._declaration_anchor
532:         else:
533:             self.represented = base.represented
534:             self.view_generation = base.view_generation
535:             self.declaration_anchor = base.declaration_anchor
536:         self._facts = facts
537:         self._ticks = ticks
538: 
539:     def facts(self, request: FactRequest):
540:         from .arrival_contract import FactPage
541: 
542:         rows = self._facts
543:         if request.fact_id is not None:
544:             rows = tuple(row for row in rows if row.id == request.fact_id)
545:         return FactPage(rows, None, False, request.order)
546: 
547:     def ticks(self, request: TickRequest) -> tuple[SnapshotTick, ...]:
548:         return self._ticks
549: 
550:     def summary(self, request: SummaryRequest) -> Any:
551:         raise RuntimeWriteRefused(
552:             "summary reads are unavailable on a pending batch planning snapshot"
553:         )
554: 
555:     def search(self, request: SearchRequest) -> SearchPage:
556:         raise RuntimeWriteRefused("search is unavailable on a pending batch planning snapshot")
557: 
558:     def close(self) -> None:
559:         # The outer preparation scope owns the real snapshot. A synthetic
560:         # extension is an immutable view over tuples and holds no resource.
561:         return None
562: 
563: 
564: def _fact_from_draft(draft: RecordDraft, ordinal: int, seq: int) -> SnapshotFact:
565:     body = draft.body
566:     return SnapshotFact(
567:         id=body["id"],
568:         kind=body["kind"],
569:         ts=body["ts"],
570:         observer=body["observer"],
571:         origin=body["origin"],
572:         payload=json.loads(body["payload"]),
573:         arrival_ordinal=ordinal,
574:         arrival_seq=seq,
575:         payload_text=body["payload"],
576:         signature=body.get("signature"),
577:     )
578: 
579: 
580: def _tick_from_draft(draft: RecordDraft, ordinal: int, seq: int) -> SnapshotTick:
581:     body = draft.body
582:     return SnapshotTick(
583:         id=body["id"],
584:         name=body["name"],
585:         ts=body["ts"],
586:         since=body["since"],
587:         origin=body["origin"],
588:         payload=json.loads(body["payload"]),
589:         arrival_ordinal=ordinal,
590:         arrival_seq=seq,
591:         payload_text=body["payload"],
592:         prev_hash=body["prev_hash"],
593:         window_start=body["window_start"],
594:         fact_cursor=body["fact_cursor"],
595:         window_hash=body["window_hash"],
596:         signature=body.get("signature"),
597:     )
598: 
599: 
600: def _window_hash(
601:     facts: tuple[SnapshotFact, ...],
602:     *,
603:     start: str,
604:     appended: tuple[object, ...] | None = None,
605: ) -> str:
606:     """The live tick window hash over exact snapshot rows plus this fact."""
607:     import hashlib
608: 
609:     digest = hashlib.sha256()
610:     if appended is not None and start == appended[0]:
611:         return digest.hexdigest()
612:     start_at = -1
613:     if start:
614:         for index, fact in enumerate(facts):
615:             if fact.id == start:
616:                 start_at = index
617:                 break
618:         else:
619:             raise RuntimeWriteRefused(f"tick window start {start!r} is absent from the snapshot")
620:     for fact in facts[start_at + 1 :]:
621:         if fact.payload_text is None:
622:             raise RuntimeWriteRefused("snapshot omitted exact fact payload text")
623:         digest.update(
624:             fact_row_hash(
625:                 (
626:                     fact.id,
627:                     fact.kind,
628:                     fact.ts,
629:                     fact.observer,
630:                     fact.origin,
631:                     fact.payload_text,
632:                     fact.signature,
633:                 )
634:             ).encode()
635:         )
636:     if appended is not None:
637:         digest.update(fact_row_hash(appended).encode())
638:     return digest.hexdigest()
639: 
640: 
641: def _current_tick_context(
642:     ticks: tuple[SnapshotTick, ...], *, current_fact_id: str
643: ) -> tuple[str | None, str]:
644:     """Return the predecessor identity and next tick's window start."""
645:     if not ticks:
646:         return None, ""
647:     previous = max(ticks, key=lambda tick: (tick.arrival_ordinal, tick.arrival_seq))
648:     if previous.payload_text is None:
649:         raise RuntimeWriteRefused("snapshot omitted exact tick payload text")
650:     previous_hash = tick_row_hash(
651:         (
652:             previous.id,
653:             previous.name,
654:             previous.ts,
655:             previous.since,
656:             previous.origin,
657:             previous.payload_text,
658:             previous.prev_hash,
659:             previous.window_start,
660:             previous.fact_cursor,
661:             previous.window_hash,
662:             previous.signature,
663:         )
664:     )
665:     if previous.window_hash is None:
666:         # The live store starts a new chain epoch at its current fact edge,
667:         # which is this just-planned fact; its first window is empty.
668:         return previous_hash, current_fact_id
669:     if previous.fact_cursor is None:
670:         raise RuntimeWriteRefused("chained tick has no fact cursor")
671:     return previous_hash, previous.fact_cursor
672: 
673: 
674: def _existing_fact_plan(
675:     snapshot: QuerySnapshot,
676:     basis: ReadBasis,
677:     fact: Fact,
678:     credentials: WriteCredentials,
679:     fact_id: str | None,
680:     *,
681:     signing_context: CapturedSigningContext | None = None,
682:     credential_session: CredentialResolutionSession | None = None,
683: ) -> OrdinaryWritePlan | None:
684:     """Recognize an exact retry before any new-fact admission decision."""
685:     if fact_id is None:
686:         return None
687:     chosen_id = fact_id
688:     existing = next(
689:         (
690:             row
691:             for row in snapshot.facts(
692:                 FactRequest(limit=None, include_internal=True, order="oldest")
693:             ).items
694:             if row.id == chosen_id
695:         ),
696:         None,
697:     )
698:     if existing is None:
699:         return None
700:     payload_text = _payload_text(fact.payload)
701:     session = credential_session or CredentialResolutionSession()
702:     inner_signature = _author_signature(
703:         credentials,
704:         session,
705:         signing_context,
706:         captured_head=basis.captured_head,
707:         observer=fact.observer,
708:         domain=SigningDomain.FACT,
709:         purpose=CredentialPurpose.AUTHORSHIP,
710:         digest=fact_commitment_hash(
711:             fact.kind, fact.ts, fact.observer, fact.origin, payload_text
712:         ),
713:         legacy_signer=credentials.fact_signer,
714:     )
715:     if existing.payload_text is None:
716:         raise RuntimeWriteRefused("snapshot omitted exact fact payload text")
717:     if (
718:         existing.kind,
719:         existing.ts,
720:         existing.observer,
721:         existing.origin,
722:         existing.payload_text,
723:         existing.signature,
724:     ) != (fact.kind, fact.ts, fact.observer, fact.origin, payload_text, inner_signature):
725:         raise RuntimeWriteRefused(f"fact id {chosen_id!r} already exists with different content")
726:     return OrdinaryWritePlan(
727:         captured_head=basis.captured_head,
728:         fact_id=chosen_id,
729:         tick_id=None,
730:         drafts=(),
731:         already_present=True,
732:         credential_bindings=session.evidence,
733:     )
734: 
735: 
736: def _ensure_current_basis(snapshot: QuerySnapshot, basis: ReadBasis) -> None:
737:     if basis.projected_through != basis.captured_head:
738:         raise ProjectionBehind("ordinary writes require a current projection snapshot")
739:     if snapshot.represented is None:
740:         raise RuntimeWriteRefused("ordinary writes require a represented projection")
741:     if snapshot.represented.lineage != basis.captured_head.lineage:
742:         raise NotAuthority("snapshot lineage differs from the captured ledger head")
743: 
744: 
745: def _current_write_basis(
746:     ledger: AttestedLedger, snapshot: QuerySnapshot, captured: Head
747: ) -> ReadBasis:
748:     """Complete a CURRENT query watermark without mistaking an advance for lag."""
749:     from .arrival_contract import ReadBasis
750: 
751:     watermark = snapshot.represented
752:     if watermark is None:
753:         raise RuntimeWriteRefused("ordinary emission requires a projection watermark")
754:     projected = complete_projection_custody(
755:         ledger,
756:         captured=captured,
757:         represented=watermark,
758:         lineage_refusal=NotAuthority,
759:         conflict_refusal=RuntimeWriteRefused,
760:     )
761:     if projected.ordinal < captured.ordinal:
762:         raise ProjectionBehind("CURRENT snapshot did not reach the captured head")
763:     return ReadBasis(
764:         lineage=captured.lineage,
765:         captured_head=captured,
766:         projected_through=projected,
767:         view_generation=snapshot.view_generation,
768:     )
769: 
770: 
771: def _require_signing_context(
772:     credentials: WriteCredentials,
773:     context: CapturedSigningContext | None,
774:     *,
775:     captured_head: Head,
776: ) -> CapturedSigningContext:
777:     if context is None or context.captured_head != captured_head:
778:         raise CredentialBindingRefused(
779:             "mapped signing requires authorization evidence from the captured head",
780:             captured_head=captured_head,
781:             use_position=captured_head.ordinal + 1,
782:         )
783:     return context
784: 
785: 
786: def _author_signature(
787:     credentials: WriteCredentials,
788:     session: CredentialResolutionSession,
789:     context: CapturedSigningContext | None,
790:     *,
791:     captured_head: Head,
792:     observer: str,
793:     domain: SigningDomain,
794:     purpose: CredentialPurpose,
795:     digest: str,
796:     legacy_signer: Callable[[str, str], str | None] | None,
797:     required: bool = False,
798: ) -> str | None:
799:     if not credentials.mapped:
800:         if legacy_signer is None:
801:             if required:
802:                 raise RuntimeWriteRefused(f"missing {domain.value} signer")
803:             return None
804:         signature = legacy_signer(observer, digest)
805:         if required and not signature:
806:             raise RuntimeWriteRefused(f"missing {domain.value} signature")
807:         return signature
808:     resolved_context = _require_signing_context(
809:         credentials, context, captured_head=captured_head
810:     )
811:     request = CredentialRequest(
812:         namespace=credentials.binding_namespace,
813:         observer=observer,
814:         domain=domain,
815:         purpose=purpose,
816:     )
817:     return session.sign(
818:         credentials,
819:         request,
820:         digest,
821:         context=resolved_context,
822:         authorized_keys=resolved_context.keys_for_author(observer),
823:         required=required,
824:     )
825: 
826: 
827: def _tick_signature(
828:     credentials: WriteCredentials,
829:     session: CredentialResolutionSession,
830:     context: CapturedSigningContext | None,
831:     *,
832:     captured_head: Head,
833:     digest: str,
834:     signed_era: bool,
835: ) -> str | None:
836:     if not credentials.mapped:
837:         signature = (
838:             credentials.tick_signer(digest)
839:             if credentials.tick_signer is not None
840:             else None
841:         )
842:         if signature is None and signed_era:
843:             raise RuntimeWriteRefused("refusing an unsigned tick in the signed tick era")
844:         return signature
845:     resolved_context = _require_signing_context(
846:         credentials, context, captured_head=captured_head
847:     )
848:     if credentials.receipt_observer is None:
849:         if signed_era:
850:             raise CredentialBindingRefused(
851:                 "missing-required",
852:                 captured_head=captured_head,
853:                 use_position=resolved_context.use_position,
854:             )
855:         return None
856:     request = CredentialRequest(
857:         namespace=credentials.binding_namespace,
858:         observer=credentials.receipt_observer,
859:         domain=SigningDomain.TICK,
860:         purpose=CredentialPurpose.RECEIPT,
861:     )
862:     return session.sign(
863:         credentials,
864:         request,
865:         digest,
866:         context=resolved_context,
867:         authorized_keys=resolved_context.receipt_keys,
868:         required=signed_era,
869:     )
870: 
871: 
872: def _build_effective_arrival_candidate(
873:     snapshot: QuerySnapshot,
874:     basis: ReadBasis,
875:     locator: VertexFile,
876:     *,
877:     fold_overrides: dict[str, FoldOverride] | None = None,
878: ) -> tuple[
879:     Vertex,
880:     VertexFile,
881:     tuple[dict[str, Any], ...],
882:     tuple[Any, ...],
883:     tuple[SnapshotFact, ...],
884:     tuple[SnapshotTick, ...],
885: ]:
886:     """Build a fresh, storeless candidate from this exact bounded snapshot.
887: 
888:     The locator contributes only ingress/residence fields. Effective documents,
889:     facts, and ticks come from the one CURRENT query snapshot, so no mutable
890:     caller vertex or legacy store can decide the next boundary.
891:     """
892:     _ensure_current_basis(snapshot, basis)
893:     facts = snapshot.facts(FactRequest(limit=None, include_internal=True, order="oldest")).items
894:     from .declaration import (
895:         Unhistorized,
896:         effective_declaration_from_documents,
897:         resolve_declaration_documents_from_snapshot,
898:         validate_arrival_declaration_anchor,
899:         validate_arrival_runtime_identity,
900:     )
901: 
902:     validate_arrival_declaration_anchor(snapshot.declaration_anchor, basis.captured_head)
903:     documents = resolve_declaration_documents_from_snapshot(snapshot.declaration_anchor, facts)
904:     if documents is None or isinstance(documents, Unhistorized):
905:         raise RuntimeWriteRefused("ordinary emission requires historized effective declarations")
906:     effective = effective_declaration_from_documents(documents, locator)
907:     if effective.vertices or effective.discover or effective.combine is not None:
908:         raise RuntimeWriteRefused("multi-store child execution is not in stage 3A")
909:     validate_arrival_runtime_identity(
910:         effective.name,
911:         effective.loops,
912:         refusal=RuntimeWriteRefused,
913:     )
914:     from .compiler import (
915:         CompiledVertex,
916:         compile_parse_pipelines,
917:         compile_sources,
918:         compile_sources_block,
919:         map_boundary,
920:         map_vertex_file,
921:         materialize_vertex,
922:     )
923: 
924:     base_dir = locator.path.parent if locator.path is not None else Path.cwd()
925:     sources, template_specs = compile_sources(effective, base_dir)
926:     for block in effective.sources_blocks or ():
927:         sources.append(compile_sources_block(block, effective.name))
928:     specs = map_vertex_file(effective)
929:     specs.update(template_specs)
930:     validate_arrival_runtime_identity(
931:         effective.name,
932:         specs,
933:         refusal=RuntimeWriteRefused,
934:     )
935:     if sources:
936:         from .executor import validate_dependency_graph
937: 
938:         validate_dependency_graph(sources)
939:     runtime_ticks = tuple(snapshot.ticks(TickRequest()))
940:     continuity_ticks = tuple(snapshot.ticks(TickRequest(since=float("-inf"))))
941:     from .arrival_boundary_continuity import (
942:         BoundaryContinuityConflict,
943:         analyze_boundary_continuity,
944:         collect_verified_parameter_rows,
945:     )
946: 
947:     verified_params = collect_verified_parameter_rows(
948:         snapshot.declaration_anchor,
949:         facts,
950:         target_documents=documents,
951:         base_dir=base_dir,
952:     )
953:     try:
954:         analyze_boundary_continuity(
955:             snapshot.declaration_anchor,
956:             facts,
957:             continuity_ticks,
958:             target_documents=documents,
959:             verified_params=verified_params,
960:             compiled_loop_names=set(specs) | {"cite"},
961:         )
962:     except BoundaryContinuityConflict as exc:
963:         raise BoundaryContinuityRefused(
964:             issue=exc.issue,
965:             basis=basis,
966:             effective_declaration=effective,
967:             cause=exc,
968:         ) from exc
969:     compiled = CompiledVertex(
970:         name=effective.name,
971:         specs=specs,
972:         children={},
973:         routes=effective.routes,
974:         store=effective.store,
975:         path=effective.path,
976:         boundary=tuple(map_boundary(boundary) for boundary in effective.boundary),
977:         parse_pipelines=compile_parse_pipelines(effective),
978:         strict=effective.strict,
979:         sources=sources or None,
980:         template_specs=template_specs or None,
981:     )
982:     candidate = materialize_vertex(compiled, fold_overrides=fold_overrides, attach_store=False)
983:     candidate.hydrate_snapshot(facts, runtime_ticks)
984:     return (
985:         candidate,
986:         effective,
987:         tuple(copy.deepcopy(documents)),
988:         tuple(copy.deepcopy(sources)),
989:         tuple(copy.deepcopy(facts)),
990:         tuple(copy.deepcopy(runtime_ticks)),
991:     )
992: 
993: 
994: def _hydrate_effective_arrival_candidate(
995:     snapshot: QuerySnapshot,
996:     basis: ReadBasis,
997:     locator: VertexFile,
998:     *,
999:     fold_overrides: dict[str, FoldOverride] | None = None,
1000: ) -> tuple[Vertex, VertexFile]:
1001:     candidate, effective, _documents, _sources, _facts, _ticks = (
1002:         _build_effective_arrival_candidate(
1003:             snapshot, basis, locator, fold_overrides=fold_overrides
1004:         )
1005:     )
1006:     return candidate, effective
1007: 
1008: 
1009: def hydrate_arrival_candidate(
1010:     snapshot: QuerySnapshot,
1011:     basis: ReadBasis,
1012:     locator: VertexFile,
1013:     *,
1014:     fold_overrides: dict[str, FoldOverride] | None = None,
1015: ) -> Vertex:
1016:     """Build the supported storeless candidate from this bounded snapshot."""
1017:     candidate, _effective = _hydrate_effective_arrival_candidate(
1018:         snapshot, basis, locator, fold_overrides=fold_overrides
1019:     )
1020:     return candidate
1021: 
1022: 
1023: def plan_ordinary_write(
1024:     snapshot: QuerySnapshot,
1025:     basis: ReadBasis,
1026:     vertex: Vertex,
1027:     fact: Fact,
1028:     *,
1029:     grant: Grant | None,
1030:     credentials: WriteCredentials,
1031:     custodian: str,
1032:     fact_id: str | None = None,
1033:     admit_undeclared: bool = False,
1034:     _mutate_candidate: bool = False,
1035:     _signing_context: CapturedSigningContext | None = None,
1036:     _credential_session: CredentialResolutionSession | None = None,
1037: ) -> OrdinaryWritePlan:
1038:     """Internal, caller-responsible planner for one local fact and tick.
1039: 
1040:     ``vertex`` must be a detached reconstruction of this exact snapshot.
1041:     Supported consumers use :func:`prepare_ordinary_write`, which creates it
1042:     through :func:`hydrate_arrival_candidate`; this lower-level seam exists
1043:     for focused engine composition and must not be offered as a coherent
1044:     writer from a stale caller-owned vertex.
1045:     """
1046:     _ensure_current_basis(snapshot, basis)
1047:     if getattr(vertex, "_store", None) is not None:
1048:         raise RuntimeWriteRefused("ordinary planning requires a detached vertex")
1049:     if getattr(vertex, "_has_children", False):
1050:         raise RuntimeWriteRefused("multi-store child execution is not in stage 3A")
1051:     from .declaration import validate_arrival_runtime_identity
1052: 
1053:     validate_arrival_runtime_identity(
1054:         vertex.name,
1055:         vertex.kinds,
1056:         refusal=RuntimeWriteRefused,
1057:     )
1058:     anchor = snapshot.declaration_anchor
1059:     if anchor.own_lineage is None or anchor.genesis is None:
1060:         raise RuntimeWriteRefused("ordinary emission requires a declaration anchor")
1061:     from .declaration import validate_arrival_declaration_anchor
1062: 
1063:     validate_arrival_declaration_anchor(anchor, basis.captured_head)
1064: 
1065:     credential_session = _credential_session or CredentialResolutionSession()
1066:     existing_plan = _existing_fact_plan(
1067:         snapshot,
1068:         basis,
1069:         fact,
1070:         credentials,
1071:         fact_id,
1072:         signing_context=_signing_context,
1073:         credential_session=credential_session,
1074:     )
1075:     if existing_plan is not None:
1076:         return existing_plan
1077:     facts = snapshot.facts(FactRequest(limit=None, include_internal=True, order="oldest")).items
1078:     # The neutral request is receipt-ordered, but keep this safety boundary at
1079:     # the chain calculation: window membership is coordinates, never a
1080:     # backend's event-time or identifier presentation order.
1081:     facts = tuple(sorted(facts, key=lambda row: (row.arrival_ordinal, row.arrival_seq)))
1082:     ticks = snapshot.ticks(TickRequest())
1083:     chosen_id = fact_id if fact_id is not None else str(ULID())
1084:     payload_text = _payload_text(fact.payload)
1085:     inner_signature = _author_signature(
1086:         credentials,
1087:         credential_session,
1088:         _signing_context,
1089:         captured_head=basis.captured_head,
1090:         observer=fact.observer,
1091:         domain=SigningDomain.FACT,
1092:         purpose=CredentialPurpose.AUTHORSHIP,
1093:         digest=fact_commitment_hash(
1094:             fact.kind, fact.ts, fact.observer, fact.origin, payload_text
1095:         ),
1096:         legacy_signer=credentials.fact_signer,
1097:     )
1098:     fact_row = (
1099:         chosen_id,
1100:         fact.kind,
1101:         fact.ts,
1102:         fact.observer,
1103:         fact.origin,
1104:         payload_text,
1105:         inner_signature,
1106:     )
1107:     candidate = vertex if _mutate_candidate else vertex.detached_copy()
1108:     receipt = candidate.plan_receive_receipt(
1109:         fact,
1110:         grant,
1111:         id_override=chosen_id,
1112:         admit_undeclared=admit_undeclared,
1113:     )
1114:     if not receipt.stored:
1115:         raise _AdmissionPlanRefused("admission rejected the fact before append")
1116:     fact_body = body_of_fact_row(fact_row)
1117:     outer_signature = _author_signature(
1118:         credentials,
1119:         credential_session,
1120:         _signing_context,
1121:         captured_head=basis.captured_head,
1122:         observer=fact.observer,
1123:         domain=SigningDomain.ARRIVAL,
1124:         purpose=CredentialPurpose.AUTHORSHIP,
1125:         digest=content_commitment(
1126:             "fact", fact.ts, fact.observer, fact.origin, fact_body
1127:         ),
1128:         legacy_signer=credentials.arrival_signer,
1129:     )
1130:     drafts = [
1131:         RecordDraft(
1132:             kind="fact",
1133:             authored_at=fact.ts,
1134:             observer=fact.observer,
1135:             origin=fact.origin,
1136:             body=fact_body,
1137:             signature=outer_signature,
1138:         )
1139:     ]
1140:     tick_id: str | None = None
1141:     if receipt.tick is not None:
1142:         previous_hash, window_start = _current_tick_context(ticks, current_fact_id=chosen_id)
1143:         tick = receipt.tick
1144:         tick_id = str(ULID())
1145:         if any(existing.id == tick_id for existing in ticks):
1146:             raise RuntimeWriteRefused(f"generated tick id {tick_id!r} already exists")
1147:         tick_payload_text = _payload_text(tick.payload)
1148:         tick_row = (
1149:             tick_id,
1150:             tick.name,
1151:             tick.ts.timestamp(),
1152:             None if tick.since is None else tick.since.timestamp(),
1153:             tick.origin,
1154:             tick_payload_text,
1155:             previous_hash,
1156:             window_start,
1157:             chosen_id,
1158:             _window_hash(facts, start=window_start, appended=fact_row),
1159:         )
1160:         signed_era = any(
1161:             existing.signature is not None and existing.window_hash is not None
1162:             for existing in ticks
1163:         )
1164:         tick_signature = _tick_signature(
1165:             credentials,
1166:             credential_session,
1167:             _signing_context,
1168:             captured_head=basis.captured_head,
1169:             digest=tick_commitment_hash(tick_row),
1170:             signed_era=signed_era,
1171:         )
1172:         tick_body = body_of_tick_row((*tick_row, tick_signature))
1173:         drafts.append(
1174:             RecordDraft(
1175:                 kind="tick",
1176:                 authored_at=tick.ts.timestamp(),
1177:                 observer=custodian,
1178:                 origin=tick.origin,
1179:                 body=tick_body,
1180:                 signature=None,
1181:             )
1182:         )
1183:     return OrdinaryWritePlan(
1184:         captured_head=basis.captured_head,
1185:         fact_id=chosen_id,
1186:         tick_id=tick_id,
1187:         drafts=tuple(drafts),
1188:         tick_run=None if receipt.tick is None else receipt.tick.run,
1189:         credential_bindings=credential_session.evidence,
1190:     )
1191: 
1192: 
1193: def _custodian_from_verified_genesis(ledger: AttestedLedger) -> str:
1194:     """Return the physical lineage's tick custodian from its verified record 0."""
1195:     genesis = ledger.read(0)
1196:     if genesis.get("k") != "genesis":
1197:         raise RuntimeWriteRefused("ledger record 0 is not a physical genesis")
1198:     observer = genesis.get("observer")
1199:     if not isinstance(observer, str) or not observer:
1200:         raise RuntimeWriteRefused("physical genesis has no custodian observer")
1201:     return observer
1202: 
1203: 
1204: def capture_runtime(
1205:     registry: BackendRegistry,
1206:     descriptor: StoreDescriptor,
1207:     locator: VertexFile,
1208:     *,
1209:     fold_overrides: dict[str, FoldOverride] | None = None,
1210:     source_mode: bool = False,
1211:     evaluated_at: float | None = None,
1212:     credentials: WriteCredentials | None = None,
1213: ) -> RuntimeCapture:
1214:     """Capture one immutable, closed-handle Authority/CURRENT runtime view."""
1215:     from .arrival_contract import Profile, ProjectionRequirement
1216: 
1217:     if descriptor.role is not Profile.AUTHORITY:
1218:         raise NotAuthority("runtime capture requires an Authority descriptor role")
1219:     ledger, query = registry.open(descriptor)
1220:     snapshot = None
1221:     try:
1222:         if not isinstance(ledger, AttestedLedger):
1223:             raise TypeError("BackendRegistry.open must return an AttestedLedger")
1224:         comparison = ledger.opened.comparison
1225:         if not isinstance(comparison, Compared):
1226:             if isinstance(comparison, PreGenesis) and comparison.ledger_refusal is not None:
1227:                 raise comparison.ledger_refusal
1228:             if isinstance(comparison, Indeterminate):
1229:                 raise comparison.refusal
1230:             raise RuntimeWriteRefused("runtime capture requires a minted compared ledger")
1231:         captured = comparison.presented
1232:         snapshot = query.open_snapshot(
1233:             captured_head=captured, requirement=ProjectionRequirement.CURRENT
1234:         )
1235:         basis = _current_write_basis(ledger, snapshot, captured)
1236:         candidate, effective, documents, sources, facts, ticks = (
1237:             _build_effective_arrival_candidate(
1238:                 snapshot, basis, locator, fold_overrides=fold_overrides
1239:             )
1240:         )
1241:         at = time.time() if evaluated_at is None else evaluated_at
1242:         post_boundary_candidate = candidate.detached_copy() if source_mode else None
1243:         pending = (
1244:             post_boundary_candidate.plan_pending_boundaries(facts, ticks, evaluated_at=at)
1245:             if post_boundary_candidate is not None
1246:             else ()
1247:         )
1248:         captured_locator = _capture_locator_ingress(locator)
1249:         signing_context = None
1250:         if credentials is not None and credentials.mapped:
1251:             from .arrival import key_registry_from_records
1252: 
1253:             verifier = credentials.signature_verifier
1254:             if verifier is None:  # guarded by WriteCredentials construction
1255:                 raise CredentialBindingRefused(
1256:                     "mapped signing requires a signature verifier",
1257:                     captured_head=captured,
1258:                     use_position=captured.ordinal + 1,
1259:                 )
1260:             try:
1261:                 key_registry, _key_rows = key_registry_from_records(
1262:                     ledger.scan(through=captured),
1263:                     lambda key, signature, digest: verifier(
1264:                         SigningDomain.ARRIVAL, key, signature, digest
1265:                     ),
1266:                 )
1267:             except CredentialBindingRefused as exc:
1268:                 raise CredentialBindingRefused(
1269:                     exc.reason,
1270:                     request=exc.request,
1271:                     captured_head=captured,
1272:                     use_position=captured.ordinal + 1,
1273:                     evidence=exc.evidence,
1274:                 ) from exc
1275:             except Exception as exc:
1276:                 raise CredentialBindingRefused(
1277:                     "key-history-unverified",
1278:                     captured_head=captured,
1279:                     use_position=captured.ordinal + 1,
1280:                 ) from exc
1281:             author_keys = tuple(
1282:                 (
1283:                     observer,
1284:                     tuple(
1285:                         key
1286:                         for key, _introduced in key_registry.keys_valid_at(
1287:                             observer, captured.ordinal + 1
1288:                         )
1289:                     ),
1290:                 )
1291:                 for observer in sorted(key_registry.introductions)
1292:             )
1293:             receipt_keys = tuple(
1294:                 observer.key
1295:                 for observer in (effective.observers or ())
1296:                 if isinstance(observer.key, str) and observer.key
1297:             )
1298:             signing_context = CapturedSigningContext(
1299:                 captured_head=captured,
1300:                 author_keys=author_keys,
1301:                 receipt_keys=receipt_keys,
1302:             )
1303:         return RuntimeCapture(
1304:             basis=basis,
1305:             custodian=_custodian_from_verified_genesis(ledger),
1306:             max_atomic_records=ledger.capabilities().max_atomic_records,
1307:             evaluated_at=at,
1308:             _effective_declaration=effective,
1309:             _locator=captured_locator,
1310:             _declaration_anchor=copy.deepcopy(snapshot.declaration_anchor),
1311:             _declaration_documents=copy.deepcopy(documents),
1312:             _facts=copy.deepcopy(facts),
1313:             _ticks=copy.deepcopy(ticks),
1314:             _candidate_template=candidate.detached_copy(),
1315:             _post_boundary_candidate_template=(
1316:                 None
1317:                 if post_boundary_candidate is None
1318:                 else post_boundary_candidate.detached_copy()
1319:             ),
1320:             _sources=copy.deepcopy(sources),
1321:             _pending_boundaries=copy.deepcopy(tuple(pending)),
1322:             _signing_context=signing_context,
1323:         )
1324:     finally:
1325:         if snapshot is not None:
1326:             _close_quietly(snapshot)
1327:         _close_quietly(query)
1328:         _close_quietly(ledger)
1329: 
1330: 
1331: def prepare_ordinary_write(
1332:     registry: BackendRegistry,
1333:     descriptor: StoreDescriptor,
1334:     locator: VertexFile,
1335:     fact: Fact,
1336:     *,
1337:     credentials: WriteCredentials,
1338:     fact_id: str | None = None,
1339:     fold_overrides: dict[str, FoldOverride] | None = None,
1340:     admit_undeclared: bool = False,
1341: ) -> OrdinaryWritePlan:
1342:     """Capture a CURRENT basis and prepare one retryable ordinary write.
1343: 
1344:     The writer opens its own registry handles because a read consumer never
1345:     receives append capability.  It closes those handles before returning the
1346:     detached plan; execution later compares the same complete head under the
1347:     ledger fence and refuses any intervening append.
1348:     """
1349:     capture = capture_runtime(
1350:         registry,
1351:         descriptor,
1352:         locator,
1353:         fold_overrides=fold_overrides,
1354:         credentials=credentials,
1355:     )
1356:     basis = capture.basis
1357:     effective = capture.effective_declaration
1358:     candidate = capture._candidate_copy()
1359:     snapshot = _BatchSnapshot(capture, capture._facts, capture._ticks)
1360:     credential_session = CredentialResolutionSession()
1361:     duplicate = _existing_fact_plan(
1362:         snapshot,
1363:         basis,
1364:         fact,
1365:         credentials,
1366:         fact_id,
1367:         signing_context=capture._signing_context,
1368:         credential_session=credential_session,
1369:     )
1370:     if duplicate is not None:
1371:         return replace(
1372:             duplicate,
1373:             effective_declaration=effective,
1374:             custodian=capture.custodian,
1375:             admit_undeclared=admit_undeclared,
1376:         )
1377:     grant = None
1378:     try:
1379:         from .admission import AdmissionError, grant_for_observer
1380: 
1381:         grant = grant_for_observer(effective, fact.observer)
1382:         plan = plan_ordinary_write(
1383:             snapshot,
1384:             basis,
1385:             candidate,
1386:             fact,
1387:             grant=grant,
1388:             credentials=credentials,
1389:             custodian=capture.custodian,
1390:             fact_id=fact_id,
1391:             admit_undeclared=admit_undeclared,
1392:             _signing_context=capture._signing_context,
1393:             _credential_session=credential_session,
1394:         )
1395:     except (AdmissionError, _AdmissionPlanRefused) as exc:
1396:         raise OrdinaryWritePreparationRefused(
1397:             effective_declaration=effective,
1398:             admission_grant=grant,
1399:             custodian=capture.custodian,
1400:             basis=basis,
1401:             fact=fact,
1402:             cause=exc,
1403:         ) from exc
1404:     return replace(
1405:         plan,
1406:         effective_declaration=effective,
1407:         admission_grant=grant,
1408:         custodian=capture.custodian,
1409:         admit_undeclared=admit_undeclared,
1410:     )
1411: 
1412: 
1413: def execute_ordinary_write(
1414:     registry: BackendRegistry,
1415:     descriptor: StoreDescriptor,
1416:     plan: OrdinaryWritePlan,
1417:     *,
1418:     after_commit: Callable[[Head], object] | None = None,
1419: ) -> CommittedWrite:
1420:     """Append a previously prepared plan once against its exact full head.
1421: 
1422:     Projection publication is deliberately not attempted in 3A.  A successful
1423:     result says only that custody committed; maintenance is an explicit later
1424:     operation.  ``after_commit`` is the optional injected maintenance seam:
1425:     it receives the durable ``Commit.after`` and cannot change the append
1426:     outcome.  ``NotWitnessed`` already carries the durable commit and is
1427:     intentionally propagated rather than relabeled as a refusal.
1428:     """
1429:     from .arrival_contract import Profile
1430: 
1431:     if descriptor.role is not Profile.AUTHORITY:
1432:         raise NotAuthority("ordinary writes require an Authority descriptor role")
1433:     ledger, query = registry.open(descriptor)
1434:     try:
1435:         if not isinstance(ledger, AttestedLedger):
1436:             raise TypeError("BackendRegistry.open must return an AttestedLedger")
1437:         comparison = ledger.opened.comparison
1438:         if isinstance(comparison, PreGenesis):
1439:             if comparison.ledger_refusal is not None:
1440:                 raise comparison.ledger_refusal
1441:             raise RuntimeWriteRefused("ordinary emission requires a minted ledger")
1442:         if isinstance(comparison, Indeterminate):
1443:             raise comparison.refusal
1444:         if not isinstance(comparison, Compared):
1445:             raise TypeError(f"unsupported head comparison {comparison!r}")
1446:         if plan.already_present:
1447:             return CommittedWrite(
1448:                 commit=None,
1449:                 fact_id=plan.fact_id,
1450:                 tick_id=None,
1451:                 projection=ProjectionOutcome.NOT_REQUESTED,
1452:             )
1453:         if comparison.presented != plan.captured_head:
1454:             raise RuntimeWriteRefused("captured head changed before append")
1455:         try:
1456:             commit = ledger.append(plan.captured_head, plan.drafts)
1457:         except NotWitnessed:
1458:             raise
1459:         except ContractRefusal:
1460:             raise
1461:         except Exception as exc:
1462:             raise WriteCommitUnknown(plan, exc) from exc
1463:         if after_commit is not None:
1464:             try:
1465:                 after_commit(commit.after)
1466:             except Exception as exc:
1467:                 raise PostCommitProjectionFailed(commit, plan, exc) from exc
1468:             projection = ProjectionOutcome.SYNCED
1469:         else:
1470:             projection = ProjectionOutcome.NOT_REQUESTED
1471:         return CommittedWrite(
1472:             commit=commit,
1473:             fact_id=plan.fact_id,
1474:             tick_id=plan.tick_id,
1475:             projection=projection,
1476:         )
1477:     finally:
1478:         _close_quietly(query)
1479:         _close_quietly(ledger)
1480: 
1481: 
1482: def _pack_batch_drafts(
1483:     drafts: Sequence[RecordDraft],
1484:     credentials: WriteCredentials,
1485:     *,
1486:     captured_head: Head | None = None,
1487:     signing_context: CapturedSigningContext | None = None,
1488:     credential_session: CredentialResolutionSession | None = None,
1489: ) -> tuple[RecordDraft, ...]:
1490:     """Turn adjacent ordinary fact drafts into wire-valid same-author batches."""
1491:     packed: list[RecordDraft] = []
1492:     held: list[RecordDraft] = []
1493:     session = credential_session or CredentialResolutionSession()
1494: 
1495:     def flush() -> None:
1496:         if not held:
1497:             return
1498:         if len(held) == 1:
1499:             packed.append(held[0])
1500:         else:
1501:             first = held[0]
1502:             rows = [
1503:                 (
1504:                     row.body["id"],
1505:                     row.body["kind"],
1506:                     row.body["ts"],
1507:                     row.body["observer"],
1508:                     row.body["origin"],
1509:                     row.body["payload"],
1510:                     row.body.get("signature"),
1511:                 )
1512:                 for row in held
1513:             ]
1514:             body = body_of_batch(rows)
1515:             digest = content_commitment(
1516:                 "batch", first.authored_at, first.observer, first.origin, body
1517:             )
1518:             if credentials.mapped:
1519:                 if captured_head is None:
1520:                     raise CredentialBindingRefused(
1521:                         "mapped batch signing requires a captured head"
1522:                     )
1523:                 signature = _author_signature(
1524:                     credentials,
1525:                     session,
1526:                     signing_context,
1527:                     captured_head=captured_head,
1528:                     observer=first.observer,
1529:                     domain=SigningDomain.ARRIVAL,
1530:                     purpose=CredentialPurpose.AUTHORSHIP,
1531:                     digest=digest,
1532:                     legacy_signer=None,
1533:                 )
1534:             else:
1535:                 signature = (
1536:                     credentials.arrival_signer(first.observer, digest)
1537:                     if credentials.arrival_signer is not None
1538:                     else None
1539:                 )
1540:             packed.append(
1541:                 RecordDraft(
1542:                     kind="batch",
1543:                     authored_at=first.authored_at,
1544:                     observer=first.observer,
1545:                     origin=first.origin,
1546:                     body=body,
1547:                     signature=signature,
1548:                 )
1549:             )
1550:         held.clear()
1551: 
1552:     for draft in drafts:
1553:         if draft.kind == "fact" and (not held or held[0].observer == draft.observer):
1554:             held.append(draft)
1555:             continue
1556:         flush()
1557:         if draft.kind == "fact":
1558:             held.append(draft)
1559:         else:
1560:             packed.append(draft)
1561:     flush()
1562:     return tuple(packed)
1563: 
1564: 
1565: def _pending_tick_draft(
1566:     tick: Any,
1567:     facts: tuple[SnapshotFact, ...],
1568:     ticks: tuple[SnapshotTick, ...],
1569:     *,
1570:     credentials: WriteCredentials,
1571:     custodian: str,
1572:     captured_head: Head,
1573:     signing_context: CapturedSigningContext | None,
1574:     credential_session: CredentialResolutionSession,
1575: ) -> RecordDraft:
1576:     """Encode one captured pending boundary without re-evaluating it."""
1577:     if not facts:
1578:         raise RuntimeWriteRefused("a pending boundary requires captured fact evidence")
1579:     current_fact = max(facts, key=lambda row: (row.arrival_ordinal, row.arrival_seq))
1580:     previous_hash, window_start = _current_tick_context(ticks, current_fact_id=current_fact.id)
1581:     tick_id = str(ULID())
1582:     payload_text = _payload_text(tick.payload)
1583:     row = (
1584:         tick_id,
1585:         tick.name,
1586:         tick.ts.timestamp(),
1587:         None if tick.since is None else tick.since.timestamp(),
1588:         tick.origin,
1589:         payload_text,
1590:         previous_hash,
1591:         window_start,
1592:         current_fact.id,
1593:         _window_hash(facts, start=window_start),
1594:     )
1595:     signed_era = any(
1596:         existing.signature is not None and existing.window_hash is not None for existing in ticks
1597:     )
1598:     signature = _tick_signature(
1599:         credentials,
1600:         credential_session,
1601:         signing_context,
1602:         captured_head=captured_head,
1603:         digest=tick_commitment_hash(row),
1604:         signed_era=signed_era,
1605:     )
1606:     return RecordDraft(
1607:         kind="tick",
1608:         authored_at=tick.ts.timestamp(),
1609:         observer=custodian,
1610:         origin=tick.origin,
1611:         body=body_of_tick_row((*row, signature)),
1612:         signature=None,
1613:     )
1614: 
1615: 
1616: def plan_batch_from_capture(
1617:     capture: RuntimeCapture,
1618:     items: Sequence[BatchFactInput],
1619:     *,
1620:     credentials: WriteCredentials,
1621:     include_pending_boundaries: bool = False,
1622: ) -> BatchWritePlan:
1623:     """Plan one batch from frozen evidence without reopening the registry."""
1624:     from .admission import AdmissionError, grant_for_observer
1625: 
1626:     pending = capture._pending_boundaries if include_pending_boundaries else ()
1627:     if not items and not pending:
1628:         raise RuntimeWriteRefused("batch requires at least one fact or pending boundary")
1629:     basis = capture.basis
1630:     effective = capture._effective_declaration
1631:     candidate = capture._candidate_copy(after_pending_boundaries=include_pending_boundaries)
1632:     from .declaration import validate_arrival_runtime_identity
1633: 
1634:     validate_arrival_runtime_identity(
1635:         candidate.name,
1636:         candidate.kinds,
1637:         refusal=RuntimeWriteRefused,
1638:     )
1639:     facts = copy.deepcopy(capture._facts)
1640:     ticks = copy.deepcopy(capture._ticks)
1641:     logical: list[RecordDraft] = []
1642:     pending_ids: list[str] = []
1643:     pending_names: list[str] = []
1644:     pending_runs: list[str | None] = []
1645:     credential_session = CredentialResolutionSession()
1646:     for sequence, tick in enumerate(copy.deepcopy(pending)):
1647:         draft = _pending_tick_draft(
1648:             tick,
1649:             facts,
1650:             ticks,
1651:             credentials=credentials,
1652:             custodian=capture.custodian,
1653:             captured_head=basis.captured_head,
1654:             signing_context=capture._signing_context,
1655:             credential_session=credential_session,
1656:         )
1657:         logical.append(draft)
1658:         pending_ids.append(str(draft.body["id"]))
1659:         pending_names.append(str(draft.body["name"]))
1660:         pending_runs.append(tick.run)
1661:         ticks = (
1662:             *ticks,
1663:             _tick_from_draft(draft, basis.captured_head.ordinal + 1, sequence),
1664:         )
1665: 
1666:     results: list[BatchItemResult] = []
1667:     for index, item in enumerate(items):
1668:         synthetic = _BatchSnapshot(capture, facts, ticks)
1669:         try:
1670:             plan = _existing_fact_plan(
1671:                 synthetic,
1672:                 basis,
1673:                 item.fact,
1674:                 credentials,
1675:                 item.fact_id,
1676:                 signing_context=capture._signing_context,
1677:                 credential_session=credential_session,
1678:             )
1679:             if plan is None:
1680:                 grant = grant_for_observer(effective, item.fact.observer)
1681:                 plan = plan_ordinary_write(
1682:                     synthetic,
1683:                     basis,
1684:                     candidate,
1685:                     item.fact,
1686:                     grant=grant,
1687:                     credentials=credentials,
1688:                     custodian=capture.custodian,
1689:                     fact_id=item.fact_id,
1690:                     admit_undeclared=item.admit_undeclared,
1691:                     _mutate_candidate=True,
1692:                     _signing_context=capture._signing_context,
1693:                     _credential_session=credential_session,
1694:                 )
1695:         except (AdmissionError, _AdmissionPlanRefused) as exc:
1696:             raise BatchWritePreparationRefused(
1697:                 input_index=index,
1698:                 item=item,
1699:                 effective_declaration=capture.effective_declaration,
1700:                 custodian=capture.custodian,
1701:                 basis=basis,
1702:                 cause=exc,
1703:                 admission_refused=True,
1704:             ) from exc
1705:         except CredentialBindingRefused as exc:
1706:             raise BatchWritePreparationRefused(
1707:                 input_index=index,
1708:                 item=item,
1709:                 effective_declaration=capture.effective_declaration,
1710:                 custodian=capture.custodian,
1711:                 basis=basis,
1712:                 cause=exc,
1713:                 admission_refused=False,
1714:             ) from exc
1715:         except RuntimeWriteRefused as exc:
1716:             raise BatchWritePreparationRefused(
1717:                 input_index=index,
1718:                 item=item,
1719:                 effective_declaration=capture.effective_declaration,
1720:                 custodian=capture.custodian,
1721:                 basis=basis,
1722:                 cause=exc,
1723:                 admission_refused=False,
1724:             ) from exc
1725:         tick_draft = next((draft for draft in plan.drafts if draft.kind == "tick"), None)
1726:         results.append(
1727:             BatchItemResult(
1728:                 fact_id=plan.fact_id,
1729:                 tick_id=plan.tick_id,
1730:                 tick_name=None if tick_draft is None else tick_draft.body["name"],
1731:                 already_present=plan.already_present,
1732:                 tick_run=plan.tick_run,
1733:             )
1734:         )
1735:         if plan.already_present:
1736:             continue
1737:         ordinal = basis.captured_head.ordinal + 1 + len(logical)
1738:         for sequence, draft in enumerate(plan.drafts):
1739:             if draft.kind == "fact":
1740:                 facts = (*facts, _fact_from_draft(draft, ordinal, sequence))
1741:             elif draft.kind == "tick":
1742:                 ticks = (*ticks, _tick_from_draft(draft, ordinal, sequence))
1743:         logical.extend(plan.drafts)
1744: 
1745:     packed = _pack_batch_drafts(
1746:         logical,
1747:         credentials,
1748:         captured_head=basis.captured_head,
1749:         signing_context=capture._signing_context,
1750:         credential_session=credential_session,
1751:     )
1752:     if capture.max_atomic_records is not None and len(packed) > capture.max_atomic_records:
1753:         raise AtomicLimitExceeded(
1754:             f"{len(packed)} packed records exceeds this backend's configured "
1755:             f"atomic limit of {capture.max_atomic_records}; the batch was refused before append"
1756:         )
1757:     return BatchWritePlan(
1758:         captured_head=basis.captured_head,
1759:         items=tuple(results),
1760:         drafts=packed,
1761:         effective_declaration=capture.effective_declaration,
1762:         custodian=capture.custodian,
1763:         pending_tick_ids=tuple(pending_ids),
1764:         pending_tick_names=tuple(pending_names),
1765:         pending_tick_runs=tuple(pending_runs),
1766:         credential_bindings=credential_session.evidence,
1767:     )
1768: 
1769: 
1770: def prepare_batch_write(
1771:     registry: BackendRegistry,
1772:     descriptor: StoreDescriptor,
1773:     locator: VertexFile,
1774:     items: Sequence[BatchFactInput],
1775:     *,
1776:     credentials: WriteCredentials,
1777:     fold_overrides: dict[str, FoldOverride] | None = None,
1778: ) -> BatchWritePlan:
1779:     """Capture once, then prepare every item against that exact evidence."""
1780:     if not items:
1781:         raise RuntimeWriteRefused("batch requires at least one fact")
1782:     capture = capture_runtime(
1783:         registry,
1784:         descriptor,
1785:         locator,
1786:         fold_overrides=fold_overrides,
1787:         credentials=credentials,
1788:     )
1789:     return plan_batch_from_capture(capture, items, credentials=credentials)
1790: 
1791: 
1792: def execute_batch_write(
1793:     registry: BackendRegistry,
1794:     descriptor: StoreDescriptor,
1795:     plan: BatchWritePlan,
1796:     *,
1797:     after_commit: Callable[[Head], object] | None = None,
1798: ) -> CommittedBatchWrite:
1799:     """Append a prepared batch exactly once; no item is retried separately."""
1800:     from .arrival_contract import Profile
1801: 
1802:     if descriptor.role is not Profile.AUTHORITY:
1803:         raise NotAuthority("batch writes require an Authority descriptor role")
1804:     if not plan.drafts:
1805:         return CommittedBatchWrite(
1806:             None,
1807:             plan.items,
1808:             ProjectionOutcome.NOT_REQUESTED,
1809:             plan.pending_tick_ids,
1810:             plan.pending_tick_names,
1811:             plan.pending_tick_runs,
1812:         )
1813:     ledger, query = registry.open(descriptor)
1814:     try:
1815:         if not isinstance(ledger, AttestedLedger):
1816:             raise TypeError("BackendRegistry.open must return an AttestedLedger")
1817:         comparison = ledger.opened.comparison
1818:         if isinstance(comparison, PreGenesis):
1819:             if comparison.ledger_refusal is not None:
1820:                 raise comparison.ledger_refusal
1821:             raise RuntimeWriteRefused("batch emission requires a minted ledger")
1822:         if isinstance(comparison, Indeterminate):
1823:             raise comparison.refusal
1824:         if not isinstance(comparison, Compared):
1825:             raise TypeError(f"unsupported head comparison {comparison!r}")
1826:         if comparison.presented != plan.captured_head:
1827:             raise RuntimeWriteRefused("captured head changed before batch append")
1828:         try:
1829:             commit = ledger.append(plan.captured_head, plan.drafts)
1830:         except NotWitnessed:
1831:             raise
1832:         except ContractRefusal:
1833:             raise
1834:         except Exception as exc:
1835:             raise BatchWriteCommitUnknown(plan, exc) from exc
1836:         if after_commit is not None:
1837:             try:
1838:                 after_commit(commit.after)
1839:             except Exception as exc:
1840:                 raise BatchPostCommitProjectionFailed(commit, plan, exc) from exc
1841:         projection = ProjectionOutcome.SYNCED if after_commit else ProjectionOutcome.NOT_REQUESTED
1842:         return CommittedBatchWrite(
1843:             commit,
1844:             plan.items,
1845:             projection,
1846:             plan.pending_tick_ids,
1847:             plan.pending_tick_names,
1848:             plan.pending_tick_runs,
1849:         )
1850:     finally:
1851:         _close_quietly(query)
1852:         _close_quietly(ledger)

===== libs/engine/src/engine/arrival_declarations.py =====
1: """Prepare, apply, and recover signed declaration edits on Arrival.
2: 
3: Preparation captures one attested read basis and exact signed records. Apply
4: uses a durable intent and full-head CAS; recovery reconciles a known suffix and
5: finishes projection/cache work without resigning or overwriting competing data.
6: """
7:

94: 
95: 
96: class DeclarationPreparationError(ArrivalError):
97:     """The declaration edit could not be prepared without mutation."""
98: 
99: 
100: class DeclarationPreparationRefused(DeclarationPreparationError):
101:     """A declaration edit input or signed draft failed a preparation gate."""
102: 
103:     def __init__(
104:         self,
105:         *args: object,
106:         coordinator_phase: str | None = None,
107:         effects: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, str]] | None = None,
108:         captured_head: Head | None = None,
109:         projected_head: Head | None = None,
110:     ) -> None:
111:         super().__init__(*args)
112:         self.coordinator_phase = coordinator_phase
113:         self.effects = effects
114:         if captured_head is not None:
115:             self.captured_head = captured_head
116:         if projected_head is not None:
117:             self.projected_head = projected_head
118: 
119: 
120: def _preparation_refused(
121:     message: str,
122:     *,
123:     captured_head: Head | None = None,
124:     projected_head: Head | None = None,
125: ) -> DeclarationPreparationRefused:
126:     return DeclarationPreparationRefused(
127:         message,
128:         coordinator_phase="prepare",
129:         effects={
130:             "custody": {"attempt": "not-entered", "state": "not-attempted"}
131:         },
132:         captured_head=captured_head,
133:         projected_head=projected_head,
134:     )
135: 
136: 
137: class DeclarationApplyError(ArrivalError):
138:     """A declaration apply or recovery operation could not complete."""
139: 
140: 
141: class DeclarationStale(DeclarationApplyError):
142:     """The captured full head or declaration cache moved before append."""
143: 
144: 
145: class DeclarationNotApplied(DeclarationApplyError):
146:     """A durable prepared intent is proven to have reached no append."""
147: 
148: 
149: class DeclarationOutcomeUnknown(DeclarationApplyError):
150:     """Append transport failed without proving whether the records landed."""
151: 
152:     def __init__(self, message: str, *, head: Head | None, intent_path: Path,
153:                  fact_ids: tuple[str, ...], cause: BaseException) -> None:
154:         super().__init__(message)
155:         self.head = head
156:         self.intent_path = intent_path
157:         self.fact_ids = fact_ids
158:         self.cause = cause
159: 
160: 
161: class DeclarationUnwitnessed(DeclarationApplyError):
162:     """Append committed, but its custody journal witness was unavailable."""
163: 
164:     def __init__(self, message: str, *, head: Head, commit: Any,
165:                  intent_path: Path, fact_ids: tuple[str, ...], cause: BaseException) -> None:
166:         super().__init__(message)
167:         self.head = head
168:         self.commit = commit
169:         self.intent_path = intent_path
170:         self.fact_ids = fact_ids
171:         self.cause = cause
172: 
173: 
174: class DeclarationCommittedIncomplete(DeclarationApplyError):
175:     """Append is known durable, but sync/publication/cleanup remains pending."""
176: 
177:     def __init__(self, message: str, *, phase: str, head: Head,
178:                  commit: Any, intent_path: Path, fact_ids: tuple[str, ...],
179:                  cause: BaseException) -> None:
180:         super().__init__(message)
181:         self.phase = phase
182:         self.head = head
183:         self.commit = commit
184:         self.intent_path = intent_path
185:         self.fact_ids = fact_ids
186:         self.cause = cause

188: 
189: @dataclass(frozen=True)
190: class DeclarationEditPlan:
191:     """Exact, signed declaration transition prepared against one Arrival head."""
192: 
193:     status: Literal["planned", "noop"]
194:     target_path: Path
195:     descriptor: StoreDescriptor
196:     lineage: str
197:     basis: ReadBasis
198:     captured_head: Head
199:     before_documents: tuple[Mapping[str, Any], ...]
200:     proposed_documents: tuple[Mapping[str, Any], ...]
201:     changes: tuple[Change, ...]
202:     authored_at: float | None
203:     observer: str
204:     drafts: tuple[RecordDraft, ...]
205:     fact_ids: tuple[str, ...]
206:     key_introductions: tuple[str, ...]
207:     old_cache_bytes: bytes
208:     old_cache_sha256: str
209:     proposed_text: str
210:     atomic_limit: int | None
211:     exact_drafts: str
212:     credential_bindings: tuple[CredentialBindingEvidence, ...] = ()
213: 
214: 
215: @dataclass(frozen=True)
216: class DeclarationEditResult:
217:     """Apply/recovery outcome with exact identity and reconciliation evidence.
218: 
219:     ``file_written`` reports publication by this invocation. ``phase`` reports
220:     the reconciled operation state, including an already-published cache.
221:     """
222: 
223:     status: Literal[
224:         "noop", "applied", "stale", "refused", "unknown", "unwitnessed",
225:         "committed-incomplete", "recovered", "not-applied", "conflict",
226:     ]
227:     target_path: Path
228:     descriptor: StoreDescriptor
229:     lineage: str
230:     basis: ReadBasis
231:     captured_head: Head
232:     head: Head | None
233:     commit: Any | None
234:     fact_ids: tuple[str, ...]
235:     intent_path: Path | None
236:     phase: str
237:     file_written: bool
238:     changes: tuple[Change, ...] | None
239: 
240: 
241: def _close_quietly(handle: object | None) -> None:
242:     if handle is None:
243:         return
244:     close = getattr(handle, "close", None)
245:     if callable(close):
246:         with suppress(Exception):
247:             close()
248: 
249: 
250: def _draft_to_dict(draft: RecordDraft) -> dict[str, Any]:
251:     return {
252:         "kind": draft.kind,
253:         "authored_at": draft.authored_at,
254:         "observer": draft.observer,
255:         "origin": draft.origin,
256:         "body": dict(draft.body),
257:         "signature": draft.signature,
258:     }
259: 
260: 
261: def _exact_drafts(drafts: Sequence[RecordDraft]) -> str:
262:     """Canonical preparation snapshot used to detect nested plan mutation."""
263:     return json.dumps(
264:         [_draft_to_dict(draft) for draft in drafts],
265:         ensure_ascii=False,
266:         sort_keys=True,
267:         separators=(",", ":"),
268:     )
269: 
270: 
271: def declaration_intent_path(target: Path | str) -> Path:
272:     """The exclusive durable sidecar for one declaration edit."""
273:     target_path = Path(target).resolve()
274:     return target_path.with_name(target_path.name + ".arrival-decl.intent")
275: 
276: 
277: def _declaration_lock_path(target: Path) -> Path:
278:     return target.with_name(target.name + ".arrival-decl.lock")
279: 
280: 
281: @contextmanager
282: def _declaration_lock(target: Path):
283:     """Serialize apply/recovery while releasing the lock on process death."""
284:     lock_path = _declaration_lock_path(target)
285:     lock_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
286:     with lock_path.open("a+b") as lock_fh:
287:         fcntl.flock(lock_fh.fileno(), fcntl.LOCK_EX)
288:         try:
289:             yield
290:         finally:
291:             fcntl.flock(lock_fh.fileno(), fcntl.LOCK_UN)
292: 
293: 
294: def _head_dict(head: Head | None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
295:     if head is None:
296:         return None
297:     return {
298:         "lineage": head.lineage,
299:         "ordinal": head.ordinal,
300:         "record_hash": head.record_hash,
301:     }
302: 
303: 
304: def _descriptor_dict(descriptor: StoreDescriptor) -> dict[str, Any]:
305:     return {
306:         "backend": descriptor.backend,
307:         "location": descriptor.location,
308:         "lineage": descriptor.lineage,
309:         "role": None if descriptor.role is None else descriptor.role.value,
310:         "query": descriptor.query,
311:         "witness": descriptor.witness,
312:     }
313: 
314: 
315: def _descriptor_from(data: Mapping[str, Any]) -> StoreDescriptor:
316:     role = data.get("role")
317:     return StoreDescriptor(
318:         backend=str(data["backend"]),
319:         location=str(data["location"]),
320:         lineage=data.get("lineage"),
321:         role=None if role is None else Profile(str(role)),
322:         query=data.get("query"),
323:         witness=data.get("witness"),
324:     )
