131: def arrival_intent_path(target: Path | str) -> Path:
132:     """The exclusive intent sidecar for one declaration target."""
133: 
134:     target_path = Path(target).resolve()
135:     return target_path.with_name(target_path.name + ".arrival-init.intent")
136: 
137: 
138: def _json_value(value: Any) -> Any:
139:     if isinstance(value, Path):
140:         return str(value)
141:     if isinstance(value, Profile):
142:         return value.value
143:     if isinstance(value, Head):
144:         return {
145:             "lineage": value.lineage,
146:             "ordinal": value.ordinal,
147:             "record_hash": value.record_hash,
148:         }
149:     if isinstance(value, Mapping):
150:         return {str(k): _json_value(v) for k, v in value.items()}
151:     if isinstance(value, (tuple, list)):
152:         return [_json_value(v) for v in value]
153:     return value
154: 
155: 
156: def _atomic_write(path: Path, text: str) -> None:
157:     path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
158:     fd, raw = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=f".{path.name}.", dir=path.parent)
159:     tmp = Path(raw)
160:     try:
161:         with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
162:             stream.write(text)
163:             stream.flush()
164:             os.fsync(stream.fileno())
165:         tmp.replace(path)
166:         _fsync_parent(path)
167:     finally:
168:         with suppress(FileNotFoundError):
169:             tmp.unlink()
170: 
171: 
172: def _reserve(path: Path, plan: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
173:     path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
174:     encoded = json.dumps(_json_value(plan), sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))
175:     flags = os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL
176:     fd = os.open(path, flags, 0o600)
177:     try:
178:         with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
179:             stream.write(encoded + "\n")
180:             stream.flush()
181:             os.fsync(stream.fileno())
182:         _fsync_parent(path)
183:     except BaseException:
184:         with suppress(FileNotFoundError):
185:             path.unlink()
186:         raise
187: 
188: 
189: def _save_intent(path: Path, plan: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
190:     _atomic_write(path, json.dumps(_json_value(plan), sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")) + "\n")
191: 
192: 
193: def _publish_exclusive(path: Path, text: str) -> None:
194:     """Publish a complete declaration without replacing a concurrent writer."""
195:     path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
196:     fd, raw = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=f".{path.name}.", dir=path.parent)
197:     temporary = Path(raw)
198:     try:
199:         with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
200:             stream.write(text)
201:             stream.flush()
202:             os.fsync(stream.fileno())
203:         os.link(temporary, path)
204:         temporary.unlink()
205:         _fsync_parent(path)
206:     except BaseException:
207:         with suppress(FileNotFoundError):
208:             temporary.unlink()
209:         raise
210: 
211: 
212: def _fsync_parent(path: Path) -> None:
213:     directory_fd = os.open(path.parent, os.O_RDONLY)
214:     try:
215:         os.fsync(directory_fd)
216:     finally:
217:         os.close(directory_fd)
218: 
219: 
220: def _reserved_genesis_signer(
221:     plan: Mapping[str, Any],
222: ) -> Callable[[str, str], str | None]:
223:     """Return the exact signature reserved before the durable mint."""
224:     genesis = plan["genesis"]
225:     digest = content_commitment(
226:         genesis["kind"], genesis["at"], genesis["observer"],
227:         genesis["origin"], genesis["body"],
228:     )
229: 
230:     def sign(observer: str, offered_digest: str) -> str | None:
231:         if observer == genesis["observer"] and offered_digest == digest:
232:             return genesis["signature"]
233:         return None
234: 
235:     return sign
236: 
237: 
238: def _read_intent(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
239:     try:
240:         data = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
241:     except (OSError, ValueError) as exc:
242:         raise InitializationConflict(f"cannot read initialization intent {path}: {exc}") from exc
243:     if not isinstance(data, dict) or data.get("schema") != _INTENT_SCHEMA:
244:         raise InitializationConflict(f"unsupported or malformed initialization intent: {path}")
245:     if data.get("phase") not in _PHASES:
246:         raise InitializationConflict(f"initialization intent has an invalid phase: {path}")
247:     return data
248: 
249: 
250: def _descriptor_dict(descriptor: StoreDescriptor) -> dict[str, Any]:
251:     return {
252:         "backend": descriptor.backend,
253:         "location": descriptor.location,
254:         "lineage": descriptor.lineage,
255:         "role": None if descriptor.role is None else descriptor.role.value,
256:         "query": descriptor.query,
257:         "witness": descriptor.witness,
258:     }
259: 
260: 
261: def _descriptor_from(data: Mapping[str, Any]) -> StoreDescriptor:
262:     role = data.get("role")
263:     return StoreDescriptor(
264:         backend=str(data["backend"]),
265:         location=str(data["location"]),
266:         lineage=data.get("lineage"),
267:         role=None if role is None else Profile(str(role)),
268:         query=data.get("query"),
269:         witness=data.get("witness"),
270:     )
271: 
272: 
273: def _draft(
274:     *,
275:     lineage: str,
276:     authored_at: float,
277:     observer: str,
278:     documents: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
279:     fact_signer: Callable[[str, str], str | None],
280:     arrival_signer: Callable[[str, str], str | None],
281:     public_key: str,
282:     fact_verify: Callable[[str, str, str], bool] | None = None,
283:     arrival_verify: Callable[[str, str, str], bool] | None = None,
284: ) -> RecordDraft:
285:     payload = json.dumps(
286:         {"protocol": 1, "documents": [dict(document) for document in documents]},
287:         ensure_ascii=False,
288:         separators=(",", ":"),
289:     )
290:     inner_digest = fact_commitment_hash(
291:         "_decl.genesis", authored_at, observer, "", payload
292:     )
293:     inner_signature = fact_signer(observer, inner_digest)
294:     if not inner_signature:
295:         raise ArrivalInitializationError(
296:             f"founding observer {observer!r} has no fact signer"
297:         )
298:     if fact_verify is not None and not fact_verify(
299:         public_key, inner_signature, inner_digest
300:     ):
301:         raise ArrivalInitializationError("founding fact signer does not match the public key")
302:     body = body_of_fact_row(
303:         (lineage, "_decl.genesis", authored_at, observer, "", payload, inner_signature)
304:     )
305:     outer_digest = content_commitment("fact", authored_at, observer, "", body)
306:     outer_signature = arrival_signer(observer, outer_digest)
307:     if not outer_signature:
308:         raise ArrivalInitializationError(
309:             f"founding observer {observer!r} has no Arrival signer"
310:         )
311:     if arrival_verify is not None and not arrival_verify(
312:         public_key, outer_signature, outer_digest
313:     ):
314:         raise ArrivalInitializationError("founding Arrival signer does not match the public key")
315:     return RecordDraft(
316:         kind="fact",
317:         authored_at=authored_at,
318:         observer=observer,
319:         origin="",
320:         body=body,
321:         signature=outer_signature,
322:     )
323: 
324: 
325: def _same_record(record: Mapping[str, Any], draft: RecordDraft) -> bool:
326:     return (
327:         record.get("k") == draft.kind
328:         and record.get("at") == draft.authored_at
329:         and record.get("observer") == draft.observer
330:         and record.get("origin") == draft.origin
331:         and record.get("body") == dict(draft.body)
332:         and record.get("sig") == draft.signature
333:     )
334: 
335: 
336: def _declared_key_drafts(
337:     documents: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
338:     *,
339:     observer: str,
340:     public_key: str,
341:     at: float,
342:     signer: Callable[[str, str], str | None],
343:     verify: Callable[[str, str, str], bool] | None,
344: ) -> tuple[RecordDraft, ...]:
345:     """Introduce declared observer keys using the valid founding key."""
346:     keys: dict[str, str] = {}
347:     for document in documents:
348:         if document["kind"] != "_decl.observer-defined":
349:             continue
350:         key = document["payload"].get("key")
351:         if key is None:
352:             continue
353:         subject = document["subject"]
354:         try:
355:             if not isinstance(key, str) or len(base64.b64decode(key, validate=True)) != 32:
356:                 raise ValueError
357:         except (ValueError, TypeError):
358:             raise InitializationConflict(
359:                 f"observer {subject!r} has a malformed public key"
360:             ) from None
361:         if subject == observer:
362:             if key != public_key:
363:                 raise InitializationConflict(
364:                     "declared founding key differs from genesis public key"
365:                 )
366:             continue
367:         if subject in keys and keys[subject] != key:
368:             raise InitializationConflict(f"observer {subject!r} has conflicting declared keys")
369:         keys[subject] = key
370:     drafts: list[RecordDraft] = []
371:     for subject, key in keys.items():
372:         body = {"observer": subject, "key": key}
373:         digest = content_commitment("key", at, observer, "", body)
374:         signature = signer(observer, digest)
375:         if not signature or (
376:             verify is not None and not verify(public_key, signature, digest)
377:         ):
378:             raise InitializationConflict("founding signer cannot authenticate key introduction")
379:         drafts.append(RecordDraft("key", at, observer, "", body, signature))
380:     return tuple(drafts)
381: 
382: 
383: def _draft_dict(draft: RecordDraft) -> dict[str, Any]:
384:     return {
385:         "kind": draft.kind,
386:         "authored_at": draft.authored_at,
387:         "observer": draft.observer,
388:         "origin": draft.origin,
389:         "body": dict(draft.body),
390:         "signature": draft.signature,
391:     }
392: 
393: 
394: def _bootstrap_matches(ledger: Any, current: Head, drafts: Sequence[RecordDraft]) -> bool:
395:     return current.ordinal >= len(drafts) and all(
396:         _same_record(ledger.read(ordinal), draft)
397:         for ordinal, draft in enumerate(drafts, start=1)
398:     )
399: 
400: 
401: def _phase(plan: dict[str, Any], value: str) -> None:
402:     plan["phase"] = value
403: 
404: 
405: def _check_preconditions(
406:     target: Path,
407:     descriptor: StoreDescriptor,
408:     documents: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
409:     declaration_text: str,
410:     observer: str,
411:     public_key: str,
412:     signer: Callable[[str, str], str | None],
413:     fact_signer: Callable[[str, str], str | None],
414:     arrival_signer: Callable[[str, str], str | None],
415: ) -> None:
416:     if descriptor.role is not Profile.AUTHORITY:
417:         raise InitializationConflict("fresh initialization requires an Authority descriptor")
418:     if not observer or not isinstance(observer, str):
419:         raise InitializationConflict("fresh initialization requires a non-empty observer")
420:     if not isinstance(public_key, str) or not public_key:
421:         raise InitializationConflict("fresh initialization requires a founding public key")
422:     try:
423:         if len(base64.b64decode(public_key, validate=True)) != 32:
424:             raise ValueError
425:     except (ValueError, TypeError):
426:         raise InitializationConflict(
427:             "founding public key must be raw-32-byte base64"
428:         ) from None
429:     if not isinstance(declaration_text, str) or not declaration_text.strip():
430:         raise InitializationConflict("declaration text must be non-empty UTF-8 text")
431:     if not callable(signer) or not callable(fact_signer) or not callable(arrival_signer):
432:         raise InitializationConflict("fresh initialization requires signer callables")
433:     if not isinstance(documents, Sequence) or isinstance(documents, (str, bytes)):
434:         raise InitializationConflict("declaration documents must be a sequence")
435:     try:
436:         json.dumps([dict(document) for document in documents], ensure_ascii=False)
437:     except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
438:         raise InitializationConflict(f"declaration documents are not JSON-safe: {exc}") from exc
439:     if target.exists():
440:         raise InitializationConflict(f"vertex file already exists: {target}")
441: 
442: 
443: def _validate_declaration(
444:     target: Path,
445:     descriptor: StoreDescriptor,
446:     documents: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
447:     declaration_text: str,
448:     lineage: str,
449: ) -> None:
450:     """Check the signed declaration inputs before reserving or minting.
451: 
452:     The declaration text is the human ingress form and the document sequence
453:     is what gets committed into ``_decl.genesis``.  They must describe the
454:     same residence and identity, otherwise recovery could faithfully preserve
455:     a plan that never represented the requested target.
456:     """
457:     try:
458:         from lang import parse_vertex, validate_vertex, vertex_to_documents
459: 
460:         ast = parse_vertex(declaration_text, path=target)
461:         validate_vertex(ast)
462:     except Exception as exc:
463:         raise InitializationConflict(
464:             f"declaration text is not valid Vertex grammar: {exc}"
465:         ) from exc
466:     from .declaration import validate_arrival_runtime_identity
467: 
468:     validate_arrival_runtime_identity(
469:         ast.name,
470:         ast.loops,
471:         refusal=InitializationConflict,
472:     )
473:     parsed_documents = [document.as_json() for document in vertex_to_documents(ast)]
474:     if [dict(document) for document in documents] != parsed_documents:
475:         raise InitializationConflict(
476:             "declaration text and declaration documents disagree"
477:         )
478:     backend = ast.store_backend
479:     if (
480:         backend is None
481:         or backend.name != descriptor.backend
482:         or backend.lineage != lineage
483:         or backend.role != Profile.AUTHORITY.value
484:     ):
485:         raise InitializationConflict(
486:             "declaration residence must match the requested backend, lineage and authority role"
487:         )
488:     location = ast.store_location
489:     if descriptor.backend == "file" and ast.store is not None:
490:         location = str(ast.store)
491:     if location is None:
492:         raise InitializationConflict("declaration residence must include a store location")
493:     if descriptor.backend == "file":
494:         parsed_location = (target.parent / location).resolve()
495:         requested_location = Path(descriptor.location).resolve()
496:         locations_match = parsed_location == requested_location
497:     else:
498:         # DSNs are adapter-owned and must survive the engine untouched.
499:         locations_match = location == descriptor.location
500:     if not locations_match:
501:         raise InitializationConflict(
502:             "declaration residence location does not match the requested descriptor"
503:         )
504: 
505: 
506: def initialize_arrival(
507:     registry: BackendRegistry,
508:     descriptor: StoreDescriptor,
509:     *,
510:     target: Path | str,
511:     documents: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
512:     declaration_text: str,
513:     observer: str,
514:     public_key: str,
515:     signer: Callable[[str, str], str | None],
516:     fact_signer: Callable[[str, str], str | None],
517:     arrival_signer: Callable[[str, str], str | None],
518:     fact_verify: Callable[[str, str, str], bool] | None = None,
519:     arrival_verify: Callable[[str, str, str], bool] | None = None,
520:     lineage: str | None = None,
521:     authored_at: float | None = None,
522:     failure_hook: Callable[[str], None] | None = None,
523: ) -> ArrivalInitializationResult:
524:     """Mint and publish a fresh Arrival declaration with recoverable intent."""
525:     target_path = Path(target).resolve()
526:     intent = arrival_intent_path(target_path)
527:     _check_preconditions(
528:         target_path, descriptor, documents, declaration_text, observer, public_key,
529:         signer, fact_signer, arrival_signer,
530:     )
531:     if intent.exists():
532:         raise InitializationRecoveryRequired(
533:             f"unfinished initialization intent exists: {intent}; recover explicitly"
534:         )
535:     chosen_lineage = lineage or descriptor.lineage or new_lineage()
536:     if descriptor.lineage is not None and descriptor.lineage != chosen_lineage:
537:         raise InitializationConflict("descriptor lineage does not match reserved lineage")
538:     _validate_declaration(
539:         target_path, descriptor, documents, declaration_text, chosen_lineage
540:     )
541:     at = time.time() if authored_at is None else authored_at
542:     genesis_body = {
543:         "protocol": 1,
544:         "lineage": chosen_lineage,
545:         "key": public_key,
546:     }
547:     genesis_digest = content_commitment(
548:         "genesis", at, observer, "", genesis_body
549:     )
550:     genesis_signature = signer(observer, genesis_digest)
551:     if not genesis_signature:
552:         raise InitializationConflict("founding signer produced no genesis signature")
553:     if arrival_verify is not None and not arrival_verify(
554:         public_key, genesis_signature, genesis_digest
555:     ):
556:         raise InitializationConflict("founding genesis signer does not match the public key")
557:     draft = _draft(
558:         lineage=chosen_lineage,
559:         authored_at=at,
560:         observer=observer,
561:         documents=documents,
562:         fact_signer=fact_signer,
563:         arrival_signer=arrival_signer,
564:         public_key=public_key,
565:         fact_verify=fact_verify,
566:         arrival_verify=arrival_verify,
567:     )
568:     key_drafts = _declared_key_drafts(
569:         documents, observer=observer, public_key=public_key, at=at,
570:         signer=arrival_signer, verify=arrival_verify,
571:     )
572:     if key_drafts:
573:         # The minimum backend contract supports one record. Additional keys
574:         # and declaration genesis form one larger atomic unit; refuse its
575:         # unsupported size before reserving an intent or minting a lineage.
576:         ledger, query = registry.open(descriptor)
577:         try:
578:             limit = ledger.capabilities().max_atomic_records
579:             if limit is not None and len(key_drafts) + 1 > limit:
580:                 raise AtomicLimitExceeded(
581:                     f"initialization needs {len(key_drafts) + 1} records; atomic limit is {limit}"
582:                 )
583:         finally:
584:             for resource in (query, ledger):
585:                 close = getattr(resource, "close", None)
586:                 if callable(close):
587:                     close()
588:     planned = {
589:         "schema": _INTENT_SCHEMA,
590:         "phase": "reserved",
591:         "target": str(target_path),
592:         "descriptor": _descriptor_dict(
593:             StoreDescriptor(
594:                 descriptor.backend, descriptor.location, chosen_lineage,
595:                 descriptor.role, descriptor.query, descriptor.witness,
596:             )
597:         ),
598:         "lineage": chosen_lineage,
599:         "observer": observer,
600:         "public_key": public_key,
601:         "at": at,
602:         "genesis": {
603:             "kind": "genesis",
604:             "at": at,
605:             "observer": observer,
606:             "origin": "",
607:             "body": genesis_body,
608:             "signature": genesis_signature,
609:         },
610:         "documents": [dict(document) for document in documents],
611:         "declaration_text": declaration_text,
612:         "draft": _draft_dict(draft),
613:         "key_drafts": [_draft_dict(key_draft) for key_draft in key_drafts],
614:     }
615:     _reserve(intent, planned)
616:     try:
617:         return _continue(
618:             registry, intent, planned, failure_hook=failure_hook,
619:         )
620:     except BaseException:
621:         # The sidecar is the recovery record.  It is removed only once the
622:         # declaration bytes have been atomically published.
623:         raise
624: 
625: 
626: def _continue(
627:     registry: BackendRegistry,
628:     intent: Path,
629:     plan: dict[str, Any],
630:     *,
631:     failure_hook: Callable[[str], None] | None,
632: ) -> ArrivalInitializationResult:
633:     descriptor = _descriptor_from(plan["descriptor"])
634:     target = Path(plan["target"])
635:     draft_data = plan["draft"]
636:     draft = RecordDraft(
637:         kind=draft_data["kind"], authored_at=draft_data["authored_at"],
638:         observer=draft_data["observer"], origin=draft_data["origin"],
639:         body=draft_data["body"], signature=draft_data["signature"],
640:     )
641:     drafts = tuple(RecordDraft(**data) for data in plan.get("key_drafts", ())) + (draft,)
642:     ledger = query = None
643:     head: Head | None = None
644:     commit = None
645:     try:
646:         if plan["phase"] == "reserved":
647:             ledger, query = registry.open(descriptor)
648:             # PreGenesis carries no absence claim.  The adapter's exclusive
649:             # mint is the authority for an absent, existing, or corrupt
650:             # location and refuses safely in the latter two cases.
651:             try:
652:                 head = ledger.mint({
653:                     "observer": plan["observer"],
654:                     "signer": _reserved_genesis_signer(plan),
655:                     "key": plan["public_key"], "lineage": plan["lineage"],
656:                     "origin": "", "at": plan["at"],
657:                 })
658:             except Exception as mint_error:
659:                 from .arrival import GenesisRefused
660:                 from .arrival_contract import ContractRefusal
661:                 from .arrival_head_seam import NotWitnessed
662: 
663:                 if isinstance(mint_error, NotWitnessed):
664:                     raise InitializationUnwitnessed(
665:                         "genesis committed but its witness is unknown; recover "
666:                         "the initialization intent before retrying",
667:                         phase="reserved", lineage=plan["lineage"],
668:                         fact_id=plan["lineage"], head=mint_error.head,
669:                         commit=mint_error.commit, intent_path=intent,
670:                         cause=mint_error,
671:                     ) from mint_error
672:                 # A prior mint may have committed while its witness was
673:                 # unknown.  The failed exclusive mint is the proof that this
674:                 # is not a fresh location; only an exact custody comparison
675:                 # can turn that case into recoverable progress.
676:                 try:
677:                     head = _head_and_check_genesis(registry, descriptor, plan)
678:                 except Exception:
679:                     if isinstance(mint_error, (ContractRefusal, GenesisRefused)):
680:                         raise mint_error from None
681:                     raise InitializationOutcomeUnknown(
682:                         "genesis mint outcome is unknown; recover the durable "
683:                         "initialization intent before retrying",
684:                         phase="reserved", lineage=plan["lineage"],
685:                         fact_id=plan["lineage"], captured_head=None,
686:                         intent_path=intent, cause=mint_error,
687:                     ) from mint_error
688:                 if head.ordinal != 0:
689:                     raise mint_error from None
690:             _phase(plan, "minted")
691:             _save_intent(intent, plan)
692:             if failure_hook:
693:                 failure_hook("after-mint")
694:         else:
695:             head = _head_and_check_genesis(registry, descriptor, plan)
696:         if plan["phase"] == "minted":
697:             if ledger is None:
698:                 ledger, query = registry.open(descriptor)
699:             current = ledger.head()
700:             if current.ordinal == 0:
701:                 try:
702:                     commit = ledger.append(current, drafts)
703:                 except Exception as exc:
704:                     # Contract/grammar refusals are precommit diagnostics;
705:                     # an adapter exception outside those public families did
706:                     # not establish whether its append landed.
707:                     from .arrival_contract import ContractRefusal
708: 
709:                     if isinstance(exc, ContractRefusal):
710:                         raise
711:                     from .arrival import GenesisRefused
712: 
713:                     if isinstance(exc, GenesisRefused):
714:                         raise
715:                     from .arrival_head_seam import NotWitnessed
716: 
717:                     if isinstance(exc, NotWitnessed):
718:                         raise InitializationUnwitnessed(
719:                             "declaration committed but its witness is unknown; "
720:                             "recover the initialization intent before retrying",
721:                             phase="minted", lineage=plan["lineage"],
722:                             fact_id=str(draft.body["id"]), head=exc.head,
723:                             commit=exc.commit, intent_path=intent, cause=exc,
724:                         ) from exc
725:                     raise InitializationOutcomeUnknown(
726:                         "declaration append outcome is unknown; recover the "
727:                         "initialization intent before retrying",
728:                         phase="minted", lineage=plan["lineage"],
729:                         fact_id=str(draft.body["id"]), captured_head=current,
730:                         intent_path=intent, cause=exc,
731:                     ) from exc
732:                 head = commit.after
733:             elif _bootstrap_matches(ledger, current, drafts):
734:                 head = current
735:             else:
736:                 raise InitializationConflict("ledger contains a conflicting post-genesis record")
737:             _phase(plan, "declared")
738:             _save_intent(intent, plan)
739:             if failure_hook:
740:                 failure_hook("after-declaration")
741:         if plan["phase"] == "declared":
742:             if ledger is None:
743:                 ledger, query = registry.open(descriptor)
744:             current = ledger.head()
745:             # The declaration append is already durable at this point.  Keep
746:             # its observed head while projection sync runs so a sync failure
747:             # is reported as committed-incomplete with concrete identity.
748:             head = current
749:             if not _bootstrap_matches(ledger, current, drafts):
750:                 raise InitializationConflict(
751:                     "ledger no longer contains the planned declaration genesis"
752:                 )
753:             result = sync_projection(registry, descriptor)
754:             head = result.captured_head
755:             _phase(plan, "synced")
756:             _save_intent(intent, plan)
757:             if failure_hook:
758:                 failure_hook("after-sync")
759:         elif plan["phase"] in {"synced", "published"}:
760:             if ledger is None:
761:                 ledger, query = registry.open(descriptor)
762:             current = ledger.head()
763:             # Recovery has an observed committed head before attempting
764:             # publication or intent cleanup.
765:             head = current
766:             if not _bootstrap_matches(ledger, current, drafts):
767:                 raise InitializationConflict(
768:                     "ledger no longer contains the planned declaration genesis"
769:                 )
770:         else:
771:             head = ledger.head() if ledger is not None else _head_and_check_genesis(
772:                 registry, descriptor, plan
773:             )
774:         if plan["phase"] == "synced":
775:             if target.exists():
776:                 existing = target.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
777:                 if existing != plan["declaration_text"]:
778:                     raise InitializationConflict(
779:                         "existing declaration differs from initialization intent"
780:                     )
781:             else:
782:                 try:
783:                     _publish_exclusive(target, plan["declaration_text"])
784:                 except FileExistsError:
785:                     existing = target.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
786:                     if existing != plan["declaration_text"]:
787:                         raise InitializationConflict(
788:                             "a concurrent declaration differs from initialization intent"
789:                         ) from None
790:             _phase(plan, "published")
791:             _save_intent(intent, plan)
792:             if failure_hook:
793:                 failure_hook("after-publication")
794:         if plan["phase"] == "published":
795:             with suppress(FileNotFoundError):
796:                 intent.unlink()
797:                 _fsync_parent(intent)
798:         return ArrivalInitializationResult(
799:             target_path=str(target), location=descriptor.location,
800:             lineage=plan["lineage"], head=head,
801:             phase=plan["phase"], file_written=target.exists(),
802:         )
803:     except Exception as exc:
804:         if isinstance(
805:             exc,
806:             (
807:                 InitializationOutcomeUnknown,
808:                 InitializationUnwitnessed,
809:                 InitializationCommittedIncomplete,
810:             ),
811:         ):
812:             raise
813:         if head is not None and plan["phase"] in {"minted", "declared", "synced", "published"}:
814:             raise InitializationCommittedIncomplete(
815:                 "initialization committed but a later phase failed; recover "
816:                 "the durable intent",
817:                 phase=plan["phase"], lineage=plan["lineage"],
818:                 fact_id=str(draft.body["id"]), captured_head=head,
819:                 commit=commit, intent_path=intent, cause=exc,
820:             ) from exc
821:         raise
822:     finally:
823:         close = getattr(query, "close", None)
824:         if callable(close):
825:             close()
826:         close = getattr(ledger, "close", None)
827:         if callable(close):
828:             close()
829: 
830: 
831: def _head_and_check_genesis(
832:     registry: BackendRegistry,
833:     descriptor: StoreDescriptor,
834:     plan: Mapping[str, Any],
835: ) -> Head:
836:     ledger, query = registry.open(descriptor)
837:     try:
838:         head = ledger.head()
839:         record = ledger.read(0)
840:         body = record.get("body")
841:         genesis = plan["genesis"]
842:         if (
843:             record.get("k") != genesis["kind"]
844:             or record.get("at") != genesis["at"]
845:             or record.get("observer") != genesis["observer"]
846:             or record.get("origin") != genesis["origin"]
847:             or body != genesis["body"]
848:             or record.get("sig") != genesis["signature"]
849:         ):
850:             raise InitializationConflict("existing genesis differs from initialization intent")
851:         if head.lineage != plan["lineage"]:
852:             raise InitializationConflict(
853:                 "existing ledger lineage differs from initialization intent"
854:             )
855:         return head
856:     finally:
857:         getattr(query, "close", lambda: None)()
858:         getattr(ledger, "close", lambda: None)()
859: 
860: 
861: def recover_arrival_initialization(
862:     registry: BackendRegistry,
863:     intent: Path | str,
864:     *,
865:     # Retained as ignored compatibility parameters for callers of the first
866:     # recovery prototype. Recovery signs nothing: it uses the exact reserved
867:     # genesis signature from the durable intent.
868:     signer: Callable[[str, str], str | None] | None = None,
869:     fact_signer: Callable[[str, str], str | None] | None = None,
870:     arrival_signer: Callable[[str, str], str | None] | None = None,
871:     failure_hook: Callable[[str], None] | None = None,
872: ) -> ArrivalInitializationResult:
873:     """Resume an intent by comparing exact custody content at every phase."""
874:     intent_path = Path(intent).resolve()
875:     if not intent_path.exists():
876:         raise InitializationConflict(f"initialization intent does not exist: {intent_path}")
877:     plan = _read_intent(intent_path)
878:     return _continue(
879:         registry, intent_path, plan, failure_hook=failure_hook,
880:     )

===== libs/engine/src/engine/arrival_sources.py =====
1: """Captured, tier-atomic source execution for an Arrival Authority.
2: 
3: Collection has no custody handle.  A CURRENT runtime capture is taken before
4: each tier's collectors run, and the resulting observations are planned against
5: that same head.  A completed tier uses one batch append and explicit projection
6: catch-up; failures retain collection and any durable commits without retrying a
7: collector.
8: """
9: 
10: from __future__ import annotations
11: 
12: import asyncio
13: import json
14: import time
15: from collections.abc import AsyncIterable, Callable, Mapping, Sequence
16: from dataclasses import dataclass, replace
17: from pathlib import Path
18: from types import MappingProxyType
19: from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any, Literal
20: 
21: from atoms import Fact, SourceError
22: from ulid import ULID
23: 
24: from .arrival_head_seam import NotWitnessed
25: from .arrival_maintenance import sync_projection
26: from .runtime_write import (
27:     BatchFactInput,
28:     BatchPostCommitProjectionFailed,
29:     BatchWriteCommitUnknown,
30:     BatchWritePlan,
31:     CommittedBatchWrite,
32:     ProjectionOutcome,
33:     RuntimeCapture,
34:     capture_runtime,
35:     execute_batch_write,
36:     plan_batch_from_capture,
37: )
38: 
39: if TYPE_CHECKING:  # pragma: no cover - annotations only
40:     from .arrival_contract import Commit, ReadBasis, StoreDescriptor
41:     from .arrival_registry import BackendRegistry
42:     from .credentials import WriteCredentials

68: 
69: class SourcePreparationRefused(Exception):
70:     """Known source/lifecycle admission refused before any collector ran."""
71: 
72:     def __init__(self, message: str, *, capture: RuntimeCapture, cause: Exception) -> None:
73:         super().__init__(message)
74:         self.capture = capture
75:         self.cause = cause
76: 
77: 
78: class InvalidSourceOutput(Exception):
79:     """A collector yielded a value that is not an observation Fact."""
80: 
81:     def __init__(self, source_index: int, value: object) -> None:
82:         super().__init__(
83:             f"source {source_index} yielded {type(value).__name__}, expected atoms.Fact"
84:         )
85:         self.source_index = source_index
86:         self.value_type = type(value).__name__
87:         self.value_repr = repr(value)
88: 
89: 
90: class SourceTierPreparationRefused(Exception):
91:     """A collected tier could not become an appendable all-or-nothing plan."""
92: 
93:     def __init__(self, collected: CollectedTier, cause: Exception) -> None:
94:         super().__init__(f"source tier {collected.tier_index} was refused: {cause}")
95:         self.collected = collected
96:         self.cause = cause
97: 
98: 
99: class DeclarationChangedDuringInvocation(Exception):
100:     """A later CURRENT capture no longer has the invocation's declaration."""
101: 
102:     def __init__(self, initial: RuntimeCapture, observed: RuntimeCapture) -> None:
103:         super().__init__(
104:             "effective declaration changed after a prior source tier committed; "
105:             "remaining collectors were not run"
106:         )
107:         self.initial_basis = initial.basis
108:         self.observed_basis = observed.basis
109:         self.initial_documents = initial.declaration_documents
110:         self.observed_documents = observed.declaration_documents
111: 
112: 
113: class DispatchFailed(Exception):
114:     """A run-clause dispatch failed after its tier commit became durable."""
115: 
116:     def __init__(
117:         self,
118:         commit: Commit,
119:         intents: tuple[DispatchIntent, ...],
120:         cause: Exception,
121:     ) -> None:
122:         super().__init__(
123:             "source tier committed but postcommit run-clause dispatch failed; "
124:             "the invocation is incomplete and is not retried"
125:         )
126:         self.commit = commit
127:         self.intents = intents
128:         self.tick_ids = tuple(intent.tick_id for intent in intents)
129:         self.tick_names = tuple(intent.tick_name for intent in intents)
130:         self.commands = tuple(intent.command for intent in intents)
131:         self.cause = cause

134: @dataclass(frozen=True)
135: class CadenceEvidence:
136:     """Why one captured source did or did not qualify at the initial H."""
137: 
138:     source_index: int
139:     kind: str
140:     mode: str
141:     evaluated_at: float
142:     latest_success_id: str | None
143:     latest_success_ts: float | None
144:     trigger_fact_ids: tuple[str, ...]
145:     interval_seconds: float | None
146:     forced: bool
147:     qualified: bool
148:

150: @dataclass(frozen=True)
151: class SourceInvocationPlan:
152:     """Fixed qualification and source order built before collection."""
153: 
154:     invocation_id: str
155:     observer: str
156:     initial_capture: RuntimeCapture
157:     cadence: tuple[CadenceEvidence, ...]
158:     qualifying_indices: tuple[int, ...]
159:     tiers: tuple[tuple[int, ...], ...]
160:     lifecycle_ids: tuple[str, ...]
161:     overall_sync_id: str
162: 
163:     @property
164:     def skipped(self) -> tuple[CadenceEvidence, ...]:
165:         return tuple(evidence for evidence in self.cadence if not evidence.qualified)
166: 
167:     @property
168:     def source_kinds(self) -> tuple[str, ...]:
169:         sources = self.initial_capture.sources
170:         return tuple(source.kind for source, _cadence in sources)

206:

209:         return tuple(fact for fact, _fact_id in self.pairs)
210:

213:         return tuple(fact_id for _fact, fact_id in self.pairs)

225: 
226: class SourceTierCollectionFailed(Exception):
227:     """Coordinator work failed after retaining an exactly paired partial tier."""
228: 
229:     def __init__(
230:         self,
231:         *,
232:         tier_index: int,
233:         basis: ReadBasis,
234:         completed_sources: tuple[CollectedSource, ...],
235:         failed_sources: tuple[PartialCollectedSource, ...],
236:         cancelled_sources: tuple[PartialCollectedSource, ...],
237:         cause: Exception,
238:     ) -> None:
239:         super().__init__(
240:             f"source tier {tier_index} collection coordinator failed: {cause}"
241:         )
242:         self.tier_index = tier_index
243:         self.basis = basis
244:         self.completed_sources = completed_sources
245:         self.failed_sources = failed_sources
246:         self.cancelled_sources = cancelled_sources
247:         self.cause = cause
248: 
249: 
250: @dataclass(frozen=True)
251: class _CollectionState:
252:     """Fixed source identity plus one coroutine-owned paired accumulator."""
253: 
254:     source_index: int
255:     kind: str
256:     command: str
257:     source_observer: str
258:     pairs: list[tuple[Fact, str]]
259: 
260:     def partial(
261:         self,
262:         phase: Literal["bookkeeping", "cleanup", "cancelled"],
263:         error: Exception | None = None,
264:     ) -> PartialCollectedSource:
265:         return PartialCollectedSource(
266:             source_index=self.source_index,
267:             kind=self.kind,
268:             command=self.command,
269:             source_observer=self.source_observer,
270:             pairs=tuple(self.pairs),
271:             phase=phase,
272:             error_type=None if error is None else type(error).__name__,
273:             error_message=None if error is None else str(error),
274:         )
275: 
276: 
277: class _CollectionStepFailed(Exception):
278:     def __init__(
279:         self,
280:         state: _CollectionState,
281:         phase: Literal["bookkeeping", "cleanup"],
282:         cause: Exception,
283:     ) -> None:
284:         super().__init__(str(cause))
285:         self.state = state
286:         self.phase = phase
287:         self.cause = cause

310:     """One actual custody commit and the collection it made durable."""

323:         assert self.outcome.commit is not None

327: @dataclass(frozen=True)
328: class SourceInvocationResult:
329:     """Complete or interrupted source execution with durable evidence separated."""
330: 
331:     invocation_id: str
332:     status: Literal["ok", "error", "incomplete"]
333:     plan: SourceInvocationPlan
334:     bases: tuple[ReadBasis, ...]
335:     collected_tiers: tuple[CollectedTier, ...]
336:     durable_tiers: tuple[TierCommitted, ...]
337:     terminal_tier: CollectedTier | None
338:     terminal_plan: BatchWritePlan | None
339:     collected_uncommitted: CollectedTier | None
340:     collected_unknown: CollectedTier | None
341:     error_lifecycle_ids: tuple[str, ...]
342:     durable_error_lifecycle_ids: tuple[str, ...]
343:     overall_sync_id: str
344:     terminal: BaseException | None = None
345: 
346: 
347: def _new_id() -> str:
348:     return str(ULID())
349: 
350: 
351: def _latest_success(facts: Sequence[Any], kind: str) -> Any | None:
352:     matching = [
353:         fact
354:         for fact in facts
355:         if fact.kind == f"_sync.{kind}" and fact.payload.get("status") == "ok"
356:     ]
357:     return max(matching, key=lambda fact: (fact.ts, fact.id), default=None)
358: 
359: 
360: def _cadence_evidence(
361:     source_index: int,
362:     source: Any,
363:     cadence: Any,
364:     facts: Sequence[Any],
365:     *,
366:     evaluated_at: float,
367:     force: bool,
368: ) -> CadenceEvidence:
369:     last = _latest_success(facts, source.kind)
370:     mode = cadence.mode
371:     trigger_ids: tuple[str, ...] = ()
372:     interval = getattr(cadence, "_interval", None)
373:     if mode == "always":
374:         qualified = True
375:     elif mode == "elapsed":
376:         qualified = last is None or evaluated_at - last.ts >= interval
377:     elif mode == "triggered":
378:         threshold = float("-inf") if last is None else last.ts
379:         trigger_ids = tuple(
380:             fact.id
381:             for fact in facts
382:             if fact.kind in cadence.trigger_kinds and fact.ts > threshold
383:         )
384:         qualified = bool(trigger_ids)
385:     else:
386:         qualified = False
387:     if force:
388:         qualified = True
389:     return CadenceEvidence(
390:         source_index=source_index,
391:         kind=source.kind,
392:         mode=mode,
393:         evaluated_at=evaluated_at,
394:         latest_success_id=None if last is None else last.id,
395:         latest_success_ts=None if last is None else last.ts,
396:         trigger_fact_ids=trigger_ids,
397:         interval_seconds=interval,
398:         forced=force,
399:         qualified=qualified,
400:     )
401: 
402: 
403: def _source_observers(source: Any) -> tuple[str, ...]:
404:     nested = getattr(source, "sources", None)
405:     if nested is not None:
406:         return tuple(observer for item in nested for observer in _source_observers(item))
407:     observer = getattr(source, "observer", "")
408:     return (observer,) if observer else ()
409: 
410: 
411: def _preflight_known_admission(
412:     capture: RuntimeCapture,
413:     sources: Sequence[tuple[Any, Any]],
414:     qualifying: Sequence[int],
415:     *,
416:     observer: str,
417:     credentials: WriteCredentials,
418:     lifecycle_ids: Sequence[str],
419:     overall_sync_id: str,
420: ) -> None:
421:     from .admission import grant_for_observer
422: 
423:     effective = capture.effective_declaration
424:     try:
425:         grant_for_observer(effective, observer)
426:         for index in qualifying:
427:             for source_observer in _source_observers(sources[index][0]):
428:                 grant_for_observer(effective, source_observer)
429:         known = [
430:             BatchFactInput(
431:                 Fact.of(
432:                     f"_sync.{sources[index][0].kind}",
433:                     observer,
434:                     ts=capture.evaluated_at,
435:                     command=sources[index][0].command,
436:                     status="ok",
437:                 ),
438:                 fact_id=lifecycle_ids[index],
439:                 admit_undeclared=True,
440:             )
441:             for index in qualifying
442:         ]
443:         known.append(
444:             BatchFactInput(
445:                 Fact.of(
446:                     "_sync",
447:                     observer,
448:                     ts=capture.evaluated_at,
449:                     status="ok",
450:                     sources_run=len(qualifying),
451:                 ),
452:                 fact_id=overall_sync_id,
453:                 admit_undeclared=True,
454:             )
455:         )
456:         # One at a time checks exact lifecycle grant/strict/tick-signing rules
457:         # without imposing a fictitious whole-invocation atomic limit.
458:         for item in known:
459:             plan_batch_from_capture(capture, (item,), credentials=credentials)
460:         if capture.pending_boundaries:
461:             # Pending ticks are already known at H and can require a tick signer
462:             # in a signed era. Refuse before any external collector runs.
463:             plan_batch_from_capture(
464:                 capture,
465:                 (),
466:                 credentials=credentials,
467:                 include_pending_boundaries=True,
468:             )
469:     except Exception as exc:
470:         raise SourcePreparationRefused(
471:             "source lifecycle admission refused before collection",
472:             capture=capture,
473:             cause=exc,
474:         ) from exc
475: 
476: 
477: def prepare_source_invocation(
478:     registry: BackendRegistry,
479:     descriptor: StoreDescriptor,
480:     locator: Any,
481:     *,
482:     observer: str,
483:     credentials: WriteCredentials,
484:     force: bool = False,
485:     evaluated_at: float | None = None,
486:     id_factory: Callable[[], str] = _new_id,
487: ) -> SourceInvocationPlan:
488:     """Capture H, cadence, source order and tiers before any collector runs."""
489:     from .executor import _build_dependency_graph, _toposort_tiers
490: 
491:     if not observer:
492:         raise ValueError("source lifecycle observer must be explicit and nonempty")
493:     capture = capture_runtime(
494:         registry,
495:         descriptor,
496:         locator,
497:         source_mode=True,
498:         evaluated_at=evaluated_at,
499:         credentials=credentials,
500:     )
501:     sources = capture.sources
502:     evidence = tuple(
503:         _cadence_evidence(
504:             index,
505:             source,
506:             cadence,
507:             capture.facts,
508:             evaluated_at=capture.evaluated_at,
509:             force=force,
510:         )
511:         for index, (source, cadence) in enumerate(sources)
512:     )
513:     qualifying = tuple(item.source_index for item in evidence if item.qualified)
514:     deps = _build_dependency_graph(list(sources))
515:     tiers = tuple(tuple(tier) for tier in _toposort_tiers(set(qualifying), deps))
516:     invocation_id = id_factory()
517:     lifecycle_ids = tuple(id_factory() for _ in sources)
518:     overall_sync_id = id_factory()
519:     _preflight_known_admission(
520:         capture,
521:         sources,
522:         qualifying,
523:         observer=observer,
524:         credentials=credentials,
525:         lifecycle_ids=lifecycle_ids,
526:         overall_sync_id=overall_sync_id,
527:     )
528:     return SourceInvocationPlan(
529:         invocation_id=invocation_id,
530:         observer=observer,
531:         initial_capture=capture,
532:         cadence=evidence,
533:         qualifying_indices=qualifying,
534:         tiers=tiers,
535:         lifecycle_ids=lifecycle_ids,
536:         overall_sync_id=overall_sync_id,
537:     )
538: 
539: 
540: async def _close_owned_streams(
541:     stream: object | None,
542:     iterator: object | None,
543:     *,
544:     primary: BaseException | None,
545: ) -> Exception | None:
546:     """Attempt async close on the iterator and owner before propagating interruption.
547: 
548:     This releases Python iterator resources only. It does not claim that an
549:     underlying collector reversed or terminated any external effect.
550:     """
551:     failures: list[Exception] = []
552:     handles: list[object] = []
553:     deferred_interrupt: BaseException | None = None
554:     primary_is_interrupt = primary is not None and not isinstance(primary, Exception)
555:     for handle in (iterator, stream):
556:         if handle is None or any(handle is existing for existing in handles):
557:             continue
558:         handles.append(handle)
559:         try:
560:             close = getattr(handle, "aclose", None)
561:             if not callable(close):
562:                 continue
563:             await close()
564:         except Exception as exc:
565:             failures.append(exc)
566:         except BaseException as exc:
567:             if not primary_is_interrupt and deferred_interrupt is None:
568:                 deferred_interrupt = exc
569:     if deferred_interrupt is not None:
570:         raise deferred_interrupt
571:     return failures[0] if failures else None
572: 
573: 
574: async def _collect_one(
575:     invocation: SourceInvocationPlan,
576:     state: _CollectionState,
577:     source: Any,
578:     collector_factory: CollectorFactory | None,
579:     *,
580:     clock: Callable[[], float],
581:     id_factory: Callable[[], str],
582: ) -> CollectedSource:
583:     status: Literal["ok", "error", "invalid"] = "ok"
584:     stderr = ""
585:     returncode: int | None = None
586:     error_type: str | None = None
587:     error_message: str | None = None
588:     invalid: InvalidSourceOutput | None = None
589:     stream: object | None = None
590:     iterator: object | None = None
591:     primary: BaseException | None = None
592:     try:
593:         stream = source.collect() if collector_factory is None else collector_factory(source)
594:         iterator = aiter(stream)
595:         async for value in iterator:
596:             if not isinstance(value, Fact):
597:                 invalid = InvalidSourceOutput(state.source_index, value)
598:                 primary = invalid
599:                 status = "invalid"
600:                 break
601:             try:
602:                 fact_id = id_factory()
603:                 state.pairs.append((value, fact_id))
604:             except Exception as exc:
605:                 failure = _CollectionStepFailed(state, "bookkeeping", exc)
606:                 primary = failure
607:                 raise failure from exc
608:     except _CollectionStepFailed:
609:         raise
610:     except asyncio.CancelledError as exc:
611:         primary = exc
612:         raise
613:     except SourceError as exc:
614:         primary = exc
615:         status = "error"
616:         stderr = exc.stderr
617:         returncode = exc.returncode
618:         error_type = type(exc).__name__
619:         error_message = str(exc)
620:     except Exception as exc:
621:         primary = exc
622:         status = "error"
623:         error_type = type(exc).__name__
624:         error_message = str(exc)
625:     except BaseException as exc:
626:         primary = exc
627:         raise
628:     finally:
629:         cleanup_error = await _close_owned_streams(
630:             stream, iterator, primary=primary
631:         )
632:         if cleanup_error is not None and primary is None:
633:             raise _CollectionStepFailed(state, "cleanup", cleanup_error) from cleanup_error
634: 
635:     try:
636:         payload: dict[str, Any] = {
637:             "command": state.command,
638:             "status": "error" if status != "ok" else "ok",
639:             "count": len(state.pairs),
640:             "invocation_id": invocation.invocation_id,
641:             "source_index": state.source_index,
642:         }
643:         if stderr:
644:             payload["stderr"] = stderr
645:         if returncode is not None:
646:             payload["returncode"] = returncode
647:         if error_type is not None:
648:             payload["error_type"] = error_type
649:         if error_message is not None:
650:             payload["error"] = error_message
651:         if invalid is not None:
652:             payload["error_type"] = type(invalid).__name__
653:             payload["error"] = str(invalid)
654:         lifecycle = Fact(
655:             f"_sync.{state.kind}",
656:             clock(),
657:             payload,
658:             observer=invocation.observer,
659:         )
660:         return CollectedSource(
661:             source_index=state.source_index,
662:             kind=state.kind,
663:             command=state.command,
664:             source_observer=state.source_observer,
665:             facts=tuple(fact for fact, _fact_id in state.pairs),
666:             fact_ids=tuple(fact_id for _fact, fact_id in state.pairs),
667:             lifecycle_fact=lifecycle,
668:             lifecycle_id=invocation.lifecycle_ids[state.source_index],
669:             status=status,
670:             stderr=stderr,
671:             returncode=returncode,
672:             error_type=error_type,
673:             error_message=error_message,
674:             invalid_output=invalid,
675:         )
676:     except Exception as exc:
677:         raise _CollectionStepFailed(state, "bookkeeping", exc) from exc
678: 
679: 
680: async def collect_source_tier(
681:     invocation: SourceInvocationPlan,
682:     capture: RuntimeCapture,
683:     tier_index: int,
684:     collector_factory: CollectorFactory | None = None,
685:     *,
686:     clock: Callable[[], float] = time.time,
687:     id_factory: Callable[[], str] = _new_id,
688: ) -> CollectedTier:
689:     """Collect one fixed tier concurrently while retaining source-list order."""
690:     indices = invocation.tiers[tier_index]
691:     sources = invocation.initial_capture.sources
692:     states = [
693:         _CollectionState(
694:             source_index=index,
695:             kind=sources[index][0].kind,
696:             command=sources[index][0].command,
697:             source_observer=sources[index][0].observer,
698:             pairs=[],
699:         )
700:         for index in indices
701:     ]
702:     tasks = [
703:         asyncio.create_task(
704:             _collect_one(
705:                 invocation,
706:                 state,
707:                 sources[state.source_index][0],
708:                 collector_factory,
709:                 clock=clock,
710:                 id_factory=id_factory,
711:             )
712:         )
713:         for state in states
714:     ]
715:     try:
716:         collected = tuple(await asyncio.gather(*tasks))
717:     except _CollectionStepFailed as primary:
718:         for task in tasks:
719:             if not task.done():
720:                 task.cancel()
721:         settled = await asyncio.gather(*tasks, return_exceptions=True)
722:         for result in settled:
723:             if isinstance(result, BaseException) and not isinstance(
724:                 result, (_CollectionStepFailed, asyncio.CancelledError)
725:             ):
726:                 # This sibling already owns its chaining evidence. Adding or
727:                 # clearing a cause here would rewrite the control exception.
728:                 raise result  # noqa: B904
729:         completed = tuple(
730:             result for result in settled if isinstance(result, CollectedSource)
731:         )
732:         failed = tuple(
733:             result.state.partial(result.phase, result.cause)
734:             for result in settled
735:             if isinstance(result, _CollectionStepFailed)
736:         )
737:         cancelled = tuple(
738:             state.partial("cancelled")
739:             for state, result in zip(states, settled, strict=True)
740:             if isinstance(result, asyncio.CancelledError)
741:         )
742:         raise SourceTierCollectionFailed(
743:             tier_index=tier_index,
744:             basis=capture.basis,
745:             completed_sources=completed,
746:             failed_sources=failed,
747:             cancelled_sources=cancelled,
748:             cause=primary.cause,
749:         ) from primary.cause
750:     except BaseException:
751:         for task in tasks:
752:             if not task.done():
753:                 task.cancel()
754:         await asyncio.gather(*tasks, return_exceptions=True)
755:         raise
756:     return CollectedTier(
757:         tier_index=tier_index,
758:         basis=capture.basis,
759:         sources=collected,
760:         include_pending_boundaries=tier_index == 0,
761:     )
762: 
763: 
764: def _overall_fact(
765:     invocation: SourceInvocationPlan,
766:     collected: Sequence[CollectedTier],
767:     *,
768:     now: float,
769: ) -> Fact:
770:     sources = [source for tier in collected for source in tier.sources]
771:     errors = sum(source.status != "ok" for source in sources)
772:     total = sum(len(source.facts) + 1 for source in sources)
773:     duration_ms = max(0, int((now - invocation.initial_capture.evaluated_at) * 1000))
774:     return Fact.of(
775:         "_sync",
776:         invocation.observer,
777:         ts=now,
778:         status="error" if errors else "ok",
779:         sources_run=len(invocation.qualifying_indices),
780:         sources_skipped=len(invocation.skipped),
781:         source_errors=errors,
782:         total_facts=total,
783:         duration_ms=duration_ms,
784:         invocation_id=invocation.invocation_id,
785:     )
786: 
787: 
788: def prepare_collected_tier(
789:     capture: RuntimeCapture,
790:     collected: CollectedTier,
791:     *,
792:     credentials: WriteCredentials,
793:     overall_fact: Fact | None = None,
794:     overall_sync_id: str | None = None,
795: ) -> BatchWritePlan:
796:     """Turn retained collection into one exact-H tier batch plan."""
797:     try:
798:         if collected.basis != capture.basis:
799:             raise ValueError(
800:                 "collected tier basis does not match the runtime capture basis"
801:             )
802:         invalid = next(
803:             (
804:                 source.invalid_output
805:                 for source in collected.sources
806:                 if source.invalid_output is not None
807:             ),
808:             None,
809:         )
810:         if invalid is not None:
811:             raise invalid
812:         items: list[BatchFactInput] = []
813:         for source in collected.sources:
814:             items.extend(
815:                 BatchFactInput(fact, fact_id=fact_id)
816:                 for fact, fact_id in zip(source.facts, source.fact_ids, strict=True)
817:             )
818:             items.append(
819:                 BatchFactInput(
820:                     source.lifecycle_fact,
821:                     fact_id=source.lifecycle_id,
822:                     admit_undeclared=True,
823:                 )
824:             )
825:         if overall_fact is not None:
826:             if overall_sync_id is None:
827:                 raise ValueError("overall_sync_id is required with overall_fact")
828:             items.append(
829:                 BatchFactInput(
830:                     overall_fact,
831:                     fact_id=overall_sync_id,
832:                     admit_undeclared=True,
833:                 )
834:             )
835:         return plan_batch_from_capture(
836:             capture,
837:             items,
838:             credentials=credentials,
839:             include_pending_boundaries=collected.include_pending_boundaries,
840:         )
841:     except Exception as exc:
842:         if isinstance(exc, SourceTierPreparationRefused):
843:             raise
844:         raise SourceTierPreparationRefused(collected, exc) from exc
845: 
846: 
847: def _dispatch_intents(
848:     capture: RuntimeCapture, plan: BatchWritePlan
849: ) -> tuple[DispatchIntent, ...]:
850:     runs = {
851:         **dict(zip(plan.pending_tick_ids, plan.pending_tick_runs, strict=True)),
852:         **{
853:             item.tick_id: item.tick_run
854:             for item in plan.items
855:             if item.tick_id is not None
856:         },
857:     }
858:     vertex_path = capture.effective_declaration.path
859:     intents: list[DispatchIntent] = []
860:     for draft in plan.drafts:
861:         if draft.kind != "tick":
862:             continue
863:         body = draft.body
864:         tick_id = str(body["id"])
865:         command = runs.get(tick_id)
866:         if not command:
867:             continue
868:         payload_text = str(body["payload"])
869:         payload = json.loads(payload_text)
870:         intents.append(
871:             DispatchIntent(
872:                 tick_id=tick_id,
873:                 tick_name=str(body["name"]),
874:                 command=command,
875:                 ts=float(body["ts"]),
876:                 since=None if body["since"] is None else float(body["since"]),
877:                 origin=str(body["origin"]),
878:                 payload_text=payload_text,
879:                 payload=MappingProxyType(payload),
880:                 fact_cursor=body.get("fact_cursor"),
881:                 window_start=body.get("window_start"),
882:                 window_hash=body.get("window_hash"),
883:                 vertex_path=vertex_path,
884:             )
885:         )
886:     return tuple(intents)
887: 
888: 
889: def _result(
890:     invocation: SourceInvocationPlan,
891:     *,
892:     bases: Sequence[ReadBasis],
893:     collected: Sequence[CollectedTier],
894:     durable: Sequence[TierCommitted],
895:     terminal_tier: CollectedTier | None = None,
896:     terminal_plan: BatchWritePlan | None = None,
897:     collected_uncommitted: CollectedTier | None = None,
898:     collected_unknown: CollectedTier | None = None,
899:     terminal: BaseException | None = None,
900: ) -> SourceInvocationResult:
901:     error_ids = tuple(
902:         source.lifecycle_id
903:         for tier in collected
904:         for source in tier.sources
905:         if source.status != "ok"
906:     )
907:     durable_indices = {tier.tier_index for tier in durable}
908:     durable_error_ids = tuple(
909:         source.lifecycle_id
910:         for tier in collected
911:         if tier.tier_index in durable_indices
912:         for source in tier.sources
913:         if source.status != "ok"
914:     )
915:     status: Literal["ok", "error", "incomplete"]
916:     if terminal is not None:
917:         status = "incomplete"
918:     elif error_ids:
919:         status = "error"
920:     else:
921:         status = "ok"
922:     return SourceInvocationResult(
923:         invocation_id=invocation.invocation_id,
924:         status=status,
925:         plan=invocation,
926:         bases=tuple(bases),
927:         collected_tiers=tuple(collected),
928:         durable_tiers=tuple(durable),
929:         terminal_tier=terminal_tier,
930:         terminal_plan=terminal_plan,
931:         collected_uncommitted=collected_uncommitted,
932:         collected_unknown=collected_unknown,
933:         error_lifecycle_ids=error_ids,
934:         durable_error_lifecycle_ids=durable_error_ids,
935:         overall_sync_id=invocation.overall_sync_id,
936:         terminal=terminal,
937:     )
938: 
939: 
940: async def execute_source_invocation(
941:     registry: BackendRegistry,
942:     descriptor: StoreDescriptor,
943:     locator: Any,
944:     invocation: SourceInvocationPlan,
945:     *,
946:     credentials: WriteCredentials,
947:     collector_factory: CollectorFactory | None = None,
948:     dispatcher: RunDispatcher | None = None,
949:     clock: Callable[[], float] = time.time,
950:     id_factory: Callable[[], str] = _new_id,
951: ) -> SourceInvocationResult:
952:     """Collect and commit fixed tiers once each; never retry external effects."""
953:     bases: list[ReadBasis] = []
954:     collected_tiers: list[CollectedTier] = []
955:     durable_tiers: list[TierCommitted] = []
956:     tiers: tuple[tuple[int, ...], ...] = invocation.tiers or ((),)
957:     current_capture = invocation.initial_capture
958:     frozen_locator = invocation.initial_capture._locator_copy()
959: 
960:     for tier_index, _indices in enumerate(tiers):
961:         if tier_index:
962:             try:
963:                 current_capture = capture_runtime(
964:                     registry,
965:                     descriptor,
966:                     frozen_locator,
967:                     credentials=credentials,
968:                 )
969:             except Exception as exc:
970:                 return _result(
971:                     invocation,
972:                     bases=bases,
973:                     collected=collected_tiers,
974:                     durable=durable_tiers,
975:                     terminal=exc,
976:                 )
977:             if (
978:                 current_capture.declaration_documents
979:                 != invocation.initial_capture.declaration_documents
980:             ):
981:                 changed = DeclarationChangedDuringInvocation(
982:                     invocation.initial_capture, current_capture
983:                 )
984:                 return _result(
985:                     invocation,
986:                     bases=(*bases, current_capture.basis),
987:                     collected=collected_tiers,
988:                     durable=durable_tiers,
989:                     terminal=changed,
990:                 )
991:         bases.append(current_capture.basis)
992: 
993:         if invocation.tiers:
994:             try:
995:                 tier = await collect_source_tier(
996:                     invocation,
997:                     current_capture,
998:                     tier_index,
999:                     collector_factory,
1000:                     clock=clock,
1001:                     id_factory=id_factory,
1002:                 )
1003:             except SourceTierCollectionFailed as exc:
1004:                 return _result(
1005:                     invocation,
1006:                     bases=bases,
1007:                     collected=collected_tiers,
1008:                     durable=durable_tiers,
1009:                     terminal=exc,
1010:                 )
1011:         else:
1012:             tier = CollectedTier(
1013:                 tier_index=0,
1014:                 basis=current_capture.basis,
1015:                 sources=(),
1016:                 include_pending_boundaries=True,
1017:             )
1018:         collected_tiers.append(tier)
1019:         final_tier = tier_index == len(tiers) - 1
1020:         try:
1021:             overall = (
1022:                 _overall_fact(invocation, collected_tiers, now=clock())
1023:                 if final_tier
1024:                 else None
1025:             )
1026:             batch_plan = prepare_collected_tier(
1027:                 current_capture,
1028:                 tier,
1029:                 credentials=credentials,
1030:                 overall_fact=overall,
1031:                 overall_sync_id=invocation.overall_sync_id if final_tier else None,
1032:             )
1033:         except Exception as exc:
1034:             return _result(
1035:                 invocation,
1036:                 bases=bases,
1037:                 collected=collected_tiers,
1038:                 durable=durable_tiers,
1039:                 terminal_tier=tier,
1040:                 collected_uncommitted=tier,
1041:                 terminal=exc,
1042:             )
1043: 
1044:         try:
1045:             intents = _dispatch_intents(current_capture, batch_plan)
1046:         except Exception as exc:
1047:             return _result(
1048:                 invocation,
1049:                 bases=bases,
1050:                 collected=collected_tiers,
1051:                 durable=durable_tiers,
1052:                 terminal_tier=tier,
1053:                 terminal_plan=batch_plan,
1054:                 collected_uncommitted=tier,
1055:                 terminal=exc,
1056:             )
1057: 
1058:         try:
1059:             outcome = execute_batch_write(
1060:                 registry,
1061:                 descriptor,
1062:                 batch_plan,
1063:                 after_commit=lambda head: sync_projection(
1064:                     registry, descriptor, through=head
1065:                 ),
1066:             )
1067:         except NotWitnessed as exc:
1068:             if exc.commit is not None:
1069:                 outcome = CommittedBatchWrite(
1070:                     exc.commit,
1071:                     batch_plan.items,
1072:                     ProjectionOutcome.PENDING,
1073:                     batch_plan.pending_tick_ids,
1074:                     batch_plan.pending_tick_names,
1075:                     batch_plan.pending_tick_runs,
1076:                 )
1077:                 durable_tiers.append(
1078:                     TierCommitted(
1079:                         tier_index,
1080:                         current_capture.basis,
1081:                         tier,
1082:                         batch_plan,
1083:                         outcome,
1084:                         witnessed=False,
1085:                         dispatch_intents=intents,
1086:                     )
1087:                 )
1088:             return _result(
1089:                 invocation,
1090:                 bases=bases,
1091:                 collected=collected_tiers,
1092:                 durable=durable_tiers,
1093:                 terminal_tier=tier,
1094:                 terminal_plan=batch_plan,
1095:                 collected_uncommitted=None if exc.commit is not None else tier,
1096:                 terminal=exc,
1097:             )
1098:         except BatchPostCommitProjectionFailed as exc:
1099:             outcome = CommittedBatchWrite(
1100:                 exc.commit,
1101:                 batch_plan.items,
1102:                 ProjectionOutcome.FAILED,
1103:                 batch_plan.pending_tick_ids,
1104:                 batch_plan.pending_tick_names,
1105:                 batch_plan.pending_tick_runs,
1106:             )
1107:             durable_tiers.append(
1108:                 TierCommitted(
1109:                     tier_index,
1110:                     current_capture.basis,
1111:                     tier,
1112:                     batch_plan,
1113:                     outcome,
1114:                     witnessed=True,
1115:                     dispatch_intents=intents,
1116:                 )
1117:             )
1118:             return _result(
1119:                 invocation,
1120:                 bases=bases,
1121:                 collected=collected_tiers,
1122:                 durable=durable_tiers,
1123:                 terminal_tier=tier,
1124:                 terminal_plan=batch_plan,
1125:                 terminal=exc,
1126:             )
1127:         except BatchWriteCommitUnknown as exc:
1128:             # The append was attempted exactly once, but its durability is unknown.
1129:             # Calling this tier "uncommitted" would invite an unsafe retry.
1130:             return _result(
1131:                 invocation,
1132:                 bases=bases,
1133:                 collected=collected_tiers,
1134:                 durable=durable_tiers,
1135:                 terminal_tier=tier,
1136:                 terminal_plan=batch_plan,
1137:                 collected_unknown=tier,
1138:                 terminal=exc,
1139:             )
1140:         except Exception as exc:
1141:             return _result(
1142:                 invocation,
1143:                 bases=bases,
1144:                 collected=collected_tiers,
1145:                 durable=durable_tiers,
1146:                 terminal_tier=tier,
1147:                 terminal_plan=batch_plan,
1148:                 collected_uncommitted=tier,
1149:                 terminal=exc,
1150:             )
1151: 
1152:         committed = TierCommitted(
1153:             tier_index=tier_index,
1154:             basis=current_capture.basis,
1155:             collected=tier,
1156:             plan=batch_plan,
1157:             outcome=outcome,
1158:             witnessed=True,
1159:             dispatch_intents=intents,
1160:         )
1161:         if dispatcher is not None and intents:
1162:             try:
1163:                 dispatcher(committed.commit, intents)
1164:             except Exception as exc:
1165:                 committed = replace(committed, dispatch_succeeded=False)
1166:                 durable_tiers.append(committed)
1167:                 failed = DispatchFailed(committed.commit, intents, exc)
1168:                 return _result(
1169:                     invocation,
1170:                     bases=bases,
1171:                     collected=collected_tiers,
1172:                     durable=durable_tiers,
1173:                     terminal_tier=tier,
1174:                     terminal_plan=batch_plan,
1175:                     terminal=failed,
1176:                 )
1177:             committed = replace(committed, dispatch_succeeded=True)
1178:         durable_tiers.append(committed)
1179: 
1180:     return _result(
1181:         invocation,
1182:         bases=bases,
1183:         collected=collected_tiers,
1184:         durable=durable_tiers,
1185:     )
