39: @dataclass(frozen=True)
40: class DeclarationEditResult(KindMutationResult):
41:     """Serializable public outcome for descriptor-backed declaration edits.
42: 
43:     ``file_written`` means this call published the cache. ``phase`` describes
44:     the reconciled state, which can already be published when recovery starts.
45:     """
46: 
47:     schema: str = "loops.sdk/declaration-edit/v2"
48:     read_path: str = "arrival"
49:     target_path: str = ""
50:     store: StoreDescriptorInfo | None = None
51:     basis: dict[str, Any] | None = None
52:     lineage: str | None = None
53:     captured_head: dict[str, Any] | None = None
54:     head: dict[str, Any] | None = None
55:     commit: Commit | None = None
56:     fact_ids: tuple[str, ...] = ()
57:     intent_path: str | None = None
58:     phase: str = ""
59:     file_written: bool = False
60:     changes: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None
61: 
62:     def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
63:         result = {item.name: getattr(self, item.name) for item in fields(self)}
64:         result["store"] = None if self.store is None else asdict(self.store)
65:         result["fact_ids"] = list(self.fact_ids)
66:         result["commit"] = _commit_dict(self.commit)
67:         return result
68: 
69: 
70: @dataclass(frozen=True)
71: class DeclarationPreviewResult(DeclarationPlanResult):
72:     """v2 semantic preview carrying the explicit Arrival read basis."""
73: 
74:     schema: str = "loops.sdk/declaration-preview/v2"
75:     read_path: str = "arrival"
76:     store: StoreDescriptorInfo | None = None
77:     basis: dict[str, Any] | None = None
78:     lineage: str | None = None
79:     captured_head: dict[str, Any] | None = None
80: 
81:     def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
82:         return asdict(self)
83: 
84: 
85: def _head_dict(head: Any) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
86:     if head is None:
87:         return None
88:     return {
89:         "lineage": head.lineage,
90:         "ordinal": head.ordinal,
91:         "record_hash": head.record_hash,
92:     }
93: 
94: 
95: def _change_dict(change: Any) -> dict[str, Any] | str:
96:     if hasattr(change, "as_dict"):
97:         return change.as_dict()
98:     if hasattr(change, "_asdict"):
99:         return dict(change._asdict())
100:     return str(change)
101: 
102: 
103: def _arrival_result(result: Any) -> DeclarationEditResult:
104:     commit = result.commit
105:     changes = (
106:         None
107:         if result.changes is None
108:         else [_change_dict(change) for change in result.changes]
109:     )
110:     return DeclarationEditResult(
111:         status=result.status,
112:         mode="arrival-edit",
113:         vertex_path=str(result.target_path),
114:         target_path=str(result.target_path),
115:         store=StoreDescriptorInfo.from_descriptor(result.descriptor),
116:         basis=asdict(result.basis),
117:         lineage=result.lineage,
118:         captured_head=_head_dict(result.captured_head),
119:         head=_head_dict(result.head),
120:         commit=commit,
121:         fact_ids=tuple(result.fact_ids),
122:         intent_path=None if result.intent_path is None else str(result.intent_path),
123:         phase=result.phase,
124:         file_written=result.file_written,
125:         changes=changes,
126:     )
127: 
128: 
129: def _arrival_credentials(
130:     vertex_path: Path, credentials: CredentialProvider | WriteCredentials | None
131: ) -> WriteCredentials:
132:     if credentials is None:
133:         from .emit import CustodyCredentialProvider
134: 
135:         credentials = CustodyCredentialProvider()
136:     if isinstance(credentials, WriteCredentials):
137:         return credentials
138:     provider = getattr(credentials, "for_write", None)
139:     if not callable(provider):
140:         raise SdkValueError("Arrival declaration edits require a write credential provider")
141:     supplied = provider(vertex_path)
142:     if not isinstance(supplied, WriteCredentials):
143:         raise SdkValueError("credential provider did not return WriteCredentials")
144:     return supplied
145: 
146: 
147: def _arrival_verifiers():
148:     from sign import ed25519
149: 
150:     def verify(domain: str, key: str, signature: str, digest: str) -> bool:
151:         try:
152:             public = ed25519.public_key_from_b64(key)
153:         except ValueError:
154:             return False
155:         return ed25519.verify(public, signature, digest.encode(), domain=domain)
156: 
157:     return (
158:         lambda key, signature, digest: verify(FACT_DOMAIN, key, signature, digest),
159:         lambda key, signature, digest: verify(ARRIVAL_DOMAIN, key, signature, digest),
160:     )
161: 
162: 
163: def edit_declaration(
164:     target: Path | str,
165:     proposed_text: str,
166:     *,
167:     observer: str,
168:     credentials: CredentialProvider | WriteCredentials | None = None,
169:     registry: BackendRegistry | None = None,
170: ) -> DeclarationEditResult:
171:     """Prepare and apply one signed edit against an explicit Arrival descriptor.
172: 
173:     The descriptor is parsed before any legacy target probe. The engine owns
174:     the attested CURRENT basis, signed draft construction, full-head append,
175:     durable intent, projection sync, and cache recovery protocol.
176:     """
177:     return _edit_arrival_declaration(
178:         target,
179:         proposed_text,
180:         observer=observer,
181:         credentials=credentials,
182:         registry=registry,
183:     )
184: 
185: 
186: def _edit_arrival_declaration(
187:     target: Path | str,
188:     proposed_text: str,
189:     *,
190:     observer: str,
191:     credentials: CredentialProvider | WriteCredentials | None,
192:     registry: BackendRegistry | None,
193:     source_cache_bytes: bytes | None = None,
194:     require_cache_basis: bool = False,
195: ) -> DeclarationEditResult:
196:     from engine.arrival_declarations import (
197:         apply_declaration_edit,
198:         prepare_declaration_edit,
199:     )
200: 
201:     from .errors import normalize_exception
202:     from .target import _arrival_descriptor
203: 
204:     vertex_path = Path(target).resolve()
205:     resolved = _arrival_descriptor(vertex_path)
206:     if resolved is None:
207:         raise TargetUnsupported(
208:             f"edit_declaration requires an explicit Arrival .vertex target: {vertex_path}"
209:         )
210:     _path, _ast, descriptor = resolved
211:     active_registry = (
212:         registry if registry is not None else BackendRegistry.with_builtin_backends()
213:     )
214:     write_credentials = _arrival_credentials(vertex_path, credentials)
215:     fact_verify, arrival_verify = _arrival_verifiers()
216:     try:
217:         plan = prepare_declaration_edit(
218:             active_registry,
219:             descriptor,
220:             target=vertex_path,
221:             proposed_text=proposed_text,
222:             observer=observer,
223:             credentials=write_credentials,
224:             fact_verify=fact_verify,
225:             arrival_verify=arrival_verify,
226:             source_cache_bytes=source_cache_bytes,
227:             require_cache_basis=require_cache_basis,
228:         )
229:         result = apply_declaration_edit(active_registry, plan)
230:     except Exception as exc:
231:         normalized = normalize_exception(exc)
232:         if normalized is exc:
233:             raise
234:         raise normalized from exc
235:     return _arrival_result(result)
236: 
237: 
238: def recover_declaration(
239:     intent: Path | str,
240:     *,
241:     registry: BackendRegistry | None = None,
242: ) -> DeclarationEditResult:
243:     """Recover one interrupted explicit Arrival declaration edit."""
244:     from engine.arrival_declarations import recover_declaration_edit
245: 
246:     from .errors import normalize_exception
247: 
248:     active_registry = (
249:         registry if registry is not None else BackendRegistry.with_builtin_backends()
250:     )
251:     try:
252:         result = recover_declaration_edit(active_registry, intent)
253:     except Exception as exc:
254:         normalized = normalize_exception(exc)
255:         if normalized is exc:
256:             raise
257:         raise normalized from exc
258:     return _arrival_result(result)
259: 
260: 
261: def _kdl_quote(value: str, *, field: str) -> str:
262:     """Encode one generated single-line KDL string value safely."""
263:     if any(ord(char) < 0x20 or ord(char) == 0x7F for char in value):
264:         raise SdkValueError(f"{field} contains KDL control characters")
265:     return json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False)
266: 
267: 
268: def init_vertex(
269:     target: Path | str,
270:     *,
271:     name: str | None = None,
272:     store_type: str = "sqlite",
273:     store_path: str | Path | None = None,
274:     is_root: bool = False,
275:     observer: str | None = None,
276:     strict: bool = False,
277:     overwrite: bool = False,
278:     backend: str | None = None,
279:     location: str | Path | None = None,
280:     documents: list[dict] | None = None,
281:     signer=None,
282:     fact_signer=None,
283:     public_key: str | None = None,
284:     credentials: CredentialProvider | None = None,
285:     recover: bool = False,
286:     registry: BackendRegistry | None = None,
287: ) -> InitVertexResult:
288:     """Scaffold a new Loops .vertex declaration file.
289: 
290:     Parameters:
291:         target: Target .vertex file path to create.
292:         name: Name of the vertex (defaults to target file stem).
293:         store_type: Backing store format ('sqlite' or 'jsonl').
294:         store_path: Custom store path (defaults to .loops/data/<name>.db or .jsonl).
295:         is_root: If True, scaffolds an aggregate root discovery vertex.
296:         observer: Initial observer identity to register.
297:         strict: If True, declares 'strict true'.
298:         overwrite: If True, overwrites existing file.
299: 
300:     Returns:
301:         InitVertexResult detailing the created vertex file and storage paths.
302:     """
303:     vertex_path = Path(target).resolve()
304: 
305:     # Arrival initialization is an explicit extension of this existing SDK
306:     # operation.  Keeping the legacy arm below preserves callers that ask for
307:     # SQLite/JSONL scaffolding while the clean Arrival cut is adopted; no
308:     # legacy opener is reached by the descriptor arm.
309:     arrival_requested = (
310:         store_type.lower() == "arrival"
311:         or backend is not None
312:         or location is not None
313:         or documents is not None
314:         or recover
315:     )
316:     if arrival_requested and not is_root:
317:         return _init_arrival(
318:             vertex_path,
319:             name=name,
320:             strict=strict,
321:             observer=observer,
322:             backend=backend,
323:             location=location,
324:             store_path=store_path,
325:             documents=documents,
326:             signer=signer,
327:             fact_signer=fact_signer,
328:             public_key=public_key,
329:             credentials=credentials,
330:             recover=recover,
331:             overwrite=overwrite,
332:             registry=registry,
333:         )
334:     if credentials is not None:
335:         raise TargetUnsupported(
336:             "initialization credentials require an explicit Arrival descriptor target"
337:         )
338:     if vertex_path.exists() and not overwrite:
339:         raise TargetError(f"vertex file already exists: {vertex_path}")
340: 
341:     v_name = name or vertex_path.stem
342: 
343:     if is_root:
344:         content = (
345:             f'name "{v_name}"\n'
346:             "// Root vertex — discovers all .vertex files under this directory\n"
347:             'discover "./**/*.vertex"\n'
348:         )
349:         resolved_store = None
350:     else:
351:         ext = ".jsonl" if store_type.lower() == "jsonl" else ".db"
352:         default_store = f".loops/data/{v_name}{ext}"
353:         resolved_store = str(store_path) if store_path is not None else default_store
354: 
355:         lines = [f'name "{v_name}"', f'store "{resolved_store}"']
356:         if strict:
357:             lines.append("strict true")
358:         lines.append("")
359: 
360:         if observer:
361:             # Ensure keypair exists for initial observer
362:             from custody import ensure_signing_key
363: 
364:             keypair = ensure_signing_key(vertex_path, observer=observer)
365:             lines.append("observers {")
366:             lines.append(f"  {observer} {{")
367:             lines.append(f'    key "{keypair.public_b64}"')
368:             lines.append("  }")
369:             lines.append("}")
370:             lines.append("")
371: 
372:         lines.append("loops {")
373:         lines.append("  item {")
374:         lines.append("    fold {")
375:         lines.append('      items "collect" 100')
376:         lines.append("    }")
377:         lines.append("  }")
378:         lines.append("}")
379:         lines.append("")
380:         content = "\n".join(lines)
381: 
382:     # Ensure parent directory exists
383:     vertex_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
384:     vertex_path.write_text(content, encoding="utf-8")
385: 
386:     return InitVertexResult(
387:         target_path=str(vertex_path),
388:         name=v_name,
389:         store_path=resolved_store,
390:         store_type=store_type,
391:         is_root=is_root,
392:         file_written=True,
393:     )
394: 
395: 
396: def _init_arrival(
397:     vertex_path: Path,
398:     *,
399:     name: str | None,
400:     strict: bool,
401:     observer: str | None,
402:     backend: str | None,
403:     location: str | Path | None,
404:     store_path: str | Path | None,
405:     documents: list[dict] | None,
406:     signer,
407:     fact_signer,
408:     public_key: str | None,
409:     credentials: CredentialProvider | None,
410:     recover: bool,
411:     overwrite: bool,
412:     registry: BackendRegistry | None,
413: ) -> InitVertexResult:
414:     """Compose custody and language inputs for the engine initializer."""
415:     from engine.arrival_contract import Profile, StoreDescriptor
416:     from engine.arrival_initialization import (
417:         arrival_intent_path,
418:         initialize_arrival,
419:         new_lineage,
420:         recover_arrival_initialization,
421:     )
422:     from engine.declaration import validate_arrival_runtime_identity
423:     from lang import parse_vertex, vertex_to_documents
424: 
425:     from .errors import normalize_exception
426: 
427:     if overwrite:
428:         raise TargetError("Arrival initialization never overwrites an existing artifact")
429:     intent_data = None
430:     if recover:
431:         try:
432:             intent_data = json.loads(arrival_intent_path(vertex_path).read_text())
433:             reserved = intent_data["descriptor"]
434:             reserved_observer = str(intent_data["observer"])
435:             if observer is None:
436:                 observer = reserved_observer
437:             elif observer != reserved_observer:
438:                 raise SdkValueError(
439:                     "recovery observer disagrees with the reserved initialization intent"
440:                 )
441:             if backend is not None and backend != reserved["backend"]:
442:                 raise SdkValueError(
443:                     "recovery backend disagrees with the reserved initialization intent"
444:                 )
445:             if public_key is not None and public_key != str(intent_data["public_key"]):
446:                 raise SdkValueError(
447:                     "recovery public key disagrees with the reserved initialization intent"
448:                 )
449:             requested_location = location if location is not None else store_path
450:             if reserved["backend"] == "file" and requested_location is not None:
451:                 requested_location = str(
452:                     (vertex_path.parent / str(requested_location)).resolve()
453:                     if not Path(str(requested_location)).is_absolute()
454:                     else Path(str(requested_location)).resolve()
455:                 )
456:             if (
457:                 requested_location is not None
458:                 and str(requested_location) != str(reserved["location"])
459:             ):
460:                 raise SdkValueError(
461:                     "recovery location disagrees with the reserved initialization intent"
462:                 )
463:         except SdkValueError:
464:             raise
465:         except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError) as exc:
466:             raise TargetError(f"cannot load Arrival initialization intent: {exc}") from exc
467:     if not observer:
468:         raise SdkValueError("Arrival initialization requires observer")
469:     if location is not None and store_path is not None and str(location) != str(store_path):
470:         raise SdkValueError(
471:             "Arrival initialization received conflicting location and store_path"
472:         )
473:     if location is None:
474:         location = store_path
475:     v_name = name or vertex_path.stem
476:     if not recover:
477:         # The SDK scaffold declares item, and the runtime also adds cite.
478:         # Refuse these collisions before the default custody path creates keys.
479:         validate_arrival_runtime_identity(v_name, ("item",), refusal=SdkValueError)
480:     chosen_backend = backend or "file"
481:     if location is None:
482:         relative_location = f".loops/data/{v_name}.arrival"
483:         descriptor_location = str((vertex_path.parent / relative_location).resolve())
484:     elif chosen_backend == "file":
485:         relative_location = str(location)
486:         descriptor_location = str((vertex_path.parent / relative_location).resolve())
487:     else:
488:         # A DSN is opaque to the SDK as well as to the engine registry.
489:         relative_location = str(location)
490:         descriptor_location = relative_location
491: 
492:     # Verification is independent of where signing callables came from.  A
493:     # caller-supplied pair still has to prove both protocol domains against
494:     # the supplied public key before an intent or mint is attempted.
495:     from custody.signing import ARRIVAL_DOMAIN, FACT_DOMAIN
496:     from sign import ed25519
497: 
498:     def _verify(domain: str, key: str, signature: str, digest: str) -> bool:
499:         try:
500:             public = ed25519.public_key_from_b64(key)
501:         except ValueError:
502:             return False
503:         return ed25519.verify(public, signature, digest.encode(), domain=domain)
504: 
505:     def _fact_verify(key: str, signature: str, digest: str) -> bool:
506:         return _verify(FACT_DOMAIN, key, signature, digest)
507: 
508:     def _arrival_verify(key: str, signature: str, digest: str) -> bool:
509:         return _verify(ARRIVAL_DOMAIN, key, signature, digest)
510: 
511:     fact_verify = _fact_verify
512:     arrival_verify = _arrival_verify
513:     if not recover:
514:         # The SDK is the custody owner. An injected signer/key pair is useful
515:         # to headless deployments; absent one, key creation/loading is the
516:         # same explicit custody operation used by the existing init command.
517:         from custody import arrival_signer_for, ensure_signing_key, fact_signer_for
518:         from engine.credentials import (
519:             CredentialBindingEvidence,
520:             CredentialPurpose,
521:             CredentialRequest,
522:             ResolvedCredential,
523:             SigningDomain,
524:         )
525: 
526:         if credentials is not None:
527:             if signer is not None or fact_signer is not None:
528:                 raise SdkValueError(
529:                     "mapped Arrival initialization does not accept separate signer inputs"
530:                 )
531:             write_credentials = _arrival_credentials(vertex_path, credentials)
532:             if not write_credentials.mapped or write_credentials.binding_resolver is None:
533:                 raise SdkValueError(
534:                     "Arrival initialization requires a mapped provider or explicit signers"
535:                 )
536:             fact_request = CredentialRequest(
537:                 write_credentials.binding_namespace or "",
538:                 observer,
539:                 SigningDomain.FACT,
540:                 CredentialPurpose.INITIALIZATION,
541:             )
542:             arrival_request = CredentialRequest(
543:                 write_credentials.binding_namespace or "",
544:                 observer,
545:                 SigningDomain.ARRIVAL,
546:                 CredentialPurpose.INITIALIZATION,
547:             )
548:             try:
549:                 fact_credential = write_credentials.binding_resolver(fact_request)
550:                 arrival_credential = write_credentials.binding_resolver(arrival_request)
551:             except Exception as exc:
552:                 normalized = normalize_exception(exc)
553:                 if normalized is not exc:
554:                     raise normalized from exc
555:                 raise SdkValueError(
556:                     "Arrival initialization could not resolve its pre-created mapped binding"
557:                 ) from exc
558:             if fact_credential is None or arrival_credential is None:
559:                 raise SdkValueError(
560:                     "mapped Arrival initialization requires a pre-created founding binding"
561:                 )
562:             def valid_bootstrap_credential(value: object, request: CredentialRequest) -> bool:
563:                 if not isinstance(value, ResolvedCredential):
564:                     return False
565:                 evidence = value.evidence
566:                 return (
567:                     isinstance(evidence, CredentialBindingEvidence)
568:                     and evidence.request == request
569:                     and all(
570:                         isinstance(item, str) and bool(item)
571:                         for item in (
572:                             evidence.key_ref,
573:                             evidence.algorithm,
574:                             evidence.public_key,
575:                             evidence.provenance,
576:                         )
577:                     )
578:                     and callable(value.sign_digest)
579:                 )
580: 
581:             fact_valid = valid_bootstrap_credential(fact_credential, fact_request)
582:             arrival_valid = valid_bootstrap_credential(arrival_credential, arrival_request)
583:             if not fact_valid or not arrival_valid:
584:                 raise SdkValueError(
585:                     "mapped Arrival initialization resolver returned contradictory binding evidence"
586:                 )
587:             if (
588:                 fact_credential.evidence.public_key != arrival_credential.evidence.public_key
589:                 or fact_credential.evidence.key_ref != arrival_credential.evidence.key_ref
590:             ):
591:                 raise SdkValueError(
592:                     "mapped Arrival initialization requires FACT and ARRIVAL bindings with one key"
593:                 )
594:             if public_key is not None and public_key != fact_credential.evidence.public_key:
595:                 raise SdkValueError(
596:                     "mapped Arrival initialization public_key disagrees with its binding"
597:                 )
598:             public_key = fact_credential.evidence.public_key
599: 
600:             def mapped_arrival_signer(author: str, digest: str) -> str | None:
601:                 return arrival_credential.sign_digest(digest) if author == observer else None
602: 
603:             def mapped_fact_signer(author: str, digest: str) -> str | None:
604:                 return fact_credential.sign_digest(digest) if author == observer else None
605: 
606:             signer = mapped_arrival_signer
607:             fact_signer = mapped_fact_signer
608:         elif signer is None and fact_signer is None and public_key is None:
609:             try:
610:                 keypair = ensure_signing_key(vertex_path, observer=observer)
611:             except ValueError as exc:
612:                 raise SdkValueError(str(exc)) from exc
613:             public_key = keypair.public_b64
614:             signer = arrival_signer_for(vertex_path)
615:             fact_signer = fact_signer_for(vertex_path)
616:         elif (
617:             signer is None
618:             or fact_signer is None
619:             or public_key is None
620:             or not callable(signer)
621:             or not callable(fact_signer)
622:         ):
623:             raise SdkValueError(
624:                 "Arrival initialization custom credentials require arrival signer, "
625:                 "fact signer, and public key"
626:             )
627:         if signer is None or fact_signer is None or public_key is None:
628:             raise SdkValueError("Arrival initialization requires usable custody signers")
629: 
630:     lineage = None
631:     if not recover:
632:         lineage = new_lineage()
633:         lines = [
634:             f"name {_kdl_quote(v_name, field='vertex name')}",
635:             f"store {_kdl_quote(relative_location, field='store location')} "
636:             f"backend={_kdl_quote(chosen_backend, field='backend')} "
637:             f"lineage={_kdl_quote(lineage, field='lineage')} role=\"authority\"",
638:         ]
639:         if strict:
640:             lines.append("strict true")
641:         lines.extend(
642:             [
643:                 "observers {",
644:                 f"  {_kdl_quote(observer, field='observer')} {{",
645:                 f"    key {_kdl_quote(public_key, field='public key')}",
646:                 "  }",
647:                 "}",
648:                 "",
649:                 "loops {",
650:                 "  item {",
651:                 "    fold {",
652:                 '      items "collect" 100',
653:                 "    }",
654:                 "  }",
655:                 "}",
656:                 "",
657:             ]
658:         )
659:         declaration_text = "\n".join(lines)
660:         try:
661:             ast = parse_vertex(declaration_text, path=vertex_path)
662:         except Exception as exc:
663:             raise SdkValueError(f"invalid Arrival declaration text: {exc}") from exc
664:         parsed_documents = [doc.as_json() for doc in vertex_to_documents(ast)]
665:         if documents is None:
666:             documents = parsed_documents
667:         elif documents != parsed_documents:
668:             raise SdkValueError(
669:                 "Arrival declaration text and declaration documents disagree"
670:             )
671:         if (
672:             ast.store_backend is None
673:             or ast.store_backend.name != chosen_backend
674:             or ast.store_backend.role != "authority"
675:             or ast.store_backend.lineage != lineage
676:         ):
677:             raise SdkValueError(
678:                 "Arrival declaration must carry the requested backend, lineage and authority role"
679:             )
680:     else:
681:         # Recovery reads the stable observer and descriptor from the intent;
682:         # the target is not re-rendered and no new lineage is selected.
683:         try:
684:             intent_data = intent_data or json.loads(arrival_intent_path(vertex_path).read_text())
685:             lineage = str(intent_data["lineage"])
686:             declaration_text = str(intent_data["declaration_text"])
687:             if documents is None:
688:                 documents = list(intent_data["documents"])
689:             reserved_descriptor = intent_data["descriptor"]
690:             chosen_backend = str(reserved_descriptor["backend"])
691:             descriptor_location = str(reserved_descriptor["location"])
692:             relative_location = descriptor_location
693:             parsed = parse_vertex(declaration_text, path=vertex_path)
694:             v_name = parsed.name
695:         except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError) as exc:
696:             raise TargetError(f"cannot load Arrival initialization intent: {exc}") from exc
697: 
698:     descriptor = StoreDescriptor(
699:         backend=chosen_backend,
700:         location=descriptor_location,
701:         lineage=lineage,
702:         role=Profile.AUTHORITY,
703:     )
704:     active_registry = (
705:         registry if registry is not None else BackendRegistry.with_builtin_backends()
706:     )
707:     try:
708:         if recover:
709:             result = recover_arrival_initialization(
710:                 active_registry,
711:                 arrival_intent_path(vertex_path),
712:             )
713:         else:
714:             result = initialize_arrival(
715:                 active_registry,
716:                 descriptor,
717:                 target=vertex_path,
718:                 documents=documents or (),
719:                 declaration_text=declaration_text,
720:                 observer=observer,
721:                 public_key=public_key,
722:                 signer=signer,
723:                 fact_signer=fact_signer,
724:                 arrival_signer=signer,
725:                 fact_verify=fact_verify,
726:                 arrival_verify=arrival_verify,
727:                 lineage=lineage,
728:             )
729:     except BaseException as exc:
730:         details = {"intent_path": str(arrival_intent_path(vertex_path))}
731:         context = {"fact_id": lineage} if lineage is not None else None
732:         normalized = normalize_exception(exc, context=context)
733:         if hasattr(normalized, "details"):
734:             normalized.details.update(details)
735:         if normalized is exc:
736:             # Keep SDK exceptions and process-control exceptions exactly as
737:             # raised.  ``raise exc from exc`` would install a self-cause and
738:             # obscure both cancellation and an already-established cause.
739:             raise
740:         raise normalized from exc
741:     return InitVertexResult(
742:         target_path=str(vertex_path),
743:         name=v_name,
744:         read_path="arrival",
745:         store=StoreDescriptorInfo(
746:             backend=chosen_backend,
747:             location=descriptor_location,
748:             lineage=result.lineage,
749:             role="authority",
750:         ),
751:         store_path=relative_location,
752:         store_type="arrival",
753:         is_root=False,
754:         file_written=result.file_written,
755:         lineage=result.lineage,
756:         head={
757:             "lineage": result.head.lineage,
758:             "ordinal": result.head.ordinal,
759:             "record_hash": result.head.record_hash,
760:         },
761:         phase=result.phase,
762:     )
763: 
764: 
765: def inspect_declaration(
766:     target: Path | str,
767:     *,
768:     registry: BackendRegistry | None = None,
769: ) -> DeclarationInspectionResult:
770:     """Inspect a .vertex declaration without materializing its projection.
771: 
772:     Parameters:
773:         target: Path to the .vertex file.
774:         registry: Optional Arrival backend registry used for descriptor opens.
775: 
776:     Returns:
777:         DeclarationInspectionResult containing AST metadata, admission rules, and syntax health.
778:     """
779:     path = Path(target).resolve()
780:     if not path.exists():
781:         raise TargetNotFound(f"target path does not exist: {path}")
782: 
783:     # A descriptor is residence, not a legacy probe hint. Establish its
784:     # attested bounded snapshot before considering the compatibility branch.
785:     from .read import _arrival_declaration, _open_arrival_read
786:     from .target import _arrival_definition
787: 
788:     definition = _arrival_definition(path, allow_aggregate=True)
789:     if definition is not None and definition[2] is not None:
790:         definition_path, definition_ast, descriptor = definition
791:         arrival = (definition_path, definition_ast, descriptor)
792:         with _open_arrival_read(arrival, registry=registry) as (
793:             locator_ast,
794:             descriptor,
795:             opened,
796:         ):
797:             effective_ast, effective_status, _facts, _own_lineage = _arrival_declaration(
798:                 locator_ast, path, opened, allow_aggregate=True
799:             )
800:             local_fingerprint = _document_fingerprint(locator_ast)
801:             effective_fingerprint = _document_fingerprint(effective_ast)
802:             declared_kinds, declared_observers, cadence_ticks, strict, is_aggregate = (
803:                 _inspection_fields(effective_ast)
804:             )
805:             local_combine, local_discover = _inspection_topology(locator_ast)
806:             effective_combine, effective_discover = _inspection_topology(effective_ast)
807:             return DeclarationInspectionResult(
808:                 read_path="arrival",
809:                 store=StoreDescriptorInfo.from_descriptor(descriptor),
810:                 basis=opened.basis,
811:                 target_path=str(path),
812:                 name=effective_ast.name,
813:                 status=effective_status,
814:                 local_status=(
815:                     "matches-effective"
816:                     if local_fingerprint == effective_fingerprint
817:                     else "drifted"
818:                 ),
819:                 effective_status=effective_status,
820:                 local_fingerprint=local_fingerprint,
821:                 effective_fingerprint=effective_fingerprint,
822:                 store_mode=descriptor.backend,
823:                 store_path=descriptor.location,
824:                 declared_kinds=declared_kinds,
825:                 declared_observers=declared_observers,
826:                 cadence_ticks=cadence_ticks,
827:                 strict=strict,
828:                 is_aggregate=is_aggregate,
829:                 local_combine=local_combine,
830:                 local_discover=local_discover,
831:                 effective_combine=effective_combine,
832:                 effective_discover=effective_discover,
833:                 syntax_valid=True,
834:                 errors=[],
835:             )
836: 
837:     # A valid storeless aggregate has no adopted declaration to consult. Its
838:     # root AST is the exact one just parsed by the target resolver, avoiding a
839:     # second file read before a legacy probe could observe replacement bytes.
840:     if (
841:         definition is not None
842:         and definition[2] is None
843:         and definition[1].store is None
844:         and (definition[1].combine is not None or definition[1].discover is not None)
845:     ):
846:         return _frozen_local_aggregate_inspection(definition[0], definition[1])
847: 
848:     probe = probe_target(path)
849:     if probe.target_type != "vertex":
850:         raise SdkValueError(
851:             f"inspect_declaration requires a .vertex target, got {probe.target_type}"
852:         )
853: 
854:     errors: list[str] = []
855:     syntax_valid = True
856:     file_ast = None
857:     try:
858:         file_ast = parse_vertex_file(path)
859:     except Exception as exc:
860:         syntax_valid = False
861:         errors.append(str(exc))
862: 
863:     decl_status = None
864:     if syntax_valid:
865:         try:
866:             decl_ast, decl_status = load_declaration_status(path)
867:         except Exception as exc:
868:             errors.append(str(exc))
869: 
870:     v_name = getattr(file_ast, "name", path.stem) if file_ast else path.stem
871:     store_mode = probe.canonical_mode
872:     store_path = str(probe.canonical_path) if probe.canonical_path else None
873: 
874:     declared_kinds, declared_observers, cadence_ticks, strict, is_aggregate = _inspection_fields(
875:         file_ast
876:     )
877:     local_combine, local_discover = _inspection_topology(file_ast)
878: 
879:     return DeclarationInspectionResult(
880:         target_path=str(path),
881:         name=v_name,
882:         status=decl_status or "uninitialized",
883:         local_status=decl_status if syntax_valid else "syntax-invalid",
884:         effective_status=decl_status,
885:         store_mode=store_mode,
886:         store_path=store_path,
887:         declared_kinds=declared_kinds,
888:         declared_observers=declared_observers,
889:         cadence_ticks=cadence_ticks,
890:         strict=strict,
891:         is_aggregate=is_aggregate,
892:         local_combine=local_combine,
893:         local_discover=local_discover,
894:         syntax_valid=syntax_valid,
895:         errors=errors,
896:     )
897: 
898: 
899: def _frozen_local_aggregate_inspection(
900:     path: Path, ast: Any
901: ) -> DeclarationInspectionResult:
902:     """Return storeless aggregate topology from one retained local AST."""
903:     declared_kinds, declared_observers, cadence_ticks, strict, is_aggregate = (
904:         _inspection_fields(ast)
905:     )
906:     combine, discover = _inspection_topology(ast)
907:     fingerprint = _document_fingerprint(ast)
908:     return DeclarationInspectionResult(
909:         read_path="local-frozen",
910:         target_path=str(path),
911:         name=ast.name,
912:         status="local-only",
913:         local_status="frozen-local",
914:         effective_status="local-only",
915:         local_fingerprint=fingerprint,
916:         effective_fingerprint=fingerprint,
917:         declared_kinds=declared_kinds,
918:         declared_observers=declared_observers,
919:         cadence_ticks=cadence_ticks,
920:         strict=strict,
921:         is_aggregate=is_aggregate,
922:         local_combine=combine,
923:         local_discover=discover,
924:         effective_combine=None if combine is None else [dict(entry) for entry in combine],
925:         effective_discover=discover,
926:         syntax_valid=True,
927:         errors=[],
928:     )
929: 
930: 
931: def _inspection_fields(ast: Any | None) -> tuple[list[str], list[str], list[str], bool, bool]:
932:     """Extract public declaration fields from one already-chosen AST."""
933:     if ast is None:
934:         return [], [], [], False, False
935:     return (
936:         sorted(ast.loops.keys()) if getattr(ast, "loops", None) else [],
937:         (
938:             sorted(observer.name for observer in ast.observers)
939:             if getattr(ast, "observers", None)
940:             else []
941:         ),
942:         sorted(tick.name for tick in ast.cadence) if getattr(ast, "cadence", None) else [],
943:         getattr(ast, "strict", False),
944:         getattr(ast, "combine", None) is not None or getattr(ast, "discover", None) is not None,
945:     )
946: 
947: 
948: def _inspection_topology(
949:     ast: Any | None,
950: ) -> tuple[list[dict[str, str | None]] | None, str | None]:
951:     """Serialize only one declaration root's unexpanded aggregate shape."""
952:     if ast is None:
953:         return None, None
954:     combine = getattr(ast, "combine", None)
955:     return (
956:         None
957:         if combine is None
958:         else [{"name": entry.name, "alias": entry.alias} for entry in combine],
959:         getattr(ast, "discover", None),
960:     )
961: 
962: 
963: def _document_fingerprint(ast: Any) -> str:
964:     """Hash semantic declaration documents, deliberately excluding residence."""
965:     from lang import vertex_to_documents
966: 
967:     documents = vertex_to_documents(ast)
968:     canonical = json.dumps(
969:         [document.as_json() for document in documents],
970:         sort_keys=True,
971:         separators=(",", ":"),
972:     )
973:     return "sha256:" + hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()

===== libs/sdk/src/sdk/emit.py =====
1: """Headless fact and batch emission operations over Loops vertices."""

6: from datetime import UTC, datetime
7: from pathlib import Path
8: from typing import Any

10: from atoms import Fact
11: from custody.binding import MappedCredentialProvider as _MappedCredentialProvider
12: from custody.signing import arrival_signer_for, fact_signer_for, tick_signer_for
13: from engine.admission import AdmissionError, UndeclaredKind, grant_for_observer
14: from engine.arrival_registry import BackendRegistry
15: from engine.credentials import CredentialProvider, WriteCredentials
16: from engine.declaration import load_declaration_status
17: from engine.handle import ReceiveCommittedError, open_vertex

47: 
48: 
49: def _tick_fields(tick: Any, change: Any) -> tuple[str | None, str | None]:
50:     """Resolve (tick_mark, tick_id) for a receipt whose write fired a boundary.
51: 
52:     The in-memory engine ``Tick`` carries no id — the store assigns one on the
53:     tick row, surfaced as ``TickEvent.tick_id`` in the post-write ``ChangeBatch``.
54:     Reading ``tick.id`` off the Tick object was an AttributeError on every
55:     boundary-firing emission.
56:     """
57:     if tick is None:
58:         return None, None
59:     tick_id: str | None = None
60:     if change is not None and change.ticks:
61:         matching = [ev for ev in change.ticks if ev.name == tick.name]
62:         if matching:
63:             tick_id = matching[-1].tick_id
64:     return tick.name, tick_id
65: 
66: 
67: def _fold_preview(
68:     declaration: Any, kind: str, payload: Mapping[str, Any]
69: ) -> tuple[bool, str | None, bool, Any | None]:
70:     """Describe the existing SDK fold fields from one effective declaration."""
71:     kind_declared = kind in declaration.loops
72:     fold_key_field: str | None = None
73:     fold_key_present = True
74:     fold_key_value: Any | None = None
75:     if kind_declared:
76:         for fold in declaration.loops[kind].folds:
77:             if isinstance(fold.op, FoldBy):
78:                 fold_key_field = fold.op.key_field
79:                 fold_key_present = fold_key_field in payload
80:                 fold_key_value = payload.get(fold_key_field)
81:                 break
82:     return kind_declared, fold_key_field, fold_key_present, fold_key_value
83: 
84: 
85: def _preparation_refusal_reason(refusal: Any) -> str:
86:     """Preserve the established strict-kind message from snapshot evidence."""
87:     effective = refusal.effective_declaration
88:     fact = refusal.fact
89:     if isinstance(refusal.cause, UndeclaredKind):
90:         return f"vertex {effective.name!r} declares strict — kind {fact.kind!r} is not declared"
91:     return str(refusal.cause)
92: 
93: 
94: class CustodyCredentialProvider:
95:     """Bridge custody's disk keypair management to engine's CredentialProvider interface."""
96: 
97:     def __init__(self, key_dir: Path | None = None) -> None:
98:         if key_dir is not None:
99:             raise SdkValueError(
100:                 "CustodyCredentialProvider key_dir overrides are not supported"
101:             )
102: 
103:     def for_write(self, vertex: Path) -> WriteCredentials:
104:         """Construct operation-fresh WriteCredentials using custody key resolution."""
105:         return WriteCredentials(
106:             tick_signer=tick_signer_for(vertex),
107:             fact_signer=fact_signer_for(vertex),
108:             arrival_signer=arrival_signer_for(vertex),
109:         )
110: 
111: 
112: class MappedCredentialProvider(_MappedCredentialProvider):
113:     """SDK export for explicit namespace-scoped, persistent custody bindings."""
114: 
115:     def __init__(
116:         self,
117:         root: Path | str,
118:         *,
119:         namespace: str,
120:         receipt_observer: str | None = None,
121:     ) -> None:
122:         try:
123:             super().__init__(root, namespace=namespace, receipt_observer=receipt_observer)
124:         except ValueError as exc:
125:             raise SdkValueError(str(exc)) from exc
126: 
127: 
128: def _mapped_credentials(
129:     value: CredentialProvider | WriteCredentials | None, *, vertex: Path | None = None
130: ) -> bool:
131:     """Mapped credentials cannot silently route through legacy store writers."""
132:     if value is None:
133:         return False
134:     if bool(getattr(value, "mapped", False)):
135:         return True
136:     if isinstance(value, WriteCredentials):
137:         return value.mapped
138:     provider = getattr(value, "for_write", None)
139:     if vertex is None or not callable(provider):
140:         return False
141:     produced = provider(vertex)
142:     if not isinstance(produced, WriteCredentials):
143:         raise SdkValueError("credential provider did not return WriteCredentials")
144:     return produced.mapped
145: 
146: 
147: def _refuse_legacy_mapped_credentials(
148:     value: CredentialProvider | WriteCredentials | None, *, vertex: Path
149: ) -> None:
150:     if _mapped_credentials(value, vertex=vertex):
151:         raise TargetUnsupported(
152:             "mapped credentials require an explicit Arrival descriptor target"
153:         )
154: 
155: 
156: def preview_emission(
157:     target: Path | str,
158:     kind_or_fact: str | Fact,
159:     payload: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
160:     *,
161:     observer: str | None = None,
162:     origin: str = "",
163:     ts: float | None = None,
164:     id_override: str | None = None,
165:     admit_undeclared: bool = False,
166:     credentials: CredentialProvider | None = None,
167:     registry: BackendRegistry | None = None,
168: ) -> EmitPreviewResult:
169:     """Preflight simulation of an emission request against declared admission and fold policies.
170: 
171:     Parameters:
172:         target: Path to the .vertex file.
173:         kind_or_fact: Kind name string or complete `Fact` atom.
174:         payload: Payload dictionary (required if `kind_or_fact` is a string).
175:         observer: Identity emitting the observation.
176:         origin: Provenance identifier string.
177:         ts: Timestamp (defaults to current UTC time).
178:         admit_undeclared: Whether to simulate emission bypassing strict kind admission.
179: 
180:     Returns:
181:         EmitPreviewResult detailing admission, predicted storage, and fold requirements.
182:     """
183:     target_path = Path(target).resolve()
184:     arrival = _arrival_descriptor(target_path)
185:     if arrival is None:
186:         _refuse_legacy_mapped_credentials(credentials, vertex=target_path)
187: 
188:     if isinstance(kind_or_fact, Fact):
189:         fact = kind_or_fact
190:         actual_kind = fact.kind
191:         actual_observer = fact.observer
192:         actual_origin = fact.origin
193:         actual_ts = fact.ts
194:         actual_payload = dict(fact.payload) if fact.payload else {}
195:     else:
196:         if observer is None:
197:             raise InvalidEmissionRequest("observer is required when previewing by kind name")
198:         actual_kind = kind_or_fact
199:         actual_observer = observer
200:         actual_origin = origin
201:         actual_ts = ts if ts is not None else datetime.now(UTC).timestamp()
202:         actual_payload = payload if payload is not None else {}
203: 
204:     if arrival is not None:
205:         from engine.runtime_write import (
206:             OrdinaryWritePreparationRefused,
207:             prepare_ordinary_write,
208:         )
209: 
210:         _path, locator, descriptor = arrival
211:         registry = registry or BackendRegistry.with_builtin_backends()
212:         cred_provider = credentials or CustodyCredentialProvider()
213:         prepared_fact = (
214:             fact
215:             if isinstance(kind_or_fact, Fact)
216:             else Fact(
217:                 kind=actual_kind,
218:                 ts=actual_ts,
219:                 payload=actual_payload,
220:                 observer=actual_observer,
221:                 origin=actual_origin,
222:             )
223:         )
224:         refusal: OrdinaryWritePreparationRefused | None = None
225:         try:
226:             plan = prepare_ordinary_write(
227:                 registry,
228:                 descriptor,
229:                 locator,
230:                 prepared_fact,
231:                 credentials=cred_provider.for_write(target_path),
232:                 fact_id=id_override,
233:                 admit_undeclared=admit_undeclared,
234:             )
235:             effective = plan.effective_declaration
236:             captured_head = plan.captured_head
237:         except OrdinaryWritePreparationRefused as exc:
238:             refusal = exc
239:             effective = exc.effective_declaration
240:             captured_head = exc.captured_head
241:             plan = None
242:         except SdkError:
243:             raise
244:         except Exception as exc:
245:             from .errors import normalize_exception
246: 
247:             normalized = normalize_exception(exc)
248:             if normalized is not exc:
249:                 raise normalized from exc
250:             raise EmissionFailed(f"emission preview failed: {exc}") from exc
251:         if effective is None:
252:             raise EmissionFailed("Arrival preparation returned no effective declaration")
253:         kind_declared, fold_key_field, fold_key_present, fold_key_value = _fold_preview(
254:             effective, actual_kind, actual_payload
255:         )
256:         return EmitPreviewResult(
257:             read_path="arrival",
258:             store=StoreDescriptorInfo.from_descriptor(descriptor),
259:             captured_head=captured_head,
260:             target=str(target_path),
261:             kind=actual_kind,
262:             observer=actual_observer,
263:             origin=actual_origin,
264:             ts=actual_ts,
265:             payload=actual_payload,
266:             kind_declared=kind_declared,
267:             fold_key_field=fold_key_field,
268:             fold_key_present=fold_key_present,
269:             fold_key_value=fold_key_value,
270:             admitted=refusal is None,
271:             reason=(None if refusal is None else _preparation_refusal_reason(refusal)),
272:             strict=bool(effective.strict),
273:             would_store=plan is not None and not plan.already_present,
274:             would_fold=(refusal is None and kind_declared and fold_key_present),
275:         )
276: 
277:     _refuse_arrival_aggregate_members(target_path)
278:     info = resolve_target(target)
279:     if info.target_type != "vertex":
280:         raise TargetUnsupported(
281:             f"preview_emission requires a .vertex target, got {info.target_type}"
282:         )
283: 
284:     try:
285:         ast, _status = load_declaration_status(target_path)
286:     except Exception as exc:
287:         raise EmissionFailed(f"could not load vertex declaration {target_path}: {exc}") from exc
288: 
289:     vertex_name = getattr(ast, "name", target_path.stem)
290:     try:
291:         grant_for_observer(ast, actual_observer)
292:     except AdmissionError as exc:
293:         return EmitPreviewResult(
294:             target=str(target_path),
295:             kind=actual_kind,
296:             observer=actual_observer,
297:             origin=actual_origin,
298:             ts=actual_ts,
299:             admitted=False,
300:             reason=str(exc),
301:             would_store=False,
302:             would_fold=False,
303:             fold_key_field=None,
304:             fold_key_value=None,
305:         )
306: 
307:     kind_declared = actual_kind in ast.loops
308:     strict = bool(getattr(ast, "strict", False))
309: 
310:     if strict and not kind_declared and not admit_undeclared:
311:         return EmitPreviewResult(
312:             target=str(target_path),
313:             kind=actual_kind,
314:             observer=actual_observer,
315:             origin=actual_origin,
316:             ts=actual_ts,
317:             payload=actual_payload,
318:             kind_declared=kind_declared,
319:             fold_key_field=None,
320:             fold_key_present=True,
321:             fold_key_value=None,
322:             admitted=False,
323:             reason=f"vertex {vertex_name!r} declares strict — kind {actual_kind!r} is not declared",
324:             strict=strict,
325:             would_store=False,
326:             would_fold=False,
327:         )
328: 
329:     fold_key_field: str | None = None
330:     fold_key_present = True
331:     fold_key_value: Any | None = None
332: 
333:     if kind_declared:
334:         loop_def = ast.loops[actual_kind]
335:         for f_decl in loop_def.folds:
336:             if isinstance(f_decl.op, FoldBy):
337:                 fold_key_field = f_decl.op.key_field
338:                 fold_key_present = fold_key_field in actual_payload
339:                 fold_key_value = actual_payload.get(fold_key_field)
340:                 break
341: 
342:     would_fold = kind_declared and fold_key_present
343: 
344:     return EmitPreviewResult(
345:         target=str(target_path),
346:         kind=actual_kind,
347:         observer=actual_observer,
348:         origin=actual_origin,
349:         ts=actual_ts,
350:         payload=actual_payload,
351:         kind_declared=kind_declared,
352:         fold_key_field=fold_key_field,
353:         fold_key_present=fold_key_present,
354:         fold_key_value=fold_key_value,
355:         admitted=True,
356:         reason=None,
357:         strict=strict,
358:         would_store=True,
359:         would_fold=would_fold,
360:     )
361: 
362: 
363: def emit_fact(
364:     target: Path | str,
365:     kind_or_fact: str | Fact,
366:     payload: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
367:     *,
368:     observer: str | None = None,
369:     origin: str = "",
370:     ts: float | None = None,
371:     id_override: str | None = None,
372:     credentials: CredentialProvider | None = None,
373:     admit_undeclared: bool = False,
374:     dry_run: bool = False,
375:     registry: BackendRegistry | None = None,
376: ) -> EmitReceipt:
377:     """Emit a single fact into a Loops vertex under declared admission rules.
378: 
379:     Parameters:
380:         target: Path to the target .vertex file.
381:         kind_or_fact: Kind name string or complete `Fact` atom.
382:         payload: Fact payload dictionary (required if `kind_or_fact` is a string).
383:         observer: Authorship identity emitting the fact.
384:         origin: Provenance identifier string.
385:         ts: Explicit timestamp (defaults to current UTC time).
386:         id_override: Optional deterministic fact ULID.
387:         credentials: Key provider for signing the fact.
388:         admit_undeclared: If True, bypasses strict declared-kind admission refusal.
389:         dry_run: If True, simulates emission without writing to storage.
390: 
391:     Returns:
392:         EmitReceipt containing stored fact ID, attestation, tick mark, and state delta count.
393:     """
394:     target_path = Path(target).resolve()
395:     arrival = _arrival_descriptor(target_path)
396:     if arrival is None:
397:         _refuse_legacy_mapped_credentials(credentials, vertex=target_path)
398: 
399:     if isinstance(kind_or_fact, Fact):
400:         fact = kind_or_fact
401:         actual_observer = fact.observer
402:     else:
403:         if observer is None:
404:             raise InvalidEmissionRequest("observer is required when emitting by kind name")
405:         if payload is None:
406:             raise InvalidEmissionRequest(
407:                 "payload dictionary is required when emitting by kind name"
408:             )
409: 
410:         actual_observer = observer
411:         actual_ts = ts if ts is not None else datetime.now(UTC).timestamp()
412:         fact = Fact(
413:             kind=kind_or_fact,
414:             ts=actual_ts,
415:             payload=payload,
416:             observer=actual_observer,
417:             origin=origin,
418:         )
419: 
420:     if dry_run:
421:         preview = preview_emission(
422:             target,
423:             kind_or_fact=fact,
424:             observer=actual_observer,
425:             origin=origin,
426:             ts=ts,
427:             id_override=id_override,
428:             admit_undeclared=admit_undeclared,
429:             credentials=credentials,
430:             registry=registry,
431:         )
432:         if not preview.admitted:
433:             raise AdmissionFailed(
434:                 preview.reason or "admission failed",
435:                 observer=preview.observer,
436:                 kind=preview.kind,
437:             )
438:         return EmitReceipt(
439:             write_path=preview.read_path,
440:             store=preview.store,
441:             id="",
442:             stored=False,
443:             signed=None,
444:             observer=actual_observer,
445:             tick_mark=None,
446:             tick_id=None,
447:             state_change=False,
448:             affected_sections=[preview.kind] if preview.would_fold else [],
449:             delta_count=0,
450:             predicted_state_change=preview.would_fold,
451:             captured_head=preview.captured_head,
452:             witnessed=None,
453:             projection="not-requested" if arrival is not None else None,
454:         )
455: 
456:     if arrival is not None:
457:         from engine.arrival_maintenance import sync_projection
458:         from engine.runtime_write import (
459:             OrdinaryWritePlan,
460:             OrdinaryWritePreparationRefused,
461:             execute_ordinary_write,
462:             prepare_ordinary_write,
463:         )
464: 
465:         _path, locator, descriptor = arrival
466:         registry = registry or BackendRegistry.with_builtin_backends()
467:         cred_provider = credentials or CustodyCredentialProvider()
468:         plan: OrdinaryWritePlan | None = None
469:         try:
470:             plan = prepare_ordinary_write(
471:                 registry,
472:                 descriptor,
473:                 locator,
474:                 fact,
475:                 credentials=cred_provider.for_write(target_path),
476:                 fact_id=id_override,
477:                 admit_undeclared=admit_undeclared,
478:             )
479:             outcome = execute_ordinary_write(
480:                 registry,
481:                 descriptor,
482:                 plan,
483:                 after_commit=lambda head: sync_projection(registry, descriptor, through=head),
484:             )
485:         except OrdinaryWritePreparationRefused as exc:
486:             cause = exc.cause
487:             raise AdmissionFailed(
488:                 _preparation_refusal_reason(exc),
489:                 observer=getattr(cause, "observer", actual_observer),
490:                 kind=getattr(cause, "kind", fact.kind),
491:                 vertex=getattr(cause, "vertex", exc.effective_declaration.name),
492:             ) from exc
493:         except SdkError:
494:             raise
495:         except Exception as exc:
496:             from .errors import normalize_exception
497: 
498:             context = (
499:                 None
500:                 if plan is None
501:                 else {
502:                     "captured_head": plan.captured_head,
503:                     "fact_id": plan.fact_id,
504:                     "tick_id": plan.tick_id,
505:                 }
506:             )
507:             normalized = normalize_exception(exc, context=context)
508:             if normalized is not exc:
509:                 raise normalized from exc
510:             raise EmissionFailed(f"fact emission failed: {exc}") from exc
511: 
512:         assert plan is not None
513:         fact_draft = next((draft for draft in plan.drafts if draft.kind == "fact"), None)
514:         tick_draft = next((draft for draft in plan.drafts if draft.kind == "tick"), None)
515:         return EmitReceipt(
516:             write_path="arrival",
517:             store=StoreDescriptorInfo.from_descriptor(descriptor),
518:             id=outcome.fact_id,
519:             stored=not plan.already_present,
520:             signed=(None if fact_draft is None else fact_draft.body.get("signature") is not None),
521:             observer=actual_observer,
522:             tick_mark=(None if tick_draft is None else str(tick_draft.body["name"])),
523:             tick_id=outcome.tick_id,
524:             state_change=None,
525:             affected_sections=[],
526:             delta_count=None,
527:             predicted_state_change=False,
528:             captured_head=plan.captured_head,
529:             commit=outcome.commit,
530:             witnessed=None if plan.already_present else True,
531:             projection=outcome.projection.value,
532:         )
533: 
534:     _refuse_arrival_aggregate_members(target_path)
535:     info = resolve_target(target)
536:     if info.target_type != "vertex":
537:         raise TargetUnsupported(f"emit_fact requires a .vertex target, got {info.target_type}")
538: 
539:     cred_provider = credentials or CustodyCredentialProvider()
540:     try:
541:         handle = open_vertex(
542:             target_path,
543:             credentials=cred_provider,
544:         )
545:     except Exception as exc:
546:         raise EmissionFailed(f"could not open vertex {target_path}: {exc}") from exc
547: 
548:     try:
549:         result = handle.receive_as(
550:             fact,
551:             id_override=id_override,
552:             admit_undeclared=admit_undeclared,
553:         )
554:         receipt = result.receipt
555: 
556:         signed = receipt.attestation.signed if receipt.attestation is not None else None
557:         tick_mark, tick_id = _tick_fields(receipt.tick, result.change)
558: 
559:         delta_count = 0
560:         affected_sections: list[str] = []
561:         if result.change is not None:
562:             delta_count = len(result.change.rows)
563:             sections = {r.address.kind for r in result.change.rows if r.address.kind}
564:             affected_sections = sorted(sections)
565:             state_change = delta_count > 0 or len(result.change.receipts) > 0
566:         else:
567:             state_change = False
568: 
569:         return EmitReceipt(
570:             id=receipt.fact_id or "",
571:             stored=receipt.stored,
572:             signed=signed,
573:             observer=actual_observer,
574:             tick_mark=tick_mark,
575:             tick_id=tick_id,
576:             state_change=state_change,
577:             affected_sections=affected_sections,
578:             delta_count=delta_count,
579:             predicted_state_change=False,
580:         )
581:     except AdmissionError as exc:
582:         obs = getattr(exc, "observer", actual_observer)
583:         k = getattr(exc, "kind", getattr(fact, "kind", None))
584:         v = getattr(exc, "vertex", None)
585:         raise AdmissionFailed(str(exc), observer=obs, kind=k, vertex=v) from exc
586:     except ReceiveCommittedError as exc:
587:         raise CommittedEmissionError(str(exc), fact_id=exc.fact_id) from exc
588:     except SdkValueError:
589:         raise
590:     except Exception as exc:
591:         raise EmissionFailed(f"fact emission failed: {exc}") from exc
592:     finally:
593:         handle.close()
594: 
595: 
596: def emit_batch(
597:     target: Path | str,
598:     facts: list[
599:         Fact | tuple[str, dict[str, Any]] | tuple[str, dict[str, Any], float] | dict[str, Any]
600:     ],
601:     *,
602:     observer: str | None = None,
603:     origin: str = "",
604:     credentials: CredentialProvider | None = None,
605:     admit_undeclared: bool = False,
606:     registry: BackendRegistry | None = None,
607: ) -> BatchEmitResult:
608:     """Emit multiple facts and return explicit batch atomicity evidence.
609: 
610:     Parameters:
611:         target: Path to the target .vertex file.
612:         facts: List of Fact atoms, (kind, payload) tuples, or fact dict mappings.
613:         observer: Default observer identity for items omitting one.
614:         origin: Default provenance origin for items omitting one.
615:         credentials: Key provider for signing emissions.
616:         admit_undeclared: Explicit default for items without their own mapping flag.
617: 
618:     Returns:
619:         One uniform result containing ordered item receipts and any shared commit.
620:     """
621:     prepared_items: list[tuple[Fact, str | None, bool]] = []
622:     for item in facts:
623:         item_id_override = None
624:         item_admit_undeclared = admit_undeclared
625:         if isinstance(item, Fact):
626:             f = item
627:         elif isinstance(item, tuple):
628:             if not observer:
629:                 raise InvalidEmissionRequest(
630:                     "observer is required when passing (kind, payload) tuples"
631:                 )
632:             if len(item) == 2:
633:                 k, p = item
634:                 f = Fact(
635:                     kind=k,
636:                     ts=datetime.now(UTC).timestamp(),
637:                     payload=p,
638:                     observer=observer,
639:                     origin=origin,
640:                 )
641:             elif len(item) == 3:
642:                 k, p, item_ts = item
643:                 f = Fact(
644:                     kind=k,
645:                     ts=item_ts,
646:                     payload=p,
647:                     observer=observer,
648:                     origin=origin,
649:                 )
650:             else:
651:                 raise InvalidEmissionRequest(f"unsupported batch fact item shape: {item}")
652:         elif isinstance(item, Mapping):
653:             k = item.get("kind")
654:             if not k:
655:                 raise InvalidEmissionRequest(f"batch item dict missing 'kind': {item}")
656:             p = dict(item.get("payload", {}))
657:             item_obs = (
658:                 str(item["observer"]) if "observer" in item and item["observer"] else observer
659:             )
660:             if not item_obs:
661:                 raise InvalidEmissionRequest("observer is required for dict fact")
662:             item_origin = str(item.get("origin", origin))
663:             item_ts = (
664:                 float(item["ts"])
665:                 if "ts" in item and item["ts"] is not None
666:                 else datetime.now(UTC).timestamp()
667:             )
668:             if "id" in item and item["id"]:
669:                 item_id_override = str(item["id"])
670:             if "admit_undeclared" in item:
671:                 explicit_admit = item["admit_undeclared"]
672:                 if not isinstance(explicit_admit, bool):
673:                     raise InvalidEmissionRequest("batch item 'admit_undeclared' must be a boolean")
674:                 item_admit_undeclared = explicit_admit
675:             f = Fact(
676:                 kind=k,
677:                 ts=item_ts,
678:                 payload=p,
679:                 observer=item_obs,
680:                 origin=item_origin,
681:             )
682:         else:
683:             raise InvalidEmissionRequest(f"unsupported batch fact item shape: {item}")
684:         prepared_items.append((f, item_id_override, item_admit_undeclared))
685: 
686:     target_path = Path(target).resolve()
687:     arrival = _arrival_descriptor(target_path)
688:     if arrival is None:
689:         _refuse_legacy_mapped_credentials(credentials, vertex=target_path)
690:     if arrival is not None:
691:         from engine.arrival_contract import NotAuthority, Profile
692:         from engine.arrival_maintenance import sync_projection
693:         from engine.runtime_write import (
694:             BatchFactInput,
695:             BatchWritePlan,
696:             execute_batch_write,
697:             prepare_batch_write,
698:         )
699: 
700:         _path, locator, descriptor = arrival
701:         if descriptor.role is not Profile.AUTHORITY:
702:             from .errors import normalize_exception
703: 
704:             refused = normalize_exception(
705:                 NotAuthority("batch writes require an Authority descriptor role")
706:             )
707:             raise refused
708:         store = StoreDescriptorInfo.from_descriptor(descriptor)
709:         if not prepared_items:
710:             return BatchEmitResult(
711:                 write_path="arrival",
712:                 store=store,
713:                 atomic=True,
714:                 atomicity="empty-noop",
715:                 witnessed=None,
716:                 projection="not-requested",
717:             )
718:         registry = registry or BackendRegistry.with_builtin_backends()
719:         cred_provider = credentials or CustodyCredentialProvider()
720:         plan: BatchWritePlan | None = None
721:         try:
722:             plan = prepare_batch_write(
723:                 registry,
724:                 descriptor,
725:                 locator,
726:                 tuple(
727:                     BatchFactInput(
728:                         fact,
729:                         fact_id=item_id,
730:                         admit_undeclared=item_admit,
731:                     )
732:                     for fact, item_id, item_admit in prepared_items
733:                 ),
734:                 credentials=cred_provider.for_write(target_path),
735:             )
736:             outcome = execute_batch_write(
737:                 registry,
738:                 descriptor,
739:                 plan,
740:                 after_commit=lambda head: sync_projection(registry, descriptor, through=head),
741:             )
742:         except SdkError:
743:             raise
744:         except Exception as exc:
745:             from .errors import normalize_exception
746: 
747:             context = None
748:             if plan is not None:
749:                 context = {
750:                     "captured_head": plan.captured_head,
751:                     "fact_ids": tuple(item.fact_id for item in plan.items),
752:                     "tick_ids": tuple(
753:                         item.tick_id for item in plan.items if item.tick_id is not None
754:                     ),
755:                     "items": plan.items,
756:                 }
757:             normalized = normalize_exception(exc, context=context)
758:             if normalized is not exc:
759:                 raise normalized from exc
760:             raise EmissionFailed(f"batch emission failed: {exc}") from exc
761: 
762:         assert plan is not None
763:         signed_by_id: dict[str, bool] = {}
764:         for draft in plan.drafts:
765:             if draft.kind == "fact":
766:                 signed_by_id[str(draft.body["id"])] = draft.body.get("signature") is not None
767:             elif draft.kind == "batch":
768:                 for row in draft.body["rows"]:
769:                     signed_by_id[str(row["id"])] = row.get("signature") is not None
770:         item_receipts = [
771:             EmitReceipt(
772:                 write_path="arrival",
773:                 store=store,
774:                 id=item_result.fact_id,
775:                 stored=not item_result.already_present,
776:                 signed=signed_by_id.get(item_result.fact_id),
777:                 observer=prepared[0].observer,
778:                 tick_mark=item_result.tick_name,
779:                 tick_id=item_result.tick_id,
780:                 state_change=None,
781:                 affected_sections=[],
782:                 delta_count=None,
783:                 predicted_state_change=False,
784:                 captured_head=plan.captured_head,
785:                 commit=None,
786:                 witnessed=None if item_result.already_present else True,
787:                 projection=(
788:                     "not-requested" if item_result.already_present else outcome.projection.value
789:                 ),
790:             )
791:             for prepared, item_result in zip(prepared_items, outcome.items, strict=True)
792:         ]
793:         return BatchEmitResult(
794:             write_path="arrival",
795:             store=store,
796:             items=item_receipts,
797:             atomic=True,
798:             atomicity=("idempotent-noop" if outcome.commit is None else "single-append"),
799:             captured_head=plan.captured_head,
800:             commit=outcome.commit,
801:             witnessed=None if outcome.commit is None else True,
802:             projection=outcome.projection.value,
803:         )
804: 
805:     _refuse_arrival_aggregate_members(target_path)
806:     info = resolve_target(target)
807:     if info.target_type != "vertex":
808:         raise TargetUnsupported(f"emit_batch requires a .vertex target, got {info.target_type}")
809: 
810:     if not prepared_items:
811:         return BatchEmitResult(
812:             write_path="legacy",
813:             atomic=True,
814:             atomicity="empty-noop",
815:         )
816: 
817:     cred_provider = credentials or CustodyCredentialProvider()
818:     try:
819:         handle = open_vertex(
820:             target_path,
821:             credentials=cred_provider,
822:         )
823:     except Exception as exc:
824:         raise EmissionFailed(f"could not open vertex {target_path}: {exc}") from exc
825: 
826:     receipts: list[EmitReceipt] = []
827:     try:
828:         for index, (f, item_id_override, item_admit) in enumerate(prepared_items):
829:             try:
830:                 result = handle.receive_as(
831:                     f,
832:                     id_override=item_id_override,
833:                     admit_undeclared=item_admit,
834:                 )
835:                 r = result.receipt
836:                 signed = r.attestation.signed if r.attestation is not None else None
837:                 tick_mark, tick_id = _tick_fields(r.tick, result.change)
838: 
839:                 delta_count = 0
840:                 affected_sections: list[str] = []
841:                 if result.change is not None:
842:                     delta_count = len(result.change.rows)
843:                     sections = {row.address.kind for row in result.change.rows if row.address.kind}
844:                     affected_sections = sorted(sections)
845:                     state_change = delta_count > 0 or len(result.change.receipts) > 0
846:                 else:
847:                     state_change = False
848: 
849:                 receipts.append(
850:                     EmitReceipt(
851:                         id=r.fact_id or "",
852:                         stored=r.stored,
853:                         signed=signed,
854:                         observer=f.observer,
855:                         tick_mark=tick_mark,
856:                         tick_id=tick_id,
857:                         state_change=state_change,
858:                         affected_sections=affected_sections,
859:                         delta_count=delta_count,
860:                         predicted_state_change=False,
861:                     )
862:                 )
863:             except AdmissionError as exc:
864:                 failure: BaseException = AdmissionFailed(
865:                     str(exc),
866:                     observer=getattr(exc, "observer", observer),
867:                     kind=getattr(exc, "kind", None),
868:                     vertex=getattr(exc, "vertex", None),
869:                 )
870:             except ReceiveCommittedError as exc:
871:                 failure = CommittedEmissionError(str(exc), fact_id=exc.fact_id)
872:             except SdkValueError as exc:
873:                 failure = exc
874:             except Exception as exc:
875:                 failure = EmissionFailed(f"batch emission failed: {exc}")
876:             else:
877:                 continue
878: 
879:             committed_fact_id = (
880:                 failure.fact_id if isinstance(failure, CommittedEmissionError) else None
881:             )
882:             if receipts or committed_fact_id is not None:
883:                 raise LegacyBatchPartialFailure(
884:                     "legacy sequential batch stopped after an observable prefix; "
885:                     "inspect retained item receipts before retrying",
886:                     failed_index=index,
887:                     items=receipts,
888:                     cause=failure,
889:                     committed_fact_id=committed_fact_id,
890:                 ) from failure
891:             raise failure
892:         return BatchEmitResult(
893:             write_path="legacy",
894:             items=receipts,
895:             atomic=False,
896:             atomicity="legacy-sequential",
897:         )
898:     finally:
899:         handle.close()

===== libs/sdk/src/sdk/kind.py =====
1: """Headless declaration mutation operations over Loops vertices."""

48: def _default_loop_def() -> LoopDef:
49:     """Least presumptive default: items collect 100."""
50:     return LoopDef(folds=(FoldDecl("items", FoldCollect(100)),))

53: def _arrival_descriptor_for(vertex_path: Path):
54:     from .target import _arrival_descriptor
55: 
56:     return _arrival_descriptor(vertex_path)

59: def _legacy_result(result: KindMutationResult):
60:     from .declare import DeclarationEditResult
61: 
62:     return DeclarationEditResult(
63:         status=result.status,
64:         reason=result.reason,
65:         mode=result.mode,
66:         vertex_path=result.vertex_path,
67:         target_path=result.vertex_path,
68:         read_path="legacy",
69:         generation_before=result.generation_before,
70:         generation_after=result.generation_after,
71:         changes=result.changes,
72:         file_written=result.file_written,
73:     )
