76: def _arrival_semantic_preview(
77:     vertex_path: Path,
78:     proposed_text: str,
79:     *,
80:     registry: Any | None,
81: ) -> DeclarationPlanResult:
82:     """Preview a splice only after confirming the cache matches CURRENT."""
83:     from engine.arrival_boundary_continuity import (
84:         BoundaryContinuityConflict,
85:         analyze_boundary_continuity,
86:         collect_verified_parameter_rows,
87:     )
88:     from engine.arrival_contract import TickRequest
89:     from engine.arrival_declarations import DeclarationPreparationRefused
90:     from engine.declaration import validate_arrival_runtime_identity
91:     from lang import diff_documents, parse_vertex, validate_vertex, vertex_to_documents
92: 
93:     from .declare import DeclarationPreviewResult
94:     from .errors import normalize_exception
95:     from .read import _arrival_declaration, _open_arrival_read
96: 
97:     resolved = _arrival_descriptor_for(vertex_path)
98:     if resolved is None:
99:         raise TargetUnsupported(f"not an explicit Arrival target: {vertex_path}")
100:     _path, locator_ast, descriptor = resolved
101:     active = registry or BackendRegistry.with_builtin_backends()
102:     try:
103:         local_ast = parse_vertex(vertex_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"), path=vertex_path)
104:         with _open_arrival_read(resolved, registry=active) as (_locator, _descriptor, opened):
105:             effective_ast, _status, _facts, _lineage = _arrival_declaration(
106:                 locator_ast, vertex_path, opened
107:             )
108:             local_docs = [doc.as_json() for doc in vertex_to_documents(local_ast)]
109:             effective_docs = [doc.as_json() for doc in vertex_to_documents(effective_ast)]
110:             if local_docs != effective_docs:
111:                 return DeclarationPreviewResult(
112:                     applicable=False,
113:                     reason="declaration cache differs from CURRENT Arrival history",
114:                     mode="arrival-semantic-preview",
115:                     vertex_path=str(vertex_path),
116:                     generation_before={"captured_head": opened.basis.captured_head},
117:                     changes=[],
118:                     store=StoreDescriptorInfo.from_descriptor(descriptor),
119:                     basis=asdict(opened.basis),
120:                     captured_head={
121:                         "lineage": opened.basis.captured_head.lineage,
122:                         "ordinal": opened.basis.captured_head.ordinal,
123:                         "record_hash": opened.basis.captured_head.record_hash,
124:                     },
125:                     lineage=opened.basis.lineage,
126:                 )
127:             proposed_ast = parse_vertex(proposed_text, path=vertex_path)
128:             validate_vertex(proposed_ast)
129:             validate_arrival_runtime_identity(
130:                 proposed_ast.name, proposed_ast.loops, refusal=DeclarationPreparationRefused
131:             )
132:             proposed_docs = [
133:                 doc.as_json() for doc in vertex_to_documents(proposed_ast)
134:             ]
135:             changes = diff_documents(local_docs, proposed_docs)
136:             try:
137:                 verified_params = collect_verified_parameter_rows(
138:                     opened.snapshot.declaration_anchor,
139:                     _facts,
140:                     target_documents=proposed_docs,
141:                     base_dir=vertex_path.parent,
142:                 )
143:                 analyze_boundary_continuity(
144:                     opened.snapshot.declaration_anchor,
145:                     _facts,
146:                     opened.snapshot.ticks(TickRequest(since=float("-inf"))),
147:                     target_documents=proposed_docs,
148:                     verified_params=verified_params,
149:                 )
150:             except BoundaryContinuityConflict as conflict:
151:                 refusal = DeclarationPreparationRefused(
152:                     f"boundary continuity refuses declaration preview: {conflict}",
153:                     coordinator_phase="prepare",
154:                     effects={
155:                         "custody": {
156:                             "attempt": "not-entered",
157:                             "state": "not-attempted",
158:                         }
159:                     },
160:                     captured_head=opened.basis.captured_head,
161:                 )
162:                 raise refusal from conflict
163:             return DeclarationPreviewResult(
164:                 applicable=True,
165:                 reason="semantic preview only; signed execution requires observer credentials",
166:                 mode="arrival-semantic-preview",
167:                 vertex_path=str(vertex_path),
168:                 generation_before={"captured_head": opened.basis.captured_head},
169:                 changes=[c.as_dict() if hasattr(c, "as_dict") else str(c) for c in changes],
170:                 store=StoreDescriptorInfo.from_descriptor(descriptor),
171:                 basis=asdict(opened.basis),
172:                 captured_head={
173:                     "lineage": opened.basis.captured_head.lineage,
174:                     "ordinal": opened.basis.captured_head.ordinal,
175:                     "record_hash": opened.basis.captured_head.record_hash,
176:                 },
177:                 lineage=opened.basis.lineage,
178:             )
179:     except Exception as exc:
180:         normalized = normalize_exception(exc)
181:         if normalized is exc:
182:             raise
183:         raise normalized from exc
184: 
185: 
186: def plan_kind_mutation(
187:     target: Path | str,
188:     op: str,
189:     kind_name: str,
190:     definition: LoopDef | None = None,
191:     *,
192:     registry: BackendRegistry | None = None,
193: ) -> DeclarationPlanResult:
194:     """Dry-run preview of a proposed kind declaration mutation.
195: 
196:     Parameters:
197:         target: Path to the .vertex file.
198:         op: Operation type ('add', 'edit', or 'remove').
199:         kind_name: The name of the kind to mutate.
200:         definition: LoopDef AST node (for add/edit).
201: 
202:     Returns:
203:         DeclarationPlanResult with proposed changes and applicability status.
204:     """
205:     vertex_path = Path(target).resolve()
206:     if _arrival_descriptor_for(vertex_path) is not None:
207:         from .declare import DeclarationPreviewResult
208: 
209:         loop_def = definition or _default_loop_def()
210:         current_text = vertex_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
211:         try:
212:             if op == "add":
213:                 new_text = add_vertex_kind(current_text, kind_name, loop_def)
214:             elif op == "edit":
215:                 new_text = edit_vertex_kind(current_text, kind_name, loop_def)
216:             elif op == "remove":
217:                 new_text = remove_vertex_kind(current_text, kind_name)
218:             else:
219:                 raise SdkValueError(
220:                     f"unsupported mutation op '{op}', expected 'add', 'edit', or 'remove'"
221:                 )
222:         except SdkValueError:
223:             raise
224:         except ValueError as exc:
225:             return DeclarationPreviewResult(
226:                 applicable=False, reason=str(exc), mode="refused",
227:                 vertex_path=str(vertex_path), generation_before=None, changes=[],
228:             )
229:         return _arrival_semantic_preview(vertex_path, new_text, registry=registry)
230: 
231:     info = resolve_target(target)
232:     if info.target_type != "vertex":
233:         raise TargetUnsupported(
234:             f"plan_kind_mutation requires a .vertex target, got {info.target_type}"
235:         )
236: 
237:     vertex_path = Path(target).resolve()
238:     current_text = vertex_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
239:     loop_def = definition or _default_loop_def()
240: 
241:     try:
242:         if op == "add":
243:             new_text = add_vertex_kind(current_text, kind_name, loop_def)
244:         elif op == "edit":
245:             new_text = edit_vertex_kind(current_text, kind_name, loop_def)
246:         elif op == "remove":
247:             new_text = remove_vertex_kind(current_text, kind_name)
248:         else:
249:             raise SdkValueError(
250:                 f"unsupported mutation op '{op}', expected 'add', 'edit', or 'remove'"
251:             )
252:     except SdkValueError:
253:         raise
254:     except ValueError as exc:
255:         # lang refused the mutation (duplicate add, missing edit/remove target, ...).
256:         # A plan is a preview of applicability, so a describable refusal is a
257:         # non-applicable plan, not an exception — the apply paths raise CeremonyFailed.
258:         return DeclarationPlanResult(
259:             applicable=False,
260:             reason=str(exc),
261:             mode="refused",
262:             vertex_path=str(vertex_path),
263:             generation_before=None,
264:             changes=[],
265:         )
266: 
267:     preview = plan_declaration_update(vertex_path, proposed_text=new_text)
268:     return DeclarationPlanResult(
269:         applicable=preview.applicable,
270:         reason=preview.reason,
271:         mode=preview.mode,
272:         vertex_path=str(vertex_path),
273:         generation_before=preview.generation,
274:         changes=[c.as_dict() if hasattr(c, "as_dict") else str(c) for c in preview.changes],
275:     )
276: 
277: 
278: def add_kind(
279:     target: Path | str,
280:     kind_name: str,
281:     definition: LoopDef | None = None,
282:     *,
283:     observer: str,
284:     credentials: CredentialProvider | None = None,
285:     registry: BackendRegistry | None = None,
286: ) -> KindMutationResult:
287:     """Add a new loop-kind definition to a vertex and orchestrate declaration persistence.
288: 
289:     Parameters:
290:         target: Path to the .vertex file.
291:         kind_name: The name of the kind to add.
292:         definition: LoopDef AST node (defaults to items 'collect' 100).
293:         observer: Authorship identity performing the declaration update.
294:         credentials: Key provider for signing the ceremony.
295: 
296:     Returns:
297:         KindMutationResult detailing the preview, apply status, and updated generation.
298:     """
299:     vertex_path = Path(target).resolve()
300:     arrival = _arrival_descriptor_for(vertex_path)
301:     if arrival is None:
302:         _refuse_legacy_mapped_credentials(credentials, vertex=vertex_path)
303:         info = resolve_target(target)
304:         if info.target_type != "vertex":
305:             raise TargetUnsupported(f"add_kind requires a .vertex target, got {info.target_type}")
306:     loop_def = definition or _default_loop_def()
307:     cred_provider = credentials or CustodyCredentialProvider()
308: 
309:     current_text = vertex_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
310:     try:
311:         new_text = add_vertex_kind(current_text, kind_name, loop_def)
312:     except Exception as exc:
313:         if arrival is not None:
314:             raise SdkValueError(f"could not generate Arrival kind mutation: {exc}") from exc
315:         raise CeremonyFailed(f"could not generate kind mutation: {exc}") from exc
316: 
317:     if arrival is not None:
318:         from .declare import _edit_arrival_declaration
319: 
320:         return _edit_arrival_declaration(
321:             vertex_path, new_text, observer=observer, credentials=cred_provider,
322:             registry=registry,
323:             source_cache_bytes=current_text.encode("utf-8"),
324:             require_cache_basis=True,
325:         )
326: 
327:     preview = plan_declaration_update(vertex_path, proposed_text=new_text)
328:     if not preview.applicable:
329:         raise CeremonyFailed(f"declaration update not applicable: {preview.reason}")
330: 
331:     result = apply_declaration_update(preview, observer=observer, credentials=cred_provider)
332:     if result.status not in ("applied", "noop"):
333:         raise CeremonyFailed(f"declaration update failed ({result.status}): {result.reason}")
334: 
335:     from engine.declaration import declaration_generation
336: 
337:     gen_after = declaration_generation(vertex_path)
338: 
339:     return _legacy_result(KindMutationResult(
340:         status=result.status,
341:         reason=result.reason,
342:         mode=preview.mode,
343:         vertex_path=str(vertex_path),
344:         generation_before=preview.generation,
345:         generation_after=gen_after,
346:         changes=[c.as_dict() if hasattr(c, "as_dict") else str(c) for c in preview.changes],
347:         file_written=result.file_written,
348:     ))
349: 
350: 
351: def edit_kind(
352:     target: Path | str,
353:     kind_name: str,
354:     definition: LoopDef,
355:     *,
356:     observer: str,
357:     credentials: CredentialProvider | None = None,
358:     registry: BackendRegistry | None = None,
359: ) -> KindMutationResult:
360:     """Edit an existing loop-kind definition in a vertex and orchestrate declaration persistence."""
361:     vertex_path = Path(target).resolve()
362:     arrival = _arrival_descriptor_for(vertex_path)
363:     if arrival is None:
364:         _refuse_legacy_mapped_credentials(credentials, vertex=vertex_path)
365:         info = resolve_target(target)
366:         if info.target_type != "vertex":
367:             raise TargetUnsupported(f"edit_kind requires a .vertex target, got {info.target_type}")
368:     cred_provider = credentials or CustodyCredentialProvider()
369: 
370:     current_text = vertex_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
371:     try:
372:         new_text = edit_vertex_kind(current_text, kind_name, definition)
373:     except Exception as exc:
374:         if arrival is not None:
375:             raise SdkValueError(f"could not generate Arrival kind mutation: {exc}") from exc
376:         raise CeremonyFailed(f"could not generate kind mutation: {exc}") from exc
377: 
378:     if arrival is not None:
379:         from .declare import _edit_arrival_declaration
380: 
381:         return _edit_arrival_declaration(
382:             vertex_path, new_text, observer=observer, credentials=cred_provider,
383:             registry=registry,
384:             source_cache_bytes=current_text.encode("utf-8"),
385:             require_cache_basis=True,
386:         )
387: 
388:     preview = plan_declaration_update(vertex_path, proposed_text=new_text)
389:     if not preview.applicable:
390:         raise CeremonyFailed(f"declaration update not applicable: {preview.reason}")
391: 
392:     result = apply_declaration_update(preview, observer=observer, credentials=cred_provider)
393:     if result.status not in ("applied", "noop"):
394:         raise CeremonyFailed(f"declaration update failed ({result.status}): {result.reason}")
395: 
396:     from engine.declaration import declaration_generation
397: 
398:     gen_after = declaration_generation(vertex_path)
399: 
400:     return _legacy_result(KindMutationResult(
401:         status=result.status,
402:         reason=result.reason,
403:         mode=preview.mode,
404:         vertex_path=str(vertex_path),
405:         generation_before=preview.generation,
406:         generation_after=gen_after,
407:         changes=[c.as_dict() if hasattr(c, "as_dict") else str(c) for c in preview.changes],
408:         file_written=result.file_written,
409:     ))
410: 
411: 
412: def remove_kind(
413:     target: Path | str,
414:     kind_name: str,
415:     *,
416:     observer: str,
417:     credentials: CredentialProvider | None = None,
418:     registry: BackendRegistry | None = None,
419: ) -> KindMutationResult:
420:     """Remove a loop-kind definition from a vertex and orchestrate declaration persistence."""
421:     vertex_path = Path(target).resolve()
422:     arrival = _arrival_descriptor_for(vertex_path)
423:     if arrival is None:
424:         _refuse_legacy_mapped_credentials(credentials, vertex=vertex_path)
425:         info = resolve_target(target)
426:         if info.target_type != "vertex":
427:             raise TargetUnsupported(
428:                 f"remove_kind requires a .vertex target, got {info.target_type}"
429:             )
430:     cred_provider = credentials or CustodyCredentialProvider()
431: 
432:     current_text = vertex_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
433:     try:
434:         new_text = remove_vertex_kind(current_text, kind_name)
435:     except Exception as exc:
436:         if arrival is not None:
437:             raise SdkValueError(f"could not generate Arrival kind mutation: {exc}") from exc
438:         raise CeremonyFailed(f"could not generate kind mutation: {exc}") from exc
439: 
440:     if arrival is not None:
441:         from .declare import _edit_arrival_declaration
442: 
443:         return _edit_arrival_declaration(
444:             vertex_path, new_text, observer=observer, credentials=cred_provider,
445:             registry=registry,
446:             source_cache_bytes=current_text.encode("utf-8"),
447:             require_cache_basis=True,
448:         )
449: 
450:     preview = plan_declaration_update(vertex_path, proposed_text=new_text)
451:     if not preview.applicable:
452:         raise CeremonyFailed(f"declaration update not applicable: {preview.reason}")
453: 
454:     result = apply_declaration_update(preview, observer=observer, credentials=cred_provider)
455:     if result.status not in ("applied", "noop"):
456:         raise CeremonyFailed(f"declaration update failed ({result.status}): {result.reason}")
457: 
458:     from engine.declaration import declaration_generation
459: 
460:     gen_after = declaration_generation(vertex_path)
461: 
462:     return _legacy_result(KindMutationResult(
463:         status=result.status,
464:         reason=result.reason,
465:         mode=preview.mode,
466:         vertex_path=str(vertex_path),
467:         generation_before=preview.generation,
468:         generation_after=gen_after,
469:         changes=[c.as_dict() if hasattr(c, "as_dict") else str(c) for c in preview.changes],
470:         file_written=result.file_written,
471:     ))
472: 
473: 
474: def grant_observer(
475:     target: Path | str,
476:     observer_name: str,
477:     *,
478:     grants: Sequence[str] | None = None,
479:     identity: str | None = None,
480:     key: str | None = None,
481:     observer: str,
482:     credentials: CredentialProvider | None = None,
483:     registry: BackendRegistry | None = None,
484: ) -> KindMutationResult:
485:     """Add or update an observer in the vertex's admission block via ceremony.
486: 
487:     Parameters:
488:         target: Path to the .vertex file.
489:         observer_name: Name of the observer to declare/grant.
490:         grants: List of kind names this observer is allowed to emit.
491:         identity: Optional backing vertex identity store name.
492:         key: Optional base64 public key (if omitted, ensured via custody).
493:         observer: Authorship identity performing the declaration update.
494:         credentials: Key provider for signing the ceremony.
495: 
496:     Returns:
497:         KindMutationResult detailing the applied ceremony outcome.
498:     """
499:     vertex_path = Path(target).resolve()
500:     arrival = _arrival_descriptor_for(vertex_path)
501:     if arrival is None:
502:         _refuse_legacy_mapped_credentials(credentials, vertex=vertex_path)
503:         info = resolve_target(target)
504:         if info.target_type != "vertex":
505:             raise TargetUnsupported(
506:                 f"grant_observer requires a .vertex target, got {info.target_type}"
507:             )
508:     cred_provider = credentials or CustodyCredentialProvider()
509: 
510:     if key is None:
511:         mapped = getattr(cred_provider, "mapped", False)
512:         if arrival is not None and not mapped:
513:             # A wrapper need not expose a provider-level marker. Classify its
514:             # actual write configuration before the legacy custody helper can
515:             # mint a key for this requested observer.
516:             from .declare import _arrival_credentials
517: 
518:             mapped = _arrival_credentials(vertex_path, cred_provider).mapped
519:         if mapped:
520:             raise SdkValueError(
521:                 "mapped observer grants require an explicitly pre-created binding public key"
522:             )
523:         from custody import ensure_signing_key
524: 
525:         try:
526:             keypair = ensure_signing_key(vertex_path, observer=observer_name)
527:         except ValueError as exc:
528:             if arrival is not None:
529:                 raise SdkValueError(f"could not prepare Arrival observer grant: {exc}") from exc
530:             raise CeremonyFailed(f"could not splice observer grant: {exc}") from exc
531:         pub_key = keypair.public_b64
532:     else:
533:         pub_key = key
534: 
535:     current_text = vertex_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
536:     try:
537:         new_text = upsert_vertex_observer(
538:             current_text,
539:             observer_name,
540:             identity=identity,
541:             key=pub_key,
542:             grants=tuple(grants) if grants else (),
543:         )
544:     except Exception as exc:
545:         if arrival is not None:
546:             raise SdkValueError(f"could not generate Arrival observer grant: {exc}") from exc
547:         raise CeremonyFailed(f"could not splice observer grant: {exc}") from exc
548: 
549:     if arrival is not None:
550:         from .declare import _edit_arrival_declaration
551: 
552:         return _edit_arrival_declaration(
553:             vertex_path, new_text, observer=observer, credentials=cred_provider,
554:             registry=registry,
555:             source_cache_bytes=current_text.encode("utf-8"),
556:             require_cache_basis=True,
557:         )
558: 
559:     preview = plan_declaration_update(vertex_path, proposed_text=new_text)
560:     if not preview.applicable:
561:         raise CeremonyFailed(f"declaration update not applicable: {preview.reason}")
562: 
563:     result = apply_declaration_update(preview, observer=observer, credentials=cred_provider)
564:     if result.status not in ("applied", "noop"):
565:         raise CeremonyFailed(f"declaration update failed ({result.status}): {result.reason}")
566: 
567:     from engine.declaration import declaration_generation
568: 
569:     gen_after = declaration_generation(vertex_path)
570: 
571:     return _legacy_result(KindMutationResult(
572:         status=result.status,
573:         reason=result.reason,
574:         mode=preview.mode,
575:         vertex_path=str(vertex_path),
576:         generation_before=preview.generation,
577:         generation_after=gen_after,
578:         changes=[c.as_dict() if hasattr(c, "as_dict") else str(c) for c in preview.changes],
579:         file_written=result.file_written,
580:     ))
581: 
582: 
583: def revoke_observer(
584:     target: Path | str,
585:     observer_name: str,
586:     *,
587:     observer: str,
588:     credentials: CredentialProvider | None = None,
589:     registry: BackendRegistry | None = None,
590: ) -> KindMutationResult:
591:     """Remove an observer from the vertex's declared admission block via ceremony."""
592:     vertex_path = Path(target).resolve()
593:     arrival = _arrival_descriptor_for(vertex_path)
594:     if arrival is None:
595:         _refuse_legacy_mapped_credentials(credentials, vertex=vertex_path)
596:         info = resolve_target(target)
597:         if info.target_type != "vertex":
598:             raise TargetUnsupported(
599:                 f"revoke_observer requires a .vertex target, got {info.target_type}"
600:             )
601:     cred_provider = credentials or CustodyCredentialProvider()
602: 
603:     current_text = vertex_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
604:     try:
605:         new_text = remove_vertex_observer(current_text, observer_name)
606:     except ValueError as exc:
607:         if arrival is not None:
608:             raise SdkValueError(f"could not generate Arrival observer removal: {exc}") from exc
609:         raise CeremonyFailed(f"could not remove observer '{observer_name}': {exc}") from exc
610: 
611:     if arrival is not None:
612:         from .declare import _edit_arrival_declaration
613: 
614:         return _edit_arrival_declaration(
615:             vertex_path, new_text, observer=observer, credentials=cred_provider,
616:             registry=registry,
617:             source_cache_bytes=current_text.encode("utf-8"),
618:             require_cache_basis=True,
619:         )
620: 
621:     preview = plan_declaration_update(vertex_path, proposed_text=new_text)
622:     if not preview.applicable:
623:         raise CeremonyFailed(f"declaration update not applicable: {preview.reason}")
624: 
625:     result = apply_declaration_update(preview, observer=observer, credentials=cred_provider)
626:     if result.status not in ("applied", "noop"):
627:         raise CeremonyFailed(f"declaration update failed ({result.status}): {result.reason}")
628: 
629:     from engine.declaration import declaration_generation
630: 
631:     gen_after = declaration_generation(vertex_path)
632: 
633:     return _legacy_result(KindMutationResult(
634:         status=result.status,
635:         reason=result.reason,
636:         mode=preview.mode,
637:         vertex_path=str(vertex_path),
638:         generation_before=preview.generation,
639:         generation_after=gen_after,
640:         changes=[c.as_dict() if hasattr(c, "as_dict") else str(c) for c in preview.changes],
641:         file_written=result.file_written,
642:     ))
643: 
644: 
645: def recover_ceremony(
646:     intent_path: Path | str,
647:     *,
648:     registry: BackendRegistry | None = None,
649: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
650:     """Recover an interrupted legacy or explicit Arrival ceremony."""
651:     try:
652:         import json
653: 
654:         intent = Path(intent_path).resolve()
655:         data = json.loads(intent.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
656:         if data.get("schema") == "loops.engine/arrival-declaration-intent/v1":
657:             from .declare import recover_declaration
658: 
659:             return recover_declaration(intent, registry=registry).as_dict()
660:     except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError):
661:         pass
662:     outcome = recover_declaration_update(intent_path)
663:     return {
664:         "classification": outcome.classification,
665:         "finished": outcome.finished,
666:         "reason": outcome.reason,
667:         "intent_path": str(outcome.intent_path),
668:     }

===== libs/sdk/src/sdk/errors.py =====
1: """Normalize engine boundary failures for SDK process callers.
2: 
3: Engine deliberately keeps backend and witness refusal families separate.  The
4: SDK is the process boundary, so it translates those families into stable SDK
5: errors while retaining the identity evidence an operator needs to reconcile a
6: refusal or an outcome that may already have committed.
7: """
8: 
9: from __future__ import annotations
10: 
11: from collections.abc import Mapping
12: from pathlib import PurePath
13: from typing import Any
14: 
15: from engine.admission import AdmissionError
16: from engine.arrival import ArrivalError
17: from engine.arrival_contract import ContractRefusal
18: from engine.arrival_declarations import (
19:     DeclarationCommittedIncomplete,
20:     DeclarationOutcomeUnknown,
21:     DeclarationStale,
22:     DeclarationUnwitnessed,
23: )
24: from engine.arrival_head_attestation import AttestationRefusal
25: from engine.arrival_head_seam import NotWitnessed
26: from engine.arrival_initialization import (
27:     InitializationCommittedIncomplete,
28:     InitializationOutcomeUnknown,
29:     InitializationUnwitnessed,
30: )
31: from engine.arrival_maintenance import ProjectionSyncError
32: from engine.arrival_restore import RestoreForwardIncomplete, RestoreForwardUnknown
33: from engine.arrival_search import SearchIndexSyncError
34: from engine.credentials import CredentialBindingRefused
35: from engine.runtime_write import (
36:     BatchPostCommitProjectionFailed,
37:     BatchWriteCommitUnknown,
38:     BatchWritePreparationRefused,
39:     PostCommitProjectionFailed,
40:     RuntimeWriteRefused,
41:     WriteCommitUnknown,
42: )

44: from .types import AdmissionFailed, SdkError
45: 
46: __all__ = [
47:     "ArrivalRefusal",
48:     "AdmissionRefusal",
49:     "CommittedOutcome",
50:     "CommittedProjectionFailed",
51:     "CommittedIncomplete",
52:     "CommittedUnwitnessed",
53:     "CommittedOutcomeUnknown",
54:     "ProjectionOutcomeUnknown",
55:     "normalize_exception",
56: ]
57: 
58: 
59: def _json_value(value: Any) -> Any:
60:     if value is None or isinstance(value, (str, int, float, bool)):
61:         return value
62:     if isinstance(value, PurePath):
63:         return str(value)
64:     # Identity diagnostics must never stringify arbitrary record/payload objects.
65:     return None
66: 
67: 
68: def _head_dict(value: Any) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
69:     fields = ("lineage", "ordinal", "record_hash")
70:     if not all(hasattr(value, field) for field in fields):
71:         return None
72:     return {field: _json_value(getattr(value, field)) for field in fields}
73: 
74: 
75: def _coverage_dict(value: Any) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
76:     """Serialize explicit search-index coverage without backend row bodies."""
77:     through = _head_dict(getattr(value, "through", None))
78:     fields_hash = getattr(value, "fields_hash", None)
79:     schema_version = getattr(value, "schema_version", None)
80:     if through is None or not isinstance(fields_hash, str) or not isinstance(schema_version, str):
81:         return None
82:     return {
83:         "through": through,
84:         "fields_hash": fields_hash,
85:         "schema_version": schema_version,
86:     }
87: 
88: 
89: def _identity_details(exc: BaseException) -> dict[str, Any]:
90:     """Copy stable coordinates without serializing mutable record payloads."""
91:     details: dict[str, Any] = {}
92:     for name in (
93:         "fact_id",
94:         "tick_id",
95:         "ordinal",
96:         "observer",
97:         "kind",
98:         "vertex",
99:         "item_index",
100:         "source_type",
101:         "lineage",
102:         "intent_path",
103:         "phase",
104:         "fields_hash",
105:         "view_generation",
106:         "tier_index",
107:     ):
108:         value = getattr(exc, name, None)
109:         if value is not None:
110:             coordinate = _json_value(value)
111:             if coordinate is not None:
112:                 details[name] = coordinate
113:     for name in ("fact_ids", "tick_ids"):
114:         value = getattr(exc, name, None)
115:         if value is not None:
116:             details[name] = [_json_value(item) for item in value]
117:     items = getattr(exc, "items", None)
118:     if items is not None:
119:         details["items"] = [
120:             {
121:                 "fact_id": _json_value(item.fact_id),
122:                 "tick_id": _json_value(item.tick_id),
123:                 "tick_name": _json_value(item.tick_name),
124:                 "already_present": bool(item.already_present),
125:             }
126:             for item in items
127:         ]
128:     for name in (
129:         "head",
130:         "captured_head",
131:         "presented",
132:         "before",
133:         "after",
134:         "target",
135:         "projected_before",
136:         "observed_after",
137:         "projected_head",
138:     ):
139:         value = getattr(exc, name, None)
140:         if value is None:
141:             continue
142:         head = _head_dict(value)
143:         if head is not None:
144:             details[name] = head
145:     for name in ("coverage_before", "coverage_after", "observed_after"):
146:         value = getattr(exc, name, None)
147:         coverage = _coverage_dict(value)
148:         if coverage is not None:
149:             details[name] = coverage
150:     # Preserve an explicitly unavailable coordinate without claiming a head or
151:     # coverage. Missing attributes on older wrappers still remain omitted.
152:     for name in (
153:         "captured_head", "projected_head", "projected_before", "observed_after",
154:         "coverage_before", "coverage_after",
155:     ):
156:         if hasattr(exc, name) and getattr(exc, name) is None:
157:             details[name] = None
158:     commit = getattr(exc, "commit", None)
159:     if commit is not None:
160:         before = _head_dict(getattr(commit, "before", None))
161:         after = _head_dict(getattr(commit, "after", None))
162:         commit_details: dict[str, Any] = {}
163:         if before is not None:
164:             commit_details["before"] = before
165:         if after is not None:
166:             commit_details["after"] = after
167:         if commit_details:
168:             details["commit"] = commit_details
169:     return details
170: 
171: 
172: def _credential_binding_details(exc: BaseException) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
173:     """Extract public mapped-binding evidence from an explicit causal chain."""
174:     seen: set[int] = set()
175:     current: BaseException | None = exc
176:     for _ in range(3):
177:         if current is None or id(current) in seen:
178:             return None
179:         seen.add(id(current))
180:         if isinstance(current, CredentialBindingRefused):
181:             result: dict[str, Any] = {"reason": current.reason}
182:             request = current.request
183:             if request is not None:
184:                 result["request"] = {
185:                     "namespace": request.namespace,
186:                     "observer": request.observer,
187:                     "domain": request.domain.value,
188:                     "purpose": request.purpose.value,
189:                 }
190:             if current.captured_head is not None:
191:                 head = _head_dict(current.captured_head)
192:                 if head is not None:
193:                     result["captured_head"] = head
194:             if current.use_position is not None:
195:                 result["use_position"] = current.use_position
196:             binding = current.evidence
197:             if binding is not None:
198:                 # A malformed provider response can be the reason for refusal.
199:                 # Do not serialize arbitrary objects retained as that evidence.
200:                 result["binding"] = {
201:                     name: value if isinstance(value, str) else None
202:                     for name in ("key_ref", "algorithm", "public_key", "provenance")
203:                     for value in (getattr(binding, name, None),)
204:                 }
205:             return result
206:         current = _causal_exception(current)
207:     return None
208: 
209: 
210: def _causal_exception(exc: BaseException) -> BaseException | None:
211:     cause = getattr(exc, "cause", None)
212:     if isinstance(cause, BaseException):
213:         return cause
214:     return exc.__cause__ if isinstance(exc.__cause__, BaseException) else None
215: 
216: 
217: def _cause_details(exc: BaseException) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
218:     """Two explicit causal nodes, never implicit context or record bodies."""
219:     seen = {id(exc)}
220:     result: dict[str, Any] | None = None
221:     parent: dict[str, Any] | None = None
222:     for _ in range(2):
223:         cause = _causal_exception(exc)
224:         if cause is None or id(cause) in seen:
225:             break
226:         seen.add(id(cause))
227:         try:
228:             message = str(cause)
229:         except Exception:
230:             message = "<message unavailable>"
231:         node: dict[str, Any] = {
232:             "type": type(cause).__name__,
233:             "message": message[:512],
234:             "details": _identity_details(cause),
235:         }
236:         if len(message) > 512:
237:             node["truncated"] = True
238:         if parent is None:
239:             result = node
240:         else:
241:             parent["cause"] = node
242:         parent = node
243:         exc = cause
244:     return result
245: 
246: 
247: def _evidence_details(exc: BaseException, details: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
248:     """Expose coordinator proofs without deriving effects from error classes."""
249:     evidence: dict[str, Any] = {}
250:     phase = getattr(exc, "coordinator_phase", None)
251:     if isinstance(phase, str):
252:         evidence["phase"] = phase
253:     effects = getattr(exc, "effects", None)
254:     if isinstance(effects, Mapping):
255:         safe_effects = {}
256:         for resource in ("custody", "witness", "derived", "cache", "artifact", "dispatch"):
257:             effect = effects.get(resource)
258:             if not isinstance(effect, Mapping):
259:                 continue
260:             state = effect.get("state")
261:             attempt = effect.get("attempt")
262:             if state not in (
263:                 "not-attempted", "known-none", "committed", "unknown", "completed", "incomplete"
264:             ):
265:                 continue
266:             if attempt == "not-entered" and state != "not-attempted":
267:                 continue
268:             if attempt == "entered" and state == "not-attempted":
269:                 continue
270:             safe_effect = {"state": state}
271:             if attempt in ("not-entered", "entered"):
272:                 safe_effect["attempt"] = attempt
273:             safe_effects[resource] = safe_effect
274:         if safe_effects:
275:             evidence["effects"] = safe_effects
276:     cause = _cause_details(exc)
277:     if cause is not None:
278:         evidence["cause"] = cause
279:     if not evidence:
280:         return None
281:     evidence["schema"] = "loops.sdk/evidence/v1"
282:     basis_fields = (
283:         "head", "captured_head", "presented", "target", "projected_before", "projected_head",
284:         "observed_after", "coverage_before", "coverage_after", "fields_hash", "view_generation",
285:     )
286:     identity_fields = (
287:         "fact_id", "tick_id", "fact_ids", "tick_ids", "items", "item_index", "tier_index",
288:         "ordinal", "observer", "kind", "vertex", "lineage", "commit",
289:     )
290:     basis = {name: details[name] for name in basis_fields if name in details}
291:     identities = {name: details[name] for name in identity_fields if name in details}
292:     if basis:
293:         evidence["basis"] = basis
294:     if identities:
295:         evidence["identities"] = identities
296:     return evidence
297: 
298: 
299: def _context_detail(name: str, value: Any) -> Any:
300:     """Serialize only stable identity fields supplied by an SDK operation."""
301:     if name in {"fact_ids", "tick_ids"}:
302:         return [_json_value(item) for item in value]
303:     if name == "items":
304:         return [
305:             {
306:                 "fact_id": _json_value(item.fact_id),
307:                 "tick_id": _json_value(item.tick_id),
308:                 "tick_name": _json_value(item.tick_name),
309:                 "already_present": bool(item.already_present),
310:             }
311:             for item in value
312:         ]
313:     head = _head_dict(value)
314:     return head if head is not None else _json_value(value)
315: 
316: 
317: class ArrivalRefusal(SdkError):
318:     """A typed, non-committing Arrival diagnostic refusal."""
319: 
320:     def __init__(self, message: str, *, source_type: str, details: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
321:         super().__init__(message)
322:         self.source_type = source_type
323:         self.details = dict(details)
324: 
325:     def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
326:         return {
327:             "schema": "loops.sdk/error/v1",
328:             "type": type(self).__name__,
329:             "message": str(self),
330:             "source_type": self.source_type,
331:             "outcome": "refused",
332:             "details": dict(self.details),
333:         }
334: 
335: 
336: class AdmissionRefusal(AdmissionFailed):
337:     """An engine admission refusal retaining its source and diagnostics."""
338: 
339:     def __init__(
340:         self,
341:         message: str,
342:         *,
343:         source_type: str,
344:         details: Mapping[str, Any],
345:         observer: str | None = None,
346:         kind: str | None = None,
347:         vertex: str | None = None,
348:     ) -> None:
349:         super().__init__(message, observer=observer, kind=kind, vertex=vertex)
350:         self.source_type = source_type
351:         self.details = dict(details)
352: 
353:     def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
354:         return {
355:             "schema": "loops.sdk/error/v1",
356:             "type": type(self).__name__,
357:             "message": str(self),
358:             "source_type": self.source_type,
359:             "outcome": "admission-refused",
360:             "details": dict(self.details),
361:         }
362: 
363: 
364: class CommittedOutcome(SdkError):
365:     """A durable or possibly durable operation with explicit reconciliation evidence."""
366: 
367:     outcome = "committed"
368: 
369:     def __init__(self, message: str, *, source_type: str, details: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
370:         super().__init__(message)
371:         self.source_type = source_type
372:         self.details = dict(details)
373: 
374:     def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
375:         return {
376:             "schema": "loops.sdk/error/v1",
377:             "type": type(self).__name__,
378:             "message": str(self),
379:             "source_type": self.source_type,
380:             "outcome": self.outcome,
381:             "details": dict(self.details),
382:         }
383: 
384: 
385: class CommittedOutcomeUnknown(CommittedOutcome):
386:     """The append outcome is unknown; reconcile before retrying."""
387: 
388:     outcome = "unknown"
389: 
390: 
391: class CommittedUnwitnessed(CommittedOutcome):
392:     """Custody committed, but witnessing failed."""
393: 
394:     outcome = "committed-unwitnessed"
395: 
396: 
397: class CommittedProjectionFailed(CommittedOutcome):
398:     """Custody committed, but post-commit projection publication failed."""
399: 
400:     outcome = "committed-projection-failed"
401: 
402: 
403: class CommittedIncomplete(CommittedOutcome):
404:     """A known commit exists while a later initialization phase is incomplete."""
405: 
406:     outcome = "committed-incomplete"
407: 
408: 
409: class ProjectionOutcomeUnknown(SdkError):
410:     """Projection maintenance may have changed derived state; reconcile it."""
411: 
412:     outcome = "projection-unknown"
413: 
414:     def __init__(self, message: str, *, source_type: str, details: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
415:         super().__init__(message)
416:         self.source_type = source_type
417:         self.details = dict(details)
418: 
419:     def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
420:         return {
421:             "schema": "loops.sdk/error/v1",
422:             "type": type(self).__name__,
423:             "message": str(self),
424:             "source_type": self.source_type,
425:             "outcome": self.outcome,
426:             "details": dict(self.details),
427:         }
428: 
429: 
430: def normalize_exception(
431:     exc: BaseException, *, context: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None
432: ) -> BaseException:
433:     """Return a stable SDK exception for engine boundary failures.
434: 
435:     Existing SDK errors are returned unchanged.  Classification is by the
436:     engine's public exception types, never by message text.
437:     """
438:     if not isinstance(exc, Exception) or isinstance(exc, SdkError):
439:         return exc
440:     details = _identity_details(exc)
441:     context_fields = {
442:         "captured_head",
443:         "fact_id",
444:         "tick_id",
445:         "fact_ids",
446:         "tick_ids",
447:         "items",
448:         "item_index",
449:     }
450:     for name, value in (context or {}).items():
451:         if name in details or name not in context_fields:
452:             continue
453:         coordinate = _context_detail(name, value)
454:         if coordinate is not None or value is None:
455:             details[name] = coordinate
456:     details["source_type"] = type(exc).__name__
457:     credential = _credential_binding_details(exc)
458:     if credential is not None:
459:         details["credential_binding"] = credential
460:     evidence = _evidence_details(exc, details)
461:     if evidence is not None:
462:         details["evidence"] = evidence
463:     if isinstance(exc, (BatchWriteCommitUnknown, WriteCommitUnknown)):
464:         return CommittedOutcomeUnknown(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
465:     if isinstance(exc, InitializationOutcomeUnknown):
466:         return CommittedOutcomeUnknown(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
467:     if isinstance(exc, (DeclarationOutcomeUnknown, RestoreForwardUnknown)):
468:         return CommittedOutcomeUnknown(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
469:     if isinstance(exc, InitializationUnwitnessed):
470:         return CommittedUnwitnessed(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
471:     if isinstance(exc, DeclarationUnwitnessed):
472:         return CommittedUnwitnessed(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
473:     if isinstance(exc, InitializationCommittedIncomplete):
474:         return CommittedIncomplete(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
475:     if isinstance(exc, (DeclarationCommittedIncomplete, RestoreForwardIncomplete)):
476:         return CommittedIncomplete(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
477:     if isinstance(exc, NotWitnessed):
478:         return CommittedUnwitnessed(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
479:     if isinstance(exc, DeclarationStale):
480:         return ArrivalRefusal(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
481:     if isinstance(exc, BatchPostCommitProjectionFailed):
482:         return CommittedProjectionFailed(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
483:     if isinstance(exc, PostCommitProjectionFailed):
484:         return CommittedProjectionFailed(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
485:     if isinstance(exc, ProjectionSyncError):
486:         return ProjectionOutcomeUnknown(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
487:     if isinstance(exc, SearchIndexSyncError):
488:         return ProjectionOutcomeUnknown(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
489:     if isinstance(exc, BatchWritePreparationRefused):
490:         fact = exc.item.fact
491:         if exc.item.fact_id is not None:
492:             details.setdefault("fact_id", exc.item.fact_id)
493:         details.setdefault("observer", fact.observer)
494:         details.setdefault("kind", fact.kind)
495:         details.setdefault("vertex", exc.effective_declaration.name)
496:         if exc.admission_refused:
497:             return AdmissionRefusal(
498:                 str(exc),
499:                 source_type=type(exc).__name__,
500:                 details=details,
501:                 observer=fact.observer,
502:                 kind=fact.kind,
503:                 vertex=exc.effective_declaration.name,
504:             )
505:         return ArrivalRefusal(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
506:     if isinstance(exc, AdmissionError):
507:         return AdmissionRefusal(
508:             str(exc),
509:             source_type=type(exc).__name__,
510:             details=details,
511:             observer=getattr(exc, "observer", None),
512:             kind=getattr(exc, "kind", None),
513:             vertex=getattr(exc, "vertex", None),
514:         )
515:     if isinstance(exc, CredentialBindingRefused):
516:         return ArrivalRefusal(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
517:     if isinstance(
518:         exc,
519:         (
520:             AttestationRefusal,
521:             ContractRefusal,
522:             ArrivalError,
523:             RuntimeWriteRefused,
524:         ),
525:     ):
526:         return ArrivalRefusal(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
527:     return exc

===== FULL PRODUCTION / TEST PATCH =====
diff --git a/libs/custody/README.md b/libs/custody/README.md

--- a/libs/custody/README.md
+++ b/libs/custody/README.md
@@ -24,3 +24,46 @@ Any client that emits signed facts or ticks must compose identically or
 Depends on `sign` (loops-agnostic Ed25519 primitives) and `engine`
 (store-canonical declaration resolution). The engine itself never imports
 this — it takes signer callables by injection.
+
+
+## Explicit mapped bindings
+
+`MappedCredentialProvider(root, namespace=..., receipt_observer=...)` provides
+an opt-in alternative to locator-based custody. Exact namespace/observer
+strings select an opaque managed key reference recorded with its public key.
+All three signing domains use that binding with separate domain prefixes.
+Hashed filesystem slots are storage details; labels retain their exact case,
+Unicode spelling, and non-path characters. A binding does not authorize an
+Arrival claim by itself: the engine separately checks captured history.
+
+`for_write` returns lazy configuration. `resolve` loads and validates the
+binding, private-derived public material, and any existing public convenience
+file without creating or repairing anything. Missing unbound identities can
+remain unsigned; malformed mappings, conflicting public material, missing
+referenced private keys, and filesystem aliases refuse.
+
+Explicit mutations are `create_binding(observer, token=...)`,
+`import_legacy(vertex_path, observer, token=...)`, and
+`bind_existing_ref(observer, key_ref, expected_public_key, token=...)`.
+Import copies private material into managed custody and preserves the original
+files. Reusing a managed reference is deliberate and does not introduce its
+public key into an Arrival lineage. Existing bindings are not silently
+replaced; local rotation is a separate future operation.
+
+Mutations use a provider-wide POSIX advisory lock, durable intents, and
+complete-file publication with no-clobber links. The supported filesystem must
+provide those lock, link, and fsync semantics. Concurrent creators converge
+on one binding; resolution remains read-only. There is no automatic orphan
+cleanup or key deletion.
+
+Keep the operation token when a mutation is interrupted.
+`BindingMutationIncomplete` retains namespace, observer, token, candidate key
+reference, and phase; the binding may already have been published.
+`recover_binding(observer, token=...)` reconciles the retained intent and
+candidate. It never replaces a missing key referenced by a published binding.
+An import interrupted before copying private material needs the original
+source re-presented with the same import token and expected public material;
+recovery cannot invent that key. A completed imported candidate can recover
+without the old source directory. Retrying with a different token does not
+bypass an unfinished operation. Creation results report the returned binding
+and effects of that invocation; they contain no private key material.
